## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

For an essential n-input function and a proper block B with 2<=|B|<=n-1, list the cofactor row r_a=(f(a,b):b on B-complement) for every assignment a to B. A factorization f=g(h(x_B),x_Bc) exists exactly when these rows have two distinct values. Necessity is the binary range of h and exclusion of irrelevant block variables. Sufficiency labels the two rows and makes h their label. Both factors are essential: an irrelevant h input would be irrelevant in f; the new g wire is essential because the rows differ; the other g inputs remain essential by surjectivity of h.

Splitting a block of size k produces arities k and n-k+1, each strictly below n. Induction produces a formula with distinct physical leaves and prime internal gates. The new wire in the outer factor occurs once by induction and is replaced by the inner formula; this preserves disjoint physical support at every internal node. There are at most n-1 nonunary internal nodes and depth at most n-1, but the entropy reduction below needs no factor depending on depth. No canonical or unique factorization is asserted. Any nontrivial simultaneous disjoint substitution yields such a block, so the criterion covers the intended general notion, not just a special binary outer gate.

The class has unbounded arity. Signed parity, AND and OR split off pairs above arity two. Conversely every symmetric threshold at 2<=t<=n-1 is prime: for a block of size k and complement size d, the possible cofactor thresholds t-j, j=0,...,k, clipped to [0,d+1], occupy at least three distinct integer positions. The exact interval calculation U-L>=2 is given in the definition file. Factoring does not leave a fixed finite gate basis.

Under any interior product physical law, every nonconstant child has both output signs with positive probability. Disjoint supports imply independence, so a parent inherits the product law with precisely those means. Nonconstant child variances v_i are positive. This law generally differs from the uniform law, even when the original physical inputs were uniform. The literal/constant conventions prevent zero-variance divisions.

Write Hplus_mu=sum_{S nonempty} fhat_mu(S)^2 log2(sigma_S^2/fhat_mu(S)^2), with sigma_S^2 the product of physical variances, and delta_mu=I_mu-Var_mu. For F=g(f_i), centered child expansions and disjoint blocks give the unique union coefficient formula in PRIME_ENDPOINT_IMPLICATION.md. With alpha_i=I_i^eta(g)/v_i it yields

    Hplus_mu(F)=Hplus_eta(g)+sum_i alpha_i Hplus_mu_i(f_i),
    delta_mu(F)=delta_eta(g)+sum_i alpha_i delta_mu_i(f_i).

All alpha_i and deficits are nonnegative. A single constant C valid for all primes and all induced interior product laws therefore propagates from literals to the root. Conversely all-function corrected FEI+ includes the prime class, proving the endpoint equivalence. On the uniform physical cube, H=Hplus+h0 and h0<=log2(e) Var, so H<=max{C,log2(e)} I. The required scalar inequality follows from -t ln t<=1-t for t in [0,1].

For residual D_C=Hplus-C delta, the exact same identity shows that a positive corrected deficit somewhere in a formula has a positive prime descendant under its actual induced product law. This is a localization statement, not an entropy payment and not a uniform FEI counterfamily. The exact remaining unsupported mathematical assertion is the existence of one finite C for all prime arities and all interior biases.

Uniform-only gate estimates cannot be substituted into this proof. The prime root in majority_3(AND_2(x1,x2),x3,x4) receives biases (1/4,1/2,1/2) from uniform physical inputs. The first unsupported step is applying a uniform prime estimate at that nonuniform root law. Ordinary H<=CI bounds also omit the deficit cancellation needed to prevent a charge accumulating at every gate. This report proves failure of those proof substitutions; it does not claim impossibility of every other abstract reduction from ordinary uniform prime FEI.

# A043 stage 2: certified biased-prime grid and a missed prime endpoint

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

The B09 lead assigned the largest corrected ratio Hplus_mu(g)/delta_mu(g), delta=I_mu-Var_mu, over essential primes of arity at most four under product biases, starting with a discovery grid and then exact certification. This stage closes one explicitly finite grid and a nonbinary boundary obstruction; it does not close the supremum over all interior product laws. Constants and literals have zero deficit and are outside the ratio domain. Entropy is in bits; influence is spectral influence in the normalized product basis.

For every essential prime g of arity n=2,3,4 and every independent bias vector with coordinates in {1/4,1/2,3/4}, the exact largest ratio is

    M_grid = 26-12 log2(3).

It is attained by sign OR_2 at probabilities (1/4,1/4), up to the input/output symmetries of the grid. A canonical table encodes +1 at assignments 1,2,3 and -1 at assignment 0: mask 14. The exact certificate encloses M_grid between 29278127/4194304 and 14639065/2097152. It certifies all 4,639,338 labeled prime/bias cases through an exact quotient of 458,356 representatives. The n=3 and n=4 grid maxima are rigorously below the binary winner; their discovered values are approximately 5.45473576 and 6.37956699. Those last decimals are discovery summaries, not all-bias bounds.

On a fixed three-input prime, majority_3, the interior path with probabilities (1-q^3,q,q) satisfies

    lim_{q->0} Hplus_mu(majority_3)/delta_mu(majority_3)=2+4/ln(2).

At q=1/8 its ratio lies in [73411827299/10000000000,734118273/100000000], hence exceeds 7. This disproves a hypothetical all-bias bound Hplus<=7 delta even when restricted to nonbinary primes of arity three or four. It is a finite strong-helper counterexample, not a disproof of ordinary uniform FEI or an unbounded ratio family. The curve is bounded in the displayed limit. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Exact domain and symmetry

Encode assignments by an integer x, with input bits Yi=(x>>i)&1 and signs Xi=2Yi-1. A table mask has bit x equal to 1 exactly when g(x)=+1. Output negation preserves every squared coefficient and the corrected ratio, so retain only masks with g(0)=-1. Input reflection Yi->1-Yi, together with p_i->1-p_i, changes signs of selected normalized characters but preserves all squared masses and input variances. It also preserves the cofactor-row prime property. Since every table of the prime family is included, biases 3/4 may therefore be represented by 1/4; keep {1/4,1/2} in each coordinate. No variable-permutation quotient or unverified orbit list is used.

The exact vectorized cofactor criterion removes all nonessential functions and then every proper block with at most two distinct rows. It returns 5,52,28620 output-negation representatives of primes on n=2,3,4, respectively, agreeing with the stage-1 exhaustive tuple census. The checked family covers 5*4+52*8+28620*16=458356 representative gate/bias pairs. Restoring all labeled tables and grid biases gives 10*9+104*27+57240*81=4639338 cases. Reflection need not act freely; this is a surjective maximum-preserving representation, not an orbit-count assertion.

### Rational biased Fourier spectra

For a reduced bias vector let b coordinates have probability 1/4 and u=n-b have probability 1/2. D=4^b 2^u is a power of two. On a biased coordinate use integer probability weight 1 for Y=1 and 3 for Y=0, and centered numerator t=4Y-1=2X+1. Its normalized character is t/sqrt(3). On a uniform coordinate use probability weight 1 and character X. Let W(x) be the product of the integer probability weights. For a Fourier set S define the exact integer moment

    N_S = sum_x g(x) W(x)
          product_{i in S, biased}(4Y_i-1)
          product_{i in S, uniform}(2Y_i-1).

If k=|S intersect biased|, the squared coefficient is P_S=N_S^2/(D^2 3^k), while sigma_S^2=(3/4)^k. Thus

    log2(sigma_S^2/P_S)
       =2 log2 D-2k+2k log2 3-2 log2 |N_S|.

Zero N_S contributes zero. Put L=D^2 3^b and w_S=N_S^2 3^(b-k). All cases verify sum_S w_S=L exactly and have positive deficit numerator d=sum_{S nonempty}(|S|-1)w_S. The corrected ratio equals the corrected logarithmic numerator divided by d; the common L cancels.

The Boolean coefficient bound gives |N_S|<=D(3/2)^k<=1296 throughout this domain. A finite table of outward logarithm intervals therefore suffices. Neither irrational basis factors nor floating Fourier coefficients are used to prove signs.

### Certified logarithms and arithmetic bounds

For x in [1,2], z=(x-1)/(x+1) is in [0,1/3]. Use

    ln x = 2 sum_{j=0}^{31} z^(2j+1)/(2j+1) + R,
    0<=R<=2 z^65/[65(1-z^2)].

Version 2 encloses every power and product by floor/ceiling operations on integers scaled by Q=2^80. Squaring the nonnegative z interval and repeatedly multiplying it gives lower and upper bounds on each odd power; dividing by the positive odd denominator and rounding outward preserves bounds on the partial sum. The added upper tail is the exact integer ceiling of Q*2*9/(65*8*3^65). At x=1 the exact interval is [0,0]. These are rational interval operations, not floating approximations.

Normalize each integer a as a/2^floor(log2 a), apply the displayed logarithm series, divide its interval by the positive similarly obtained ln(2) interval, and add the integer part. Round outward at scale s=2^24. The saved table has width at most one integer unit for every a=1,...,1296. Use the upper log(3) and lower log(|N_S|) for the entropy upper bound, and the opposite endpoints for the lower bound. Parseval, positive deficit and both maximum comparisons are checked on every representative. A floating quotient may select an index for a per-bias maximum, but all comparisons against that index are subsequently verified by arbitrary-integer cross-products, so a wrong candidate could not pass the certificate.

The moments have absolute value at most 1296; powers and products used to form w_S fit in signed 64-bit arithmetic. L<=256^2*3^4=5308416. Verified Parseval bounds the sum of all weights by L, and the absolute logarithm bracket by 50s, so the entropy sum has absolute value at most 50sL<2^63. Ratio cross-products may exceed 64 bits and explicitly use Python arbitrary integers through object arrays. Final reported interval ratios use Fraction. This avoids silent integer overflow as well as numerical underflow.

### Exact winner

The winner's spectrum has empty mass 1/64, singleton masses 27/64 and 27/64, and pair mass 9/64. Its deficit is 9/64. Therefore

    Hplus=(27/32)log2(16/9)+9/32,
    Hplus/delta=26-12log2(3).

Every n=3 and n=4 per-bias upper certificate is strictly below the winner's exact lower enclosure. The final small checker independently compares all other 19 binary representatives with that lower enclosure. The canonical winner alone remains. This closes the exact grid maximum, rather than just bounding a discovered witness.

### Nonbinary boundary and the unsupported extension

MAJORITY_BOUNDARY.md gives the complete prime test, normalized derivative coefficients, rational spectrum and Taylor expansion for probabilities (1-q^3,q,q). In particular delta=4q^2+O(q^3) and Hplus=(8+16/ln(2))q^2+O(q^3); the other terms are O(q^4), O(q^5), or O(q^5 log(1/q)). The ratio limit is consequently 2+4/ln(2). All bias coordinates stay interior for q>0. The twelve dyadic points q=2^-k, k=1,...,12, independently reconstruct the spectrum from all eight physical assignments and check the closed masses exactly.

The first unsupported line toward the lead's full target would be extending this finite grid upper bound, or its winner, to arbitrary interior biases. That extension is false for the bound 7: the saved majority point at q=1/8 is already above 7. The continuous supremum on ternary/quaternary primes is not determined here, nor is a finite arity-uniform prime constant paid. No divergent family was found or proved; the displayed sequence has a finite proved limit. Its lower bound does not assert that the continuous supremum equals that limit.

# A043 stage 3: sharp ternary prime closure and a prime reserve obstruction

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Determine corrected product-biased Hplus/(I-Var) for essential Boolean prime gates, beginning with the entire ternary bias continuum left unresolved by the previous finite grid. A prime admits no genuine one-bit substitution on a proper essential block of size at least two; supports are disjoint. All probabilities are independent and interior. Delta=I-Var uses spectral influence. Hplus is in bits; E=(ln2)Hplus is its natural-unit version. Ordinary Fourier entropy H also includes the empty spectral atom. Degenerate boundary laws are treated by contraction or by limits of interior ratios, never division by a zero deficit.

Result: the sharp common supremum over all 104 ternary primes and all product biases is Cstar=2+4/ln2. Three previously remaining symmetry classes are proved here; majority and mux are reconstructed from the original A042/A044 sources with scoped reviews. All interior ternary prime ratios are strictly below Cstar. Exact disjoint composition closes every Boolean gate on at most three essential inputs and every read-once formula with fan-in at most three at this corrected constant. The ordinary uniform-input conclusion is H<=Cstar I, without claiming that this ordinary constant is optimal.

A signed singleton reserve holds in every dimension. Its tempting higher-degree companion fails even on a specified four-input prime, with an exact certified positive gap >13/100. Full FEI and the all-arity product-biased prime bound remain OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

CORE_PROOF.md is the full local derivation. Its universal signed reserve uses Di=(f|Yi=1-f|Yi=0)/2 in {-1,0,1}. If ci=E Di then Var(Di)>=|ci|(1-|ci|) and ci^2 ln(1/ci^2)<=2|ci|(1-|ci|). Parseval yields

    E_single <= 4 delta - 2 R,
    R=sum_{|S|>=3}(|S|-2)P(S).

The exact catalogue exhausts all 256 labeled ternary sign tables, and identifies five prime input-permutation/reflection/output-negation orbits of sizes 16,8,8,48,24, with minimal masks 22,23,24,25,27. The symmetry preserves the full bias domain and ratio. The essential-block criterion is exhaustive: a proper block substitution gives exactly two distinct rows; conversely the row label supplies the Boolean inner gate. Thus the catalogue omits no prime class.

For all-equal signs (orbit 24), pair amplitudes are 1/2 and there is no triple. The scalar inequality h(|(a+b)/2|)<=[(1-a^2)+(1-b^2)]/2 pays singletons and gives E<=(4+ln4)delta. Its all-rare equal-bias path reaches Cstar.

For exactly-one (orbit 22), c_ij=(3mk-1)/4 and c_123=3/4. Each pair excess over ln4 P(ij) is at most (2/3) of the same triple mass T. Keeping the signed reserve's -2T gives E<=(4+ln4)delta-ln9 T. The path (1-q^3,q,q) reaches Cstar.

For mask 25, c_12=1-p3/2, c_13=-p2/2, c_23=-p1/2, c_123=-1/4. The distinguished pair contributes a negative -2T credit, while each other pair needs at most +2T. Together with triple entropy ln16 T, the higher-degree cost is at most ln4 delta+2T. The signed singleton reserve cancels it exactly. The path (q,q,1-q^3) reaches Cstar. Discarding the distinguished negative credit would leave an unpaid +2T.

The majority reconstruction gives E<=(4+ln4)delta-(ln4-1)T. The mux reconstruction gives E/delta=ln4+[d^2 ln(4/d^2)+2v0 A(1-q)+2v1 A(q)]/(v0+v1), where d=|m1-m0|. Geometry v0+v1>=d(2-d) and ln(2/d)<=2/d-1 pay the data-means term. All five classes therefore have the same sharp supremum. CORE_PROOF.md gives explicit centered-coordinate composition and contraction, establishing the stated formula closure. Its lower paths are analytic limits, not extrapolations of grid samples.

The companion E_ge2<=ln4 delta+2R is false on primes. The sign gate positive exactly at assignments 7,8,15, mask 33152, is essential and passes all ten proper block row tests. At probabilities (1/2,1/2,1/2,1/1024), its exact delta is 5257209/16777216 and R is 262911/4194304. The certified companion excess exceeds 13/100. Its Y4=0 slice is AND3, whose uniform companion excess is (3ln2-1)/8>0. The prime slice explains the obstruction. The actual total corrected inequality has margin greater than 9/20 in natural units at this point; this is not an FEI counterexample.

The first unsupported replacement is an all-arity cofactor-sensitive entropy payment that retains unused singleton slack and pays the higher-degree excess without charging the same mass twice. Prime irreducibility alone does not eliminate a degenerate composite slice. Neither the signed reserve nor ternary affine-coefficient identities establish the required replacement.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md and DECOMPOSITION.md give the complete analytic argument. If conjunction events A and B disagree on at least two common literal coordinates, they are disjoint and their physical derivatives have disjoint supports. Consequently I(F)=I(fA)+I(fB), Var(F)=Var(fA)+Var(fB)-8P(A)P(B), and delta(F)>=delta(fA)+delta(fB), for F=fA+fB+1.

Each individual conjunction's normalized mixed difference on a set S of size>=2 has absolute value<=1/2. The scalar inequality h(a+b)<=2h(a)+2h(b), h(t)=t^2ln(1/t^2), holds at that cap. For equal signs, Cauchy and log inequalities pay the squared amplitude; for opposite signs h is increasing on[0,1/2], so cancellation is paid by the larger original entropy. This does not permit arbitrary singleton amplitudes near1.

Apply the scalar bound only to higher-degree sets and use the known read-once conjunction bound E(fi)<=C0 delta(fi), C0=4+ln4. The universal signed singleton reserve pays E_single(F)<=4delta(F). Thus E(F)<=4delta(F)+2C0[delta(fA)+delta(fB)]<=(12+4ln2)delta(F). The constituent higher-degree entropy ledgers and singleton cost are explicitly added with these constants against the output deficit; no triple mass or coefficient-one slack is spent twice.

For arbitrary two-term DNFs, group coordinates into agreeing common literals, opposing common literals, first-private literals and second-private literals. These are disjoint physical groups. Zero conflicts give an OR of two private conjunctions, multiplied by the common conjunction; one conflict gives a mux of those private conjunctions; both have genuine composition constant Cstar. At least two conflicts leave T_{r,g}. Its three distinct cofactor rows are exhibited for every type of proper block, establishing exact primality. This proves the entire class at K and identifies its irreducible root gates. The exact census is10,52,168 natural two-term primes at n=2,3,4, or10,80,336 with output negation. The n4 natural cases are8 T_{4,0},64 T_{3,1},96 T_{2,2} instances. Every outer bias is the actual induced bias of its disjoint child conjunction; there is no balanced-output assumption.

A concrete next fixture is independently certified: prime mask56067, positive on A={Y1=1,Y2=1,Z=1}, B={Y1=0,Y3=1,Z=1}, C={Y2=0,Y3=0}. Its three events are mutually exclusive, but A/B share the agreeing guard Z in addition to their opposing Y1 literal. The exact all-bias deficit loss is delta(F)-sum delta(fi)=-2v1*p2*p3*p4*(1-p4). At uniform bias it is-1/8: constituent deficits5/16,5/16,1/4, output I=7/4, Var=1, delta=3/4. NEXT_FIXTURE.md gives the derivation, which distinguishes influence cross terms that cancel against covariance from true deficit loss.

The exact first unsupported replacement is an exclusive payment for that coherent shared-guard loss, together with retained constituent entropy, when arbitrary primes or three-term cofactor collisions replace the separated two-event interface. Neither irreducibility nor the A042 slack identity alone proves it. No general corrected bound or sharp optimum for mask56067 is asserted here.

# A044: equal-mean addressing and the exact unpaid mean-profile residual

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. This run narrows the packet to a checked explicit nonsymmetric family: for every a>=0, the addressing gate F(z,y)=y_z has a address bits and 2^a distinct sign data inputs. The address bits have arbitrary independent Bernoulli probabilities q_i. All data inputs have one common sign mean m, with variance v=1-m^2. All coordinates are independent. For this class the sharp all-arity estimate is H_mu^+(F)<=4(I_mu(F)-Var_mu(F)). The common-mean restriction is part of the theorem, not something imposed after composition. Endpoints are included by restriction. Logarithms in H^+ are base two. I_mu is spectral expected degree, not unweighted pivotal count.

Artifact complete; narrowed mathematical result proved below and checked exactly; independent acceptance pending. The packet as broadly contracted is NOT complete: an all-product-bias gate result with unrestricted induced data means is not proved, and publication novelty is not established. B09's pending-review cap was already reached. This is a verification/narrow exploration and gap handoff, not a request to add another broad acceptance-ready candidate to the queue.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

For a Bernoulli coordinate with probability q use phi=(z-q)/sqrt(q(1-q)), and sigma^2=4q(1-q). Define P(S)=fhat(S)^2 and

H_mu^+(f)=sum_{S nonempty} P(S) log2(sigma_S^2/P(S)), Delta_mu(f)=sum_{S nonempty} (|S|-1)P(S).

Zero masses contribute zero. For each address c in {0,1}^a put r_ci=q_i if c_i=1 and r_ci=1-q_i otherwise, and p_c=product_i r_ci. Its cell indicator A_c has coefficient magnitude r_ci on an omitted coordinate and sqrt(q_i(1-q_i)) on an included coordinate. Thus, for T subseteq [a],

ahat_c(T)^2=p_c product_{i in T}(1-r_ci) product_{i notin T}r_ci.

Write each data sign y_c=m+sqrt(v) psi_c. Then F=m+sqrt(v)sum_c A_c psi_c. Every nonconstant Fourier set has exactly one data coordinate c, so there is no collision. Its squared coefficient is v ahat_c(T)^2. The empty squared coefficient is m^2. Consequently Var(F)=v, total data influence is v, and

Delta(F)=I_address(F)=v sum_i 2q_i(1-q_i).

The data variance sigma_c^2=v cancels exactly in the corrected logarithm. For a fixed c, the conditional law of T includes i with probability 1-r_ci. Using q_i(1-q_i)=r_ci(1-r_ci), that coordinate's mean corrected log is

ell(r)= (1-r)log2(4r^2)-2log2 r = 2(1-r)-2r log2 r.

Hence the complete formula, without omitted normalizers, is

H^+(F)=v sum_i [4q_i(1-q_i)-2q_i^2 log2 q_i-2(1-q_i)^2 log2(1-q_i)].

To bound it, put g(t)=t ln(1/t)/(1-t). Its integral representation is

g(t)=integral_0^1 t/[s+(1-s)t] ds.

For every s in (0,1), the integrand has second derivative -2s(1-s)/[s+(1-s)t]^3<=0. Therefore g is concave on (0,1). Jensen gives g(q)+g(1-q)<=2g(1/2)=2 ln2. Multiplying by q(1-q)/ln2 gives

q^2 log2(1/q)+(1-q)^2 log2(1/(1-q))<=2q(1-q).

Substitution proves H^+(F)<=8v sum_i q_i(1-q_i)=4Delta(F). If a>=1, all q_i=1/2 and v>0, equality holds: H^+=2av and Delta=av/2. Thus 4 is sharp. At a=0 the function is a dictator and both quantities vanish. Deterministic address bits are removed and unused data discarded; a deterministic common data value makes F constant. The same formulas extend continuously with t log t=0 at t=0.

### Exact heterogeneous-data decomposition and the remaining gap

Now allow arbitrary data means m_c with v_c=1-m_c^2, and define the real address mean profile h(z)=m_z. Then

F=h(z)+sum_c sqrt(v_c) A_c(z) psi_c(y_c).

The address-only spectrum of h and the distinct one-data-coordinate spectra are disjoint. Apply the same calculation, interpreting H_q^+(h) by the same coefficient formula for the bounded real function h. This gives the exact identities

H_mu^+(F)=H_q^+(h)+sum_c p_c v_c sum_i ell(r_ci),

Delta_mu(F)=Delta_q(h)+sum_c p_c v_c sum_i(1-r_ci).

Here Delta_q(h)=I_q(h)-Var_q(h)>=0 by degree counting. For every r in (0,1), -r ln r<=1-r, so

ell(r)<=(2+2/ln2)(1-r).

Thus the entire centered-data part is arity-uniformly paid at K0=2+2/ln2; the only unpaid part is the actual mean-profile entropy. The first unsupported line for an unrestricted address-gate theorem is a bound on H_q^+(h)-C Delta_q(h) using the centered-data slack with one absolute C. It is not valid to assert H_q^+(h)<=C Delta_q(h) for every bounded real h: a scaled dictator has positive H^+ and zero deficit.

More decisively, at deterministic data endpoints v_c=0, choose m_c=b(c) for any Boolean b on the address cube. Then F=b(z), and both identities reduce to those for b. At uniform address inputs, a universal H^+(address)<=C Delta(address) over all independent data biases would therefore imply H(b)<= (C+1/ln2)I(b): the omitted empty atom h0<=Var(b)/ln2<=I(b)/ln2. This is an actual Boolean embedding, not a relaxed spectral law. Consequently extending this gate to unrestricted data biases is at least full-FEI strength. Conversely no claim that full uniform FEI supplies the all-biased gate inequality is made.

### Composition corollary with every bias condition explicit

Consider a finite read-once formula on mutually disjoint physical child blocks. At each addressing node, all its data children have the same output mean under the ORIGINAL specified product input law; its address children may have arbitrary output means. Assume terminal children satisfy H^+<=C_leaf Delta. Then the root satisfies H^+<=max(4,C_leaf)Delta, with no factor depending on depth or any fan-in. For literal leaves C_leaf=0, the constant is 4.

For completeness, if an outer gate G receives child functions f_j with means mu_j and variances V_j>0, an occupied set of child blocks A and nonempty child subsets T_j determine exactly one physical Fourier set. Its coefficient is Ghat(A)product_{j in A} fhat_j(T_j)/sqrt(V_j). Taking corrected logs and expected degrees gives

H^+(G(f))=H^+(G)+sum_j alpha_j H^+(f_j),

Delta(G(f))=Delta(G)+sum_j alpha_j Delta(f_j), alpha_j=I_j(G)/V_j>=0.

Each gate and child term is therefore paid by the maximum constant; induction proves the corollary. Constant children are frozen before division. Deterministic address children reduce fan-in; common deterministic data means make the node constant. Equal data means are checked at the induced law at every node, not presumed to persist under arbitrary changes of the leaf law or arbitrary physical restrictions. For uniform physical input and balanced data subtrees, all addressing outputs are balanced and the corollary yields H<=4(I-1). If the root is unbalanced, H<=4(I-Var)+h0<=4I using h0<=Var/ln2. Generic read-once formulas over these gates with unrestricted inherited data biases are not covered.

# A044: sharp unequal-mean mux, arity-uniform closure, and the two-mean residual

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. The B09 lead asks A044 to drop the common-data-mean hypothesis in two-mean address gates. This stage finishes the unfinished predecessor's exact one-address-bit optimization and checks the transfer to arbitrary two-mean address tables. For every fixed interior address bias q, the three-input Boolean mux F(z,y0,y1)=(1-z)y0+z y1 has

    sup_{-1<m0,m1<1} H_mu^+(F)/Delta_mu(F)
      = K(q) = 2+2[1+g(max(q,1-q))]/ln2,
    g(t)=t ln(1/t)/(1-t).

The exact supremum over all three interior input biases is K*=2+4/ln2. It is finite and sharp. On independent-cell address tables of arbitrary arity whose data means take two values, the profile-scale entropy is paid from the actual centered-data deficit. The exact remaining charge is t^2 H_q^+(b), where b is the Boolean table assigning the two means and t=(m1-m0)/2. The same root deficit is spent only once.

An all-product-bias family with no equal-data-mean condition is also explicit: gates selecting between two distinct data wires according to the parity of a arbitrary-fan-in selector block. These gates and genuine disjoint read-once formulas built from them satisfy H^+<=K*Delta, uniformly in all physical and induced biases, depth and fan-in. Literals, AND/OR and parity may be included at the same maximum constant. This is a sharp quantitative application of known composition; neither the class's existence nor publication novelty is claimed new. All entropy is corrected entropy in bits; Delta=I-Var is spectral deficit, and I is spectral expected degree, not unweighted pivotal count.

Artifact complete locally. The fixed-q mux target is resolved on the page; unrestricted arbitrary-arity two-mean address tables retain universal biased FEI+ difficulty. Independent review/adoption remain pending. This is completion/repair of the already active A044 candidate plus a scoped independent review of A042's majority main theorem, respecting the B09 cap on new broad candidates.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Unequal-data-mean mux: exact spectrum and fixed-q supremum

Let z be Bernoulli q and let independent data signs have means m0,m1 and variances vj=1-mj^2. Put d=|m1-m0| and W=v0+v1. The actual normalized biased spectrum has exactly these possible masses:

    P(empty)=((1-q)m0+q m1)^2,
    P(z)=q(1-q)d^2,
    P(y0)=(1-q)^2 v0,       P(z,y0)=q(1-q)v0,
    P(y1)=q^2 v1,           P(z,y1)=q(1-q)v1.

Sets containing both data coordinates have zero mass. Thus Delta=q(1-q)W. Under interior data biases W>0. The address singleton contributes q(1-q)d^2 log2(4/d^2), interpreted as zero at d=0. With ell(r)=2(1-r)-2r log2 r, the remaining complete corrected entropy is (1-q)v0 ell(1-q)+q v1 ell(q). Dividing the sum by Delta gives the exact ratio

    R=2+[d^2 ln(4/d^2)+2v0 g(1-q)+2v1 g(q)]/[W ln2].       (1)

No ordinary Shannon entropy or missing empty-atom term is used in (1).

The integral representation g(t)=integral_0^1 t/[s+(1-s)t]ds shows g is strictly increasing on (0,1). Its limits are g(0)=0 and g(1)=1. For d in [0,2], write u=(m0+m1)/2; |u|<=1-d/2. Hence

    W=2-2u^2-d^2/2 >= d(2-d).                             (2)

The inequality ln x<=x-1, applied to x=2/d, gives

    d^2 ln(4/d^2)=2d^2 ln(2/d)<=2d(2-d)<=2W,              (3)

with d=0 treated continuously. Combining (1)--(3) and the weighted-average bound proves R<K(q) at every interior data pair. Strictness follows from (3) for d>0 and from its unused positive allowance when d=0.

To prove the exact lower bound, fix q<=1/2 and take m0=-1+e, m1=1-e^2, with e decreasing to zero. Then v0=2e-e^2, v1=2e^2-e^4, W=2e+e^2-e^4, and d=2-e-e^2. Consequently v0/W->1 and d^2 ln(4/d^2)/W->2, by ln(1+x)/x->1. Equation (1) tends to K(q). For q>=1/2 exchange the variance roles: m0=-1+e^2, m1=1-e. All data biases remain interior. The limiting contracted table is a literal; its zero deficit is not divided. Thus K(q) is a supremum over genuine interior laws, not an entropy ratio assigned to a degenerate function.

Finally g(t)<1 gives R<K*=2+4/ln2. The joint interior path q=e, m0=-1+e, m1=1-e^2 tends to K*. The recovered rational certificate at e=1/65536 proves R>777/100. At q=1/2 the exact fixed-selector supremum is 4+2/ln2, whereas the common-data-mean slice had maximum 4. If q is actually deterministic, contract it and obtain the selected literal with H^+=Delta=0; K(q)'s endpoint limit is not a value of a 0/0 quotient.

### 2. Arbitrary-arity independent-cell two-mean tables: exact root ownership

Let a selector bits have arbitrary independent probabilities q_i, and let c index all 2^a cells. Each cell has its own independent data sign. Its mean is m0 or m1 according to a Boolean label profile b(c) in {-1,1}. Put u=(m0+m1)/2, t=(m1-m0)/2, h=u+t b, r_ci=P(z_i=c_i), p_c=product_i r_ci and v_c=1-(u+t b(c))^2.

The mean-only spectrum and the distinct one-data-coordinate spectra are disjoint. Direct expansion yields

    H^+(F)=t^2 H_q^+(b)+t^2 Var_q(b)log2(1/t^2)+E_c,
    Delta(F)=t^2 Delta_q(b)+D_c,
    E_c=sum_c p_c v_c sum_i ell(r_ci),
    D_c=sum_c p_c v_c sum_i(1-r_ci).                       (4)

At t=0 the scale term is zero by continuity. These are the previously checked unequal-mean ledger, now supplied with a complete payment of its scale normalization.

For each selector coordinate i, averaging over its other coordinates gives exactly

    (D_c)_i=q_i(1-q_i) E_{-i}[v_{b(z_i=0)}+v_{b(z_i=1)}].

A crossing edge of b has the two different labels, so its bracket is W=v0+v1. A noncrossing edge has a nonnegative bracket. The actual spectral coordinate influence of b is 4q_i(1-q_i)P(edge crosses). Therefore

    D_c >= W I_q(b)/4.                                   (5)

Equation (3), with d=2|t|, gives t^2 ln(1/t^2)<=W/2. Poincare Var(b)<=I(b) and (5) pay the scale term by 2D_c natural units. Also -r ln r<=1-r gives (ln2)E_c<=(2ln2+2)D_c. Adding these two charges gives the arity-uniform repaired interface

    H^+(F)<=t^2 H_q^+(b)+K*D_c.                           (6)

Together with (4), a certified H_q^+(b)<=C_b Delta_q(b) implies H^+(F)<=max(C_b,K*)Delta(F). The scale charge and the centered-data charge are subdivisions of the one D_c reserve; no copy of t^2 Delta(b) is also spent on them. The 96 recovered exact profile cases cover all 16 two-address-bit Boolean label tables at three bias vectors and two mean pairs.

### 3. Two reused data wires: different coefficient, unrestricted induced means

The controlled two-wire gate M_b(z,y0,y1) selects y0 or y1 according to b(z), and is a genuine disjoint composition mux(b,y0,y1). It is not the independent-cell gate in (4). Let the actual output probability of b be q_b. The coefficient multiplying the selector profile is

    beta=I_selector(mux)/Var(b)=(1-m0 m1)/2,               (7)

because, conditional on data values, the outer sign derivative in its selector is (y1-y0)/2. Its squared expectation is (1-m0m1)/2. Exact disjoint composition therefore gives

    H^+(M_b)=H^+(mux at q_b,m0,m1)+beta H_q^+(b),
    Delta(M_b)=Delta(mux at q_b,m0,m1)+beta Delta_q(b).     (8)

The initial new pilot incorrectly imported t^2 from the mean-only sector in (4); the first parity-mux fixture detected it. For q_selector=(1/8,1/4,1/2), m0=-1/2,m1=1/2, the correct beta is 5/8, not 1/4. The preserved code/spec and exact diagnosis are in out/PILOT_FAILURE.md. The corrected code uses (7). This repair changes no recovered independent-cell theorem and supplies no repair to A042's majority proof.

For any profile with corrected constant C_b, (8) gives constant max(K(q_b),C_b), hence max(K*,C_b). In particular arbitrary-fan-in parity profiles have C_b=2/ln2, so every parity-controlled mux has constant K*. All physical input laws are permitted; there is no common-mean or equal-output restriction to be imposed after composition. The family includes a=1, where the sharp lower-bound path above makes K* optimal for the family.

For completeness the elementary parity bound is independently reconstructed. Put a_i=(E sign_i)^2, Z=product_i a_i, R=sum_i(1-a_i)-1+Z. Parity has Delta=R and natural corrected entropy Omega=sum_i(a_i-Z)ln(1/a_i). Appending another squared mean a changes R by (1-a)(1-Z), and changes Omega by (1-a)Z ln(1/Z)+a ln(1/a)(1-Z). Each of the two terms is at most (1-a)(1-Z), using -s ln s<=1-s. Starting from one literal, where R=Omega=0, proves Omega<=2R. Zero squared means follow by continuity. This is Lemma 7.5's established scalar mechanism, not a new parity claim.

At a generic disjoint composition G(f1,...,fk), let V_j=Var(fj)>0 and alpha_j=I_j(G)/V_j. Occupied child blocks A and their nonempty physical subsets T_j uniquely determine each output set; its coefficient is Ghat(A)product_j fhat_j(T_j)/sqrt(V_j). Splitting the corrected logarithm and counting physical degrees proves

    H^+(G(f))=H^+(G)+sum_j alpha_j H^+(fj),
    Delta(G(f))=Delta(G)+sum_j alpha_j Delta(fj).          (9)

All alpha_j and deficits are nonnegative. Induction applies the maximum constant to every finite read-once tree of parity-controlled muxes, literals and established AND/OR/parity gates, with arbitrary arities and no depth factor. Constant children are frozen before division. An outer mux with a constant selector reduces to one data child; with both data children constant it is a constant or signed selector. These contractions are explicitly checked. The original product law determines every induced mean used in (7)--(9).

On uniform physical inputs H=H^++h0, with h0=-m^2 log2(m^2)<=Var/ln2. Since K*>1/ln2, ordinary H<=K*I follows. A balanced root satisfies H<=K*(I-1). The broad existence of such read-once closure is prior O'Donnell--Tan; the delta is the explicit sharp mux constant, unequal-mean normalization and the complete frozen contraction checks.

### 4. Scoped independent review of A042's majority main theorem

The reviewed source is A042-SHARP-MAJORITY3-BIASED-ENDPOINT@1, statement hash 396cfaaabff2fa337f7a038ca4380cbede25d84a6ccd96032829a36dbecad11e. Only theorem (T), sections 1--3, is accepted in this review; the independent-rate 400-error certificate and full endpoint/edge classification are not accepted by this record.

Independently integrate the sign polynomial 2x1x2+2x1x3+2x2x3-4x1x2x3-1. If sigma_i^2=4p_i(1-p_i), normalized coefficients are c_i=p_j+p_k-2p_jp_k, c_ij=(1-2p_k)/2 and c_123=-1/2. With pair mass B and triple mass T=product_i sigma_i^2/4, Delta=B+2T. Indicator pivotal derivatives give sum_i sigma_i^2 c_i(1-c_i)=2B+3T. The scalar c^2 ln(1/c^2)<=2c(1-c) gives singleton entropy <=4Delta-2T. A pair's remaining natural logarithmic cost is (sigma_i^2 sigma_j^2/4)z ln(1/z), z=(1-2p_k)^2; -z ln z<=1-z makes this at most the very same T. Adding the three pair costs and the triple cost produces

    E <= (4+ln4)Delta-(ln4-1)T.                           (10)

This reconstructs the exact shared-triple ownership and favorable remainder. It uses no numerical optimization or source code from A042. The independent centered-moment implementation checks (10) on 27 cases, including fair inputs and zero pair coefficients. Verdict: HOLDS for the global inequality (10), no substantive repair supplied, reviewer A044 is not a contributor to the original version. This reduces an unpaid review obligation but does not mark the entire bundled boundary theorem accepted.

### 5. Exact first unsupported universal line

For arbitrary label tables b, (6) still contains t^2 H_q^+(b); (8) still contains beta H_q^+(b). There is no dimension-independent payment for either inherited Boolean profile entropy by its profile deficit in this run.

This is actual universal difficulty, not just a loose estimate. In independent-cell addressing choose means -s,+s according to any fixed Boolean profile b, s<1. All data biases are interior. Put v=1-s^2, A_q=2sum_i q_i(1-q_i), and S_q=sum_i[4q_i(1-q_i)-2q_i^2 log2 q_i-2(1-q_i)^2 log2(1-q_i)]. The exact identities are

    Delta(F_s)=s^2 Delta_q(b)+v A_q,
    H^+(F_s)=s^2 H_q^+(b)+s^2 Var_q(b)log2(1/s^2)+v S_q. (11)

For every fixed b with positive deficit the ratio tends to its own corrected ratio as s increases to one. Thus the supremum on all arbitrary-profile two-mean address tables over all arities and product laws equals the universal product-biased corrected FEI+ supremum, as an extended real number: one direction is class inclusion, and the reverse direction follows from (11). No claim that full uniform FEI implies universal biased FEI+ is made. Uniform b at deterministic endpoints already gives full uniform FEI difficulty. Rarity, two means, or an arity-uniform centered-data payment alone cannot close the conjecture.

NEXT must pay an actual higher-degree profile/prime residual, with its inherited terminal cost retained. Another mean-only base case would not move that universal obligation. No divergent corrected ratio or Boolean unbounded ordinary H/I family has been found.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Fiber rotation and uniform rare tax: HOLDS

Let p=P(X=1), g=f|X=0, h=f|X=1. For each residual set T let u=hat g(T), v=hat h(T), q=(1-p)u^2+p v^2. Zero-q fibers are omitted. The normalized physical-posterior vector is

    a=(sqrt(1-p) u, sqrt(p) v)/sqrt(q),

and the normalized spectral vector is

    b=((1-p)u+p v, sqrt(p(1-p))(v-u))/sqrt(q).

Exactly b=R a, R=[[sqrt(1-p),sqrt(p)],[-sqrt(p),sqrt(1-p)]]. Equivalently a=R^T b, which is the lead/earlier B09 convention b_physical=U a_spectral. Reversing the labels without transposing U is a notation error, not a substantive theorem change. Both vectors are unit, and their squared entries are respectively P(X|T) and P(S_X|T). The posterior comes from the physical experiment X drawn at its actual bias, followed by the actual child's squared spectrum.

Write M=sum_T q H(b_T^2), J=h2(p)-sum_T q H(a_T^2). In particular

    J-M = h2(p)-sum_T q[H(a_T^2)+H(b_T^2)].

For real coefficients put theta=arcsin(sqrt(p)); any real unit vector is parameterized by an angle phi. Consequently the sum of its two entropies is k(phi)+k(phi+theta), where k(t)=h2(sin^2 t), up to harmless sign changes. With m(p)=min_phi[k(phi)+k(phi+theta)], it follows exactly that J-M<=h2(p)-m(p), in any dimension and at all residual product biases. This variational upper bound does not assert that all minimizing vectors are attainable as all fibers of one Boolean function.

Here is a complete reconstruction of the explicit endpoint bound. Assume 0<p<=e^(-3); in fact the argument works for p<=1/16. Let d,e in [0,pi/4] be the distances of phi and phi+theta to the nearest coordinate axes (multiples of pi/2). Since theta<=pi/4, the triangle inequality on angles modulo pi/2 gives d+e>=theta. The function k is increasing on [0,pi/4] and

    k''(t)=(4/ln2)[cos(2t) ln(cot t)-1].

For 0<t<=theta, sin^2 t<=1/16, so cos(2t)>=7/8 and ln(cot t)>=ln15/2>7/6. For the last strict inequality use ln15>ln12=ln3+2ln2>1+4/3=7/3; e<3 and ln2>2/3 follow respectively from the exponential series and the first positive atanh term. Thus the bracket is greater than 49/48-1>0. Also e^3>16 follows already from the first five terms of its series (sum 16.375). Hence k is convex on [0,theta] throughout the claimed regime.

If d or e is at least theta, monotonicity and convexity give k(d)+k(e)>=k(theta)>=2k(theta/2). Otherwise Jensen and d+e>=theta give the same bound. Equality is attained for the scalar vector at phi=-theta/2. Therefore in this regime

    m(p)=2h2((1-sqrt(1-p))/2).

Put t=(1-sqrt(1-p))/2>=p/4. Monotonicity gives 2h2(t)>=2h2(p/4)>=(p/2)log2(1/p)+p. Also h2(p)<=p log2(1/p)+(log2 e)p, using -(1-p)ln(1-p)<=p. Subtracting proves

    J-M <= (p/2)log2(1/p)+(log2 e-1)p.

This is a complete analytic proof of the statement in the lead notes, stronger than the lead's finite floating grid. It gives no influence payment. The first unpaid line toward C019 is a bound for the summed conditional cost M plus these per-bit taxes and the retained terminal entropy, by nonreused physical influence.

### 2. Unequal-child-bias disjoint composition: HOLDS

For F=g(f_1,...,f_k) with independent disjoint child blocks, put eta_i=P(f_i=1), v_i=Var(f_i)>0. A nonempty physical set S uniquely identifies the occupied blocks A and nonempty pieces T_i. Centering each child in the outer normalized basis gives

    hat F(S)=hat g_eta(A) prod_{i in A} hat f_i(T_i)/sqrt(v_i),
    P_F(S)=P_g(A) prod_{i in A} P_i(T_i)/v_i.

The empty mass is P_g(empty), because the centered child terms have zero means. Each P_i(T)/v_i on nonempty T is normalized by Parseval. Thus this is the law of an actual Boolean composition, not merely a normalized candidate measure. Define alpha_i=I_i(g)/v_i. Summing degree, and then splitting the logarithm of the unique product mass, gives

    Var(F)=Var(g),  I(F)=sum_i alpha_i I(f_i),
    Hplus(F)=Hplus(g)+sum_i alpha_i Hplus(f_i),
    D(F)=D(g)+sum_i alpha_i D(f_i),  D=I-Var.

These derivations use the actual output biases eta, including unequal values. Zero-variance children must be substituted as constants before the parent law is formed. The exact checker reconstructs three full truth tables, tests every signed coefficient and mass, and verifies the entropy identity as a rational linear combination of prime logarithms, without rounding logs.

### 3. A042 equations (3)-(4) and deterministic contraction: HOLDS

Let a_T=hat g(T), b_T=hat h(T)-hat g(T). Direct integration of X gives

    hat f(T)=a_T+p b_T,
    hat f(T union {X})=sqrt(p(1-p)) b_T.

Writing D=sum_{|S|>=2}(|S|-1)P(S), expanding the squares gives

    D(f)=D(g)+p L-p^2 Q,
    L=2sum_{T nonempty}(|T|-1)a_T b_T+sum_T |T|b_T^2,
    Q=sum_{T nonempty}b_T^2.

The quadratic coefficient is ((|T|-1)-|T|)b_T^2=-b_T^2 for nonempty T; it is zero for the empty packet. This checks the potentially dangerous empty-atom cancellation. At p=0 or 1 the selected-coordinate basis is undefined; after physical contraction the surviving masses equal those of g or h, exactly as the polynomial formula says. No division by zero deficit or zero variance is licensed.

### 4. Realizability guards: normalized laws alone do not suffice

At an interior product law a proposed P is realizable exactly when signs can be chosen for coefficients eps_S sqrt(P(S)) such that their inverse normalized product transform takes values in {-1,1} at every physical input. Necessity is Fourier inversion; sufficiency is the same identity and uniqueness of the orthonormal coefficients. P>=0 and sum P=1 alone only ensure L2 norm one. For example, a unit singleton atom on one bit at p=1/3 reconstructs +/-phi with magnitudes sqrt(1/2),sqrt(2), and is not Boolean. The actual literal has masses (1/9,8/9).

Reused-variable guard: sign XOR with both wires equal to the same physical variable is constant +1, so its spectrum is concentrated at empty. Treating the two p=1/3 wires as independent gives empty mass 1/81 and two-wire mass 64/81. Hence the disjoint composition law is invalid after identification. With overlap, signed contributions collide and combine before squaring; normalization or marginal-bias matching cannot repair this.

Conditioning guard: the fair two-bit AND sign has four masses 1/4. Spectrally conditioning on X absent yields the residual masses (1/2,1/2). Physically restricting X=0 gives a constant child with masses (1,0). They are different laws. The correct physical-child/spectral joint experiment does share the unconditioned residual marginal with the parent; that marginal identity is the source of L=M+J, not an equality of these two conditionings.

# A045 stage 2: sharp taxes cannot be paid separately by biased deficit

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Audit the B09 lead's proposed gate payment `sum_i tau*(eta_i) <= K delta_eta(g)` on address gates and symmetric thresholds of arity at most six, where `delta=I-Var`, `I` is spectral influence, and `tau*` is the sharp scalar singleton uncertainty tax. Test both the raw input sum and the actual reach-weighted query bill with constant/literal leaves. All gates are genuine Boolean signs on independent product inputs. Entropies and taxes are bits. Biases are the actual gate-input probabilities, not squared-spectrum marginals.

Outcome: the continuum supremum is infinite already for an essential binary AND with equal biases tending to zero. This remains true for the best singleton gate-input query tree stopped at zero-deficit leaves, and even the actual positive Shannon correction `(J-M)_+` cannot be paid separately by root biased deficit. The corrected entropy remains bounded: `Hplus/delta -> 2` on the same family. This refutes a payment mechanism, not FEI. The finite exact search covers 126 threshold and 32 address cases, with certified finite-grid maxima. A separate independent reconstruction accepts A042's existing sharp all-bias binary endpoint claim at its stated scope, reducing the review backlog rather than opening another binary class target.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

At stage start read BOARD.md, supervisor snapshot 2026-10-13 21:24 UTC; B09 STREAM_NOTES.md v2, 2026-10-11 23:33 UTC; own STATUS.md, LEARNED.md and WORKLOG.md. Re-read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2. The B09 lead explicitly assigns the tax-sum versus deficit test and asks for arity trends. A098 stage 2 retires repeated half-tax reviews and directs A045 toward the higher-level normalized erasure frontier. This stage does not recompute or claim the half-tax endpoint coefficient as new; it tests its proposed downstream payment and consumes a binary review dependency.

Critical source originals inspected: B09 lead CLAIMS.json, `B09-CLAUDE-SHARP-ONE-BIT-TAX@1`, including the proof text in its proof_source field (REPORT.md was not written by the lead harness); prior A045 REPORT.md, Section 1, scalar convexity on p<=1/16; A100 REPORT.md tax-tree diagnostic (unsharp A096 tax positive at fair bias); A098 stage-2 REPORT.md retirement and `R_ge2` frontier; A042 stage-2 REPORT.md equations (1)-(9), `A042-SHARP-BINARY-PRIME-ENDPOINT@1`; A024 REPORT.md equations (1)-(7), signed normalization and child law transfer; A050 REPORT.md equal-overlap repair and terminal exclusions; A057 stage-2 REPORT.md capped activation bank and unpaid inner/terminal term; A056 disjoint-composition and physical-scope reports. Threshold 38pp Section 6.1 and Proposition 7.1 are the inherited normalization baseline. No percolation theorem or online source is a premise. Local source receipts distinguish raw files and hashes from repository or mirror verification.

The new obstruction is distinct from A100: A100 prices the weaker `h2(p)-h2(1-sqrt(1-p))`, which is positive at p=1/2; the present sharp tax is exactly zero there, and the obstruction uses interior biases approaching zero. No existing source is edited and no global invalidation is inferred from a helper failure.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Exact, realizable input laws and frozen finite domain

Let `p_m=1-(1-2^-m)^2`, for m=5,6,8,10,14, and include the fair bias 1/2. Each p_m<=1/16 has the rational half-angle parameter

    sqrt(1-p_m)=1-2^-m,
    (1-sqrt(1-p_m))/2=2^(-m-1).

The already reconstructed scalar convexity formula therefore gives the exact tax expression

    tau*(p_m)=h2(p_m)-2h2(2^(-m-1)).                 (1)

At p=1/2 the sharp tax is zero. No approximate scalar minimizer is used. These are actual possible induced means from uniform physical children: OR of two AND_m indicators on disjoint uniform blocks has probability p_m. Giving each gate wire its own fresh pair of blocks realizes the independent product law. A fair wire is a fresh literal. This verifies Boolean realizability without enumerating the much larger substituted truth table.

There is a scope distinction: querying a gate input in its product space is a valid gate restriction. Querying the output of a composed child is not automatically a singleton physical restriction of the underlying uniform cube; conditioning a child block on its output can correlate its physical inputs. The present gate-level bill must not be silently promoted to an underlying physical-tree budget.

For a symmetric threshold `2*1{sum X_i>=t}-1`, every truth-table coefficient and squared mass is computed by an exact centered tensor transform. In sign input coordinates, one transform sends a pair (a,b) to `((1-p)a+pb,(b-a)/2)`; a coefficient c_S then has mass `c_S^2 prod_{i in S}4p_i(1-p_i)`. This preserves all signs before squaring and includes the empty coefficient. An independent mixed-difference computation checks every subset coefficient for threshold arity at most four. The larger specified arities are still full actual truth tables, not candidate spectral laws.

### 2. The requested raw tax payment is false, already at arity two

Consider the essential sign conjunction `f_n=2 prod_{i=1}^n X_i-1`, every input independently Bernoulli(p), 0<p<1. Direct pivotal conditioning or the exact Boolean spectrum gives

    I(f_n)=4n p^n(1-p),
    Var(f_n)=4p^n(1-p^n),
    delta(f_n)=4p^n[n(1-p)-1+p^n].                 (2)

For fixed n>=2, the previously established sharp tax asymptotic `tau*(p)=(p/2)log2(1/p)+O(p)` yields

    n tau*(p)/delta(f_n)
       ~ n/[8(n-1)] p^(1-n) log2(1/p).             (3)

Thus the all-interior-bias supremum is infinite for every fixed n>=2. No growth of arity is needed. In particular `delta(AND_2)=4p^2(1-p)^2`, and the ratio is asymptotic to `log2(1/p)/(4p)`. The sequence p_m gives exact rational instances on this path. This is the first failing line in the lead's proposed payment; finite-grid ratios are not promoted to a finite continuum bound.

Unary gates already have delta=0 and positive rare tax, but they are legitimate terminal gates with Hplus=0. The counterexample deliberately uses essential binary gates with strictly positive deficit, so it does not rely on dividing by zero or charging an irrelevant coordinate.

The finite grid's worst threshold at each arity n=3,...,6 is AND_n at m=14. At n=2, OR_2 and AND_2 tie exactly because their deficits and raw numerators are identical at equal bias. The diagnostic ratios (exact rational enclosures in CHECK_OUTPUT.json) are:

| arity | worst raw tax/deficit on the frozen grid |
|---|---:|
| 2 | 25,488.4763517 |
| 3 | 156,596,420.3449 |
| 4 | 1.14031193077e12 |
| 5 | 8.75777379660e15 |
| 6 | 6.88756171192e19 |

Each finite-grid maximum is certified by disjoint rational intervals after grouping exact rational ties. These values describe only the frozen domain. Formula (3) supplies the infinite continuum supremum.

### 3. Sequential exposure does not repair the AND obstruction

Define a gate-query tax bill as the sum over queried singleton nodes of their actual product reach probability times tau*(p_i). Require every terminal restriction to be a constant or signed literal, equivalently delta=Hplus=0. This admits early stopping at affine leaves and retains no uncharged nonaffine leaf.

For AND_2 every admissible deterministic policy must query at least one input: the root has positive deficit. Either query gives a constant child when the input is zero and a literal child when it is one, so exactly one query attains the minimum. Both wires have the same tax. Therefore

    inf_policy bill(AND_2)=tau*(p),
    inf_policy bill/delta ~ log2(1/p)/(8p).        (4)

Randomized policies are mixtures and have the same lower bound. A full computing policy, rather than affine-leaf stopping, pays tau*(p)(1+p). Additional queries cannot lower the nonnegative tax bill. The obstruction is therefore not only an unconditional input-sum overcharge.

For AND_n, until a zero is found or one input remains, all surviving restrictions are AND gates on the remaining wires. Every adaptive choice has the same state transition at equal independent bias. Consequently the exact best affine-leaf bill is

    tau*(p) sum_{j=0}^{n-2}p^j.                    (5)

Its ratio diverges as `[8(n-1)]^-1 p^(1-n)log2(1/p)`. The checker evaluates all symmetric threshold stopping policies via the exact state recurrence `Q(n,t)=1+(1-p)Q(n-1,t)+pQ(n-1,t-1)`, with Q=0 at constants/literals. The order independence here follows from symmetry and independence; it is not claimed for general gates. Allowing a nonaffine terminal root with separately paid Hplus could bypass the tax bill; that is a different mechanism, not refuted by (4).

### 4. Address gates expose the distinction between raw input sum and a stopped policy

Let a=1 or 2 address bits choose among N=2^a independent data bits, all address bits of bias r and all data bits of bias s; output is the chosen data sign. Then

    Var=4s(1-s),
    delta=8a r(1-r)s(1-s),
    raw tax=a tau*(r)+N tau*(s).                   (6)

To verify the deficit, data influences sum to 4s(1-s). Flipping a selector replaces one independent data bit by another; their disagreement probability is 2s(1-s), so each selector spectral influence is `8r(1-r)s(1-s)`. This uses the actual product law and accounts for all data labels.

Querying only addresses, then stopping at the selected literal, has bill `a tau*(r)`. If r=1/2 this is zero even for rare data, while the raw input bill divided by deficit diverges as `N/(4a)log2(1/s)`. Moreover in this fair-selector/common-data-bias case the exact corrected entropy is `Hplus=2a Var=4 delta`. Thus the raw input sum can count unnecessary data queries even on a well-paid address gate.

If data are fair but selectors tend to zero, the address-first bill divided by deficit is asymptotic to `log2(1/r)/4`; if r=s=p tends to zero, that bill/deficit is asymptotic to `log2(1/p)/(16p)`. We do not claim that address-first is optimal among all address-gate query orders. The AND obstruction already settles the best-policy universal failure. The search covers the three specified address regimes and retains all actual data labels.

### 5. Even the actual positive Shannon correction cannot be paid separately by delta

The blind scalar tax might seem merely inefficient. On AND_2 with both biases p, the actual correction has a separate obstruction. In order empty, X, Y, XY, its exact squared spectrum is

    P=((1-2p^2)^2,4p^3(1-p),4p^3(1-p),4p^2(1-p)^2).

Let v=4p(1-p). The physical X=0 child is constant and X=1 is the Y literal, with nonempty spectral mass v. The residual parent spectral bit has inclusion probability pv. Thus

    J=h2(pv)-p h2(v),
    M=H(P)-h2(pv),
    J-M=2h2(pv)-H(P)-p h2(v).                     (7)

These are physical-child/spectral mixture quantities. Spectral event conditioning is never substituted for those children. A direct simultaneous-bias expansion of the four explicit masses gives, with L=log2(1/p),

    h2(pv)=8p^2 L+(4log2(e)-8)p^2+O(p^3 L),
    H(P)=8p^2 L+(4log2(e)-8)p^2+O(p^3 L),
    p h2(v)=4p^2 L+(4log2(e)-8)p^2+O(p^3 L).

The leading constants cancel in (7), hence

    (J-M)/delta(AND_2)=log2(1/p)+O(p log2(1/p)).    (8)

For completeness, the remainders are justified by `-(1-x)ln(1-x)=x-R(x)`, where `0<=R(x)<=x^2/[2(1-x)]`, obtained by the convergent series `R=sum_{k>=2}x^k/[k(k-1)]`. Write each small mass as a fixed power of p times a nonvanishing polynomial and use `|ln(1-p)|<=p/(1-p)`. These estimates are uniform for 0<p<=1/16. This is a direct finite-polynomial path calculation, not an application of a fixed-child O(p) expansion while the residual bias moves. Equation (8) proves eventual positivity and a divergent ratio for the actual positive correction.

Five exact interval certificates have `(J-M)/delta` about 3.35394, 4.59188, 6.85328, 8.95179, 12.99553; their ratios to log2(1/p_m) are about .83375, .91630, .97865, .99457, .99965. At m=14 the certificate gives `12.99552 < (J-M)/delta < 12.99554`. The analytic calculation proves the limit; the intervals corroborate it.

### 6. Preserve corrected normalization before charging: the large logarithm cancels

For any finite Boolean sign under independent interior product biases, write `P0=(E f)^2`, `e(z)=z log2(1/z)`, `v_i=4p_i(1-p_i)`. The definition gives exactly

    Hplus(f)=H(P)-e(P0)+sum_i I_i(f)log2(v_i).     (9)

At a physical singleton query i, let Delta be parent minus actual biased average of its two children. Residual spectral marginals equal physical averages, so for j!=i, `Delta I_j=0`. The complete one-node ledgers are

    Delta Hplus=M_i+J_i+I_i log2(v_i)-Delta e(P0),
    Delta delta=I_i-P({i})
               =sum_{T nonempty}P(T union {i}).    (10)

The second equation uses `Delta Var=Var(E[f|X_i])=P({i})`. Both terms are nonnegative in the deficit decrement. These are the elementary corrected-entropy/variance chain rules; they specialize the existing A024 normalization interface rather than claiming a new class theorem.

For every actual gate restriction tree, telescoping (10) preserves the terminal terms:

    Hplus(root)=E Hplus(leaves)+sum_v reach_v[complete bracket in (10)],
    delta(root)=E delta(leaves)+sum_v reach_v[I_i-P({i})].

This is an identity, not a universal payment. Terminal Hplus and deficit cannot be omitted except at proved zero-deficit leaves.

On the same AND_2 family, actual children are constant/literal and their corrected entropies vanish. Direct normalized Fourier calculation gives

    Hplus/delta=2+[4p/(1-p)]log2(1/p) -> 2.         (11)

Thus the unbounded Shannon correction in (8) is canceled by the signed bias and empty-sector terms before payment. Charging tau* or `(J-M)_+` separately throws away precisely that cancellation. Neither obstruction disproves corrected product-biased FEI+, nor ordinary uniform FEI for the actual substituted Boolean family. The underlying disjoint AND/OR realization lies in the already paid binary read-once class.

**First unsupported line:** for higher prime gates or actual composed physical trees, pay the entire signed corrected bracket / recentered outer residual against one owned deficit or influence budget, together with all retained inner and terminal entropy. Equations (9)-(10) do not give that bound by themselves. A bound on scalar taxes alone is now rigorously excluded as that missing premise.

### 7. Independent review of A042's queued binary endpoint: HOLDS at stated scope

All essential binary tables are eight exceptional-corner gates and two parity gates. Output negation and input reflection with the corresponding bias reflection preserve each normalized squared mass and its input variance. Put `A(x)=x ln(1/x)/(1-x)`, with A(0)=0, A(1)=1. The inequality `ln y<=y-1` gives 0<A(x)<1, and `A'(x)=(-ln x-1+x)/(1-x)^2>0` proves strict monotonicity.

For a corner gate, let u,v be the two actual probabilities of its exceptional input values. The masses are `(2uv-1)^2,4u(1-u)v^2,4v(1-v)u^2,4u(1-u)v(1-v)`. Dividing its corrected entropy by its pair deficit gives `2+2[A(u)+A(v)]/ln2`. Its sharp supremum is 2+4/ln2, approached precisely when u,v both tend to one, with independently arbitrary rates. For parity, put a=4p(1-p), b=4q(1-q); the ratio is `[A(1-a)+A(1-b)]/ln2`, with sharp supremum 2/ln2 as both variances tend to zero. Fair zero singleton packets are omitted correctly; the fair unit pair still has positive deficit. Deterministic contracted gates have Hplus=delta=0 and no defined ratio.

The earlier independently reconstructed disjoint composition law has nonnegative weights alpha_i and exact `Hplus(F)=Hplus(g)+sum alpha_i Hplus(f_i)`, `delta(F)=delta(g)+sum alpha_i delta(f_i)` at actual induced means. Induction therefore transfers the sharp common binary constant through a genuine disjoint binary read-once tree, with constants contracted. On uniform physical inputs, `e((E F)^2)<=Var(F)/ln2` turns the corrected bound into ordinary `H<= [2+4/ln2] I`. No fixed bias relation, depth penalty or unpaid deterministic division is introduced.

This reconstructs every assertion in `A042-SHARP-BINARY-PRIME-ENDPOINT@1`, without the source checker and without supplying a substantive repair. The independent finite check covers all 16 tables at 25 rational bias pairs (400 cases): 200 corner, 50 parity, 150 constant/literal cases. Exact prime-log coefficient equality verifies both formulas, including signs and zero packets. The source's extra quantitative remainder inequalities are not part of that exact claim statement and are not separately certified here. The review does not extend the binary constant to higher primes.

# A045 stage 3: mixed spectral references and Boolean admission

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Referee A041-COHERENT-PURE-SECTOR-PAYMENT@1 and audit its proposed mixed-sector continuation. The existing claim holds at its exact stated scope, conditional on the explicitly cited symmetric-threshold baseline. The new result is a scoped reference/admission audit, not a weighted-threshold or universal FEI theorem.

Let F be an actual Boolean sign on disjoint physical coordinate groups A,B under an independent product law with interior biases p_i. Use X_i in {0,1}, v_i=4p_i(1-p_i), phi_i=(2X_i-1-(2p_i-1))/sqrt(v_i), P(U,T)=Fhat(U,T)^2 and v_U=product_{i in U}v_i. Corrected entropy E=sum_{S nonempty}P(S) ln(v_S/P(S)) is in nats. Delta=I-Var=sum_{S nonempty}(|S|-1)P(S). Zero summands are omitted. This normalized product basis uses actual physical biases, including unequal biases, not biases inferred from a spectral conditional law.

On the mixed sector U,T nonempty put M=sum P, a(U)=sum_T P(U,T), b(T)=sum_U P(U,T), d(U,T)=|U|+|T| and Delta_m=sum(d-1)P. For M>0 define Q0=a b/M^2 and Qd=(d-1)a b/(M Delta_m). Both are normalized reference laws on the FULL mixed label space, including labels of zero actual mass. Neither is asserted to be a Boolean spectrum. Contract deterministic coordinates before using the normalized basis. If M=0, mixed entropy and its obligation vanish; do not form any conditional distribution or divisor.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independent review of the complete A041 claim

For s>0 define psi_s(z)=z^2 ln(s/z^2), continuously extended at zero. The function psi_s(z)+3z^2 has second derivative 2 ln(s/z^2)>=0 on |z|<=sqrt(s), with a continuous first derivative at zero. Jensen gives psi_s(E z)<=E psi_s(z)+3 Var(z) for arbitrary signed amplitudes in this interval. No entropy of the slice labels is needed.

For any actual Boolean physical slice f_x(B), |fhat_x(T)|<=E|phi_T|=sqrt(v_T), since E|phi_i|=sqrt(v_i) and the coordinates are independent. In the signed coefficient process c_T(x)=fhat_x(T), the A-Fourier coefficients are Fhat(U,T). Thus E_A c_T=Fhat(empty,T) and Var_A c_T=sum_{U nonempty}P(U,T). Jensen summed over |T|>=2 proves exactly A041's coherent pure-B bound, and likewise for A. Physical restrictions are Boolean; the averaged function E_A F need not be Boolean, which is why its squared spectrum cannot replace the slice spectra.

Orient each negative weight by flipping its physical coordinate and bias, delete zero weights, and freeze deterministic coordinates. Restrictions to a positive equal-weight group G_j are symmetric thresholds, even with unequal biases. Let a_j(S)=|S intersect G_j| and r(S) count occupied groups. Atomwise, |S|-1=sum_{j:a_j>0}(a_j-1)+(r(S)-1). Therefore Delta=sum_j D_j+R with the claim's definitions. Parseval identifies D_j as the expected physical slice deficit. Every corrected entropy summand is nonnegative by the coefficient bound above. The baseline pays expected slice degree>=2 entropy by c D_j, c=460+80 ln2. Summing Jensen's variance charges costs at most

    3 sum_{S:r(S)>=2} #{j:a_j(S)>=2} P(S)
       <=6 sum_S(r(S)-1)P(S)=6R.

This proves E_pure,ge2<=c sum_j D_j+6R<=cDelta. In two groups R=M. In all-distinct weights the pure higher-degree sector is empty, so this theorem does not pay the mixed sector. No weight ratios, equal biases, or bounded arity were assumed.

For completeness the source's singleton reduction also follows directly: let signed derivative mean a_i and pivotal probability rho_i. Then |a_i|<=rho_i, I_i=v_i rho_i and P({i})=v_i a_i^2. Since x ln(1/x)<=1-x, the singleton contribution is <=2v_i(rho_i-a_i^2). Also sum_i[I_i-P({i})]=sum_{|S|>=2}|S|P(S)<=2Delta. Hence all singleton corrected entropy costs <=4Delta nats. This does not pay mixed entropy or allow Delta to be spent twice without adding the constants.

For the actual fair conjunction on m coordinates in each group, realizable as weights 2 and 1 with threshold 3m, every nonempty mass is P=4^(1-2m). Therefore M=(2^m-1)^2P and E_mix/M=2(2m-1)ln2, divergent. Delta_m/M=2m*2^(m-1)/(2^m-1)-1 grows with m. This verifies the claim's mass-only obstruction without alleging an FEI counterexample.

Direct expansion, with E_A^m=sum_U a ln(v_U/a), gives

    E_mix=E_A^m+E_B^m+M ln M-M TC_spec,
    TC_spec=KL(P/M || Q0)>=0.

This reference is complete on its declared mixed sector and normalized; it is spectral total correlation, not physical total correlation conditioned on a gate event. None of these conclusions requires Q0 or its marginals to be Boolean spectra. All clauses of A041's exact claim are accepted at their stated scope; no strengthening to E_mix<=KDelta is accepted.

### 2. Boolean admission fails after spectral conditioning

At bias p=1/4 the actual one-bit Boolean spectra are only the constant law (1,0) or the literal law (1-v,v)=(1/4,3/4). The normalized law (0,1) is NOT Boolean: its inverse is the normalized centered literal, whose values are -1/sqrt(3), sqrt(3). An actual OR sign with weights 2,1, threshold 1 and two biases 1/4 has M=9/64. Conditioning on its sole mixed Fourier set yields both marginals (0,1). Thus normalization, physical Booleanity of the original gate, and a perfectly valid reference do not license applying a Boolean-gate inequality to either conditional marginal. This example is a physical threshold, not an abstract probability law.

For fair AND4 split into groups of size two, all nonempty P=1/64, M=9/64, both normalized mixed marginals are (0,1/3,1/3,1/3), and Q0 is uniform on nine mixed labels. A two-bit Boolean coefficient is an integer multiple of 1/4, so its squared mass is an integer square divided by 16. Mass 1/3 is impossible. On four fair bits coefficients are integers divided by 16; Q0 mass 1/9 is likewise impossible (256/9 is not even an integer). This proves that a normalized product reference can fail joint Boolean admission.

The converse inference also fails. On four fair physical bits define

    F=(2X0-1)(2X2-1)(-1)^(X1 X3),
    A={0,1}, B={2,3}.

This is an actual Boolean truth table. Its four Fourier masses are 1/4 at (U,T) in {{0},{0,1}} x {{2},{2,3}}, all mixed. Both marginals are (0,1/2,0,1/2), not Boolean on two fair bits, because 16*(1/2)=8 is not an integer square. Nevertheless Q0=P is itself Boolean-realizable and TC_spec=0. The 2x2 signed coefficient matrix has nonzero determinant: phases are coupled across groups. Product squared magnitudes do not force product Boolean functions.

The fair examples are applications of the existing A032 uniform admission and four-point affine-plane classification, not a new phase classification. Their purpose here is to test the mixed-reference interface before importing a Boolean inequality. The catalog addresses inherited biased marginals and does not repeat A032's four-bit enumeration.

Precisely, for arbitrary interior product biases and normalized nonnegative laws q_A,q_B, a joint product law q_A q_B has a Boolean realization with separable coefficient signs iff each marginal has a Boolean realization in its inherited basis. To prove necessity, separable signs reconstruct F(x,y)=a(x)b(y), with each factor's L2 norm one. Full physical support and F in {+1,-1} imply neither factor vanishes and |a(x)| is a constant alpha, |b(y)|=1/alpha. Unit norms force alpha=1. Each factor is Boolean. Conversely tensor any two Boolean realizations on disjoint inputs. A product law may have a Boolean realization with nonseparable signs despite failing marginal admission, as the explicit witness shows. No claim about exhaustive phases in arbitrary dimensions is made.

### 3. Exact degree-weighted reference and the KL distinction

Write R0=P/M on mixed labels. Its two marginals are a/M,b/M. Product Q0 preserves each degree mean, since d-1=(|U|-1)+|T| is additive. Therefore

    Z=sum(d-1)Q0=Delta_m/M,
    Qd=(d-1)Q0/Z=(d-1)a b/(M Delta_m),
    Rd=(d-1)P/Delta_m.

Qd and Rd are normalized, Delta_m>=M>0, and Qd is positive wherever P is positive. Their pointwise likelihood is unchanged:

    Rd/Qd=R0/Q0=M P/(a b).

Their KL averages are NOT unchanged: KL(Rd||Qd) weights that log by (d-1)P/Delta_m, whereas TC_spec weights it by P/M. The mixed entropy identity above must retain TC_spec with its original measure. To rewrite that rebate under Rd one needs the harmonic factor:

    M TC_spec=Delta_m E_Rd[ln(MP/(ab))/(d-1)].

For the actual five-bit threshold 2(X0+X1)+X2+X3+X4>=3 at all biases 1/4, the checker certifies M=60993/262144, Delta_m=122391/262144. TC_spec lies in [3301600245612203/200000000000000000, 2063500153507627/125000000000000000], while KL(Rd||Qd) lies in [825202941607491/50000000000000000, 16504058832149821/1000000000000000000]. Their difference lies in the strictly negative rational interval [-985598977799/250000000000000000,-788479182239/200000000000000000]. Equality of pointwise likelihoods is therefore insufficient for exchanging the rebates. The full exact P and rigorous intervals are saved in out/CHECK_OUTPUT.json.

The exact payment obligation, retaining the actual normalization, is

    E_mix/Delta_m=E_Rd[ln(v_U v_T/P(U,T))/(d-1)].

This stage proves the normalization and interface, NOT a uniform bound on the right-hand side. The first unsupported line is that this expectation is bounded by one absolute K for all actual two-weight thresholds, or that a complete signed reference pays it with a root-Delta budget after including all remaining sectors. The general logarithmic bound is still open; numerical certificates in a frozen finite domain do not prove it.

### 4. Deterministic inputs, reused variables and physical conditioning

At p_i=0 or 1 the normalized phi_i is undefined. Freeze X_i first, obtain the contracted Boolean function and its interior basis, and compute its actual spectrum there. The centered tensor formula has a polynomial extension whose masses containing the frozen coordinate vanish; the eight exact endpoint fixtures compare that extension with the contracted truth table. This is not a definition of a normalized basis on a deterministic input. Limits of ratios and ratios of contracted functions remain different questions.

Every factorization here requires disjoint physical coordinate groups. Reusing a literal in two gate wires gives, for example, XOR(X,X)=0, a constant sign with unit empty mass, whereas independent fair copies have unit mass on the two-coordinate parity label. One must substitute repeated variables into the truth table before taking the Fourier transform; relabeling two occurrences as independent invalidates both product bias and spectral convolution.

Physical conditioning means restricting a truth table at X_A=x with its original remaining product biases; its amplitudes average coherently. Spectral conditioning means renormalizing masses after an event such as U,T nonempty; its marginals are abstract laws of labels. The one-bit example above distinguishes them exactly. Degree tilting is a third change of spectral measure, and requires the harmonic correction just derived. No physical tree, inner-child payment, empty-sector correction or terminal entropy has been eliminated by any of these operations.

# A045 stage 4: a quadratic physical-tail reference with an owned energy budget

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite actual two-weight Boolean sign F=2*1{w N_A+N_B>=t}-1, w>0 real, arbitrary threshold and interior independent physical biases (unequal within a group allowed), set v_i=4p_i(1-p_i). For nonempty T subset B let h_T(k)=Fhat_B(T;N_A=k)/sqrt(v_T). These g_j are genuine physical Boolean gates; the h_T process generally is not Boolean. Freeze deterministic coordinates before forming this basis. Empty groups or zero mixed mass require no reference.

New scoped result: the squared-tail INFLUENCE budget is exactly the actual mixed A-influence and is bounded by the mixed/root deficit. The remaining complete-reference cross-entropy bound and full FEI are open.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators
