## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independent acceptance of every clause of the new A041 statement

For real score z, h_T(z) jumps at z=(t-l)/w by 2 E_B[1{N_B=l}phi_T]/sqrt(v_T). The level events partition physical B inputs, so the total absolute jump is at most 2 E|phi_T|/sqrt(v_T)=2, since E|phi_i|=sqrt(v_i). For nonempty T both exterior values are zero. Restriction to attained counts cannot increase variation. The canonical tail decomposition follows by discrete telescoping; its absolute coefficients sum to at most one. Integrating its nonempty A Fourier coefficients gives Fhat(U,T)/sqrt(v_T)=sum_j alpha_Tj ghat_j(U). All biases here are physical; spectral conditional marginals need not be Boolean, and none is used.

For A041's actual family take two fair A bits, n=4r+1 fair B bits, w=n+1 and t=w+(n+1)/2. The attained physical branches are -1,g,+1, where g is balanced odd majority. With s_i=2X_i-1,

    F=(s_1+s_2)/2+(1-s_1s_2)g/2.

Thus the canonical coefficients are ghat(T)/2 and -ghat(T)/2; the OR2/AND2 tail deficits are 1/4. Hence B_abs=||ghat||_1/4, Delta(F)=I(g)/2, M=1/4, E_mix=[E(g)+ln4]/4, Delta_m=[I(g)+1]/4 and E(F)=E(g)/2+ln4. The signs of the mixed heavy-pair coefficients are minus ghat(T)/2; the sign has no effect on masses but must be retained in the coherent derivation.

Differentiate majority in one selected bit. The derivative is the indicator that the remaining 4r bits have exactly 2r ones. For 2r further selected bits, its signed level sum is the coefficient of z^(2r) in (1-z)^(2r)(1+z)^(2r)=(1-z^2)^(2r). Therefore every degree2r+1 coefficient has magnitude binom(2r,r)/2^(4r). The maximal central binomial term is at least the binomial sum divided by its number of terms. Applying this to both factors gives ||ghat||_1>=2^(2r)/(2r+1)^2. Since I(g)<=n,

    B_abs/Delta(F)>=2^(2r)/[2(4r+1)(2r+1)^2] -> infinity.

This proves the infinite-family obstruction without extrapolating finite tests. The exact E formulas also show why it is not an FEI counterfamily. No gap is found within A041's complete exact statement. The symmetric-threshold baseline remains its contextual diagnosis, not a dependency of the obstruction. The new downstream quadratic theorem is separate work, not a repair to A041's original version.

### 2. The quadratic influence budget is exact, with arbitrary unequal biases

For a real physical function h(A), define D_i h=[h(X_i=1)-h(X_i=0)]/2. The product Fourier identity is I_A(h)=sum_i v_i E[(D_i h)^2]. At any physical A edge with other-count k,

    D_i g_j=1{k=j-1}.

Distinct count tails have disjoint derivative supports at every edge. Therefore D_i h_T=alpha_T,k+1, and all cross products between different tail derivatives vanish pointwise. This gives the exact diagonal identity

    I_A(h_T)=sum_j alpha_Tj^2 I_A(g_j).

It is valid even though the functions themselves and their spectra are not orthogonal, and even when A-coordinate biases differ. The constant in h_T contributes no influence. Squaring nonempty coefficients and summing over T proves

    B_quad=sum_{T nonempty}v_T sum_j alpha_Tj^2 I_A(g_j)
          =sum_{U,T nonempty}|U|P(U,T).

For each mixed atom |T|>=1, so |U|<=|U|+|T|-1. Consequently

    B_quad<=Delta_mixed<=Delta(F).

This is an exact ownership statement with constant one, not an estimate inferred from a finite grid. It uses influence rather than the false absolute-weight deficit budget. Spending it for both orientations without accounting is invalid: our ledger fixes A as the paid derivative direction. A convex combination of the two orientation budgets is also bounded by Delta_mixed, but their sum is not a second independent reserve.

### 3. A complete normalized quadratic reference and its normalizer cost

Define, on U,T nonempty,

    K(U,T)=v_T sum_j alpha_Tj^2 ghat_j(U)^2,
    Z=sum K=sum_T v_T sum_j alpha_Tj^2 Var(g_j).

Since Var(g_j)<=I_A(g_j), 0<=Z<=B_quad<=Delta. If P(U,T)>0, at least one summand in the coherent amplitude sum is nonzero, so K(U,T)>0. Thus M=sum_mixed P>0 implies Z>0. For M>0, Q=K/Z is a complete normalized reference on the mixed label space; R=P/M is the actual conditional spectral law. Retain possible Q-positive/P-zero labels when normalizing. No Boolean admission of Q is asserted or needed. Each tail g_j is Boolean, but its mixture is a reference law of labels.

Let C_ref=sum_mixed P ln(v_U v_T/K). Direct log expansion gives

    E_mix=C_ref+M ln(Z/M)-M KL(R||Q).

This is an ordinary KL with the original mixed measure, so its negative rebate is genuine. It does not replace spectral TC by a degree-weighted KL. The mass-normalizer cost can be positive because coherent cancellation changes the actual total mass. Nevertheless, for x=M/Z, x ln(1/x)<=1/e (differentiate; the maximum is at x=1/e). Therefore

    [M ln(Z/M)]_+<=Z/e<=3Delta/8.

The final rational bound follows from e>=1+1+1/2+1/6+1/24=65/24>8/3. This normalizer payment is independent of arity and every physical bias. It cannot pay C_ref without another argument.

The bounded-variation representation also shows K<=v_U v_T: every Boolean tail coefficient satisfies |ghat_j(U)|<=sqrt(v_U), and sum alpha_Tj^2<= (sum|alpha_Tj|)^2<=1. Thus C_ref is nonnegative. Nonnegativity is not an upper bound.

To combine with the previous product-marginal reference Q0 without losing its TC rebate, retain the signed conversion exactly:

    TC_spec=KL(R||Q)+E_R ln(Q/Q0).

Either use the new complete identity above or carry that conversion term. It is not free, and the two negative rebates cannot be taken simultaneously. If a degree tilt is introduced later, every ordinary rebate still requires the prior harmonic factor under that tilted measure.

### 4. Exact control on the exponential absolute-mixture obstruction

For the two-heavy majority family, I(OR2)=I(AND2)=1 and Var(OR2)=Var(AND2)=3/4. Parseval for balanced g gives

    B_quad=1/2, Z=3/8, M=1/4

for every r, while B_abs grows exponentially relative to the root deficit. Q gives mass P_g(T)/3 to each of the three nonempty A labels. R gives all mass to U=A, so KL(R||Q)=ln3. The complete reference cross term and actual entropy are

    C_ref=[E(g)+ln8]/4,
    E_mix=C_ref+(ln(3/2))/4-(ln3)/4=[E(g)+ln4]/4.

These are analytic all-r identities, checked from actual full truth tables for r=1,2. They show that the quadratic energy and normalization genuinely avoid the excluded absolute bill. They do not make the arbitrary coefficient-process logarithm disappear.

First unsupported line: C_ref<=C B_quad, or C_ref<=C Delta(F), for one absolute C at every actual two-weight gate and every product bias. No such bound is proved here. Keeping KL(R||Q) may allow a weaker signed inequality even when a separate cross-entropy bound fails. An inequality C_ref+M ln(Z/M)-M KL<=CDelta would suffice; simply restating it is not progress. The energy owner and mass-normalizer payment are now proved, and the remaining logarithmic obligation is explicit.

Deterministic coordinates contract first; then every physical edge and tail is on the inherited remaining product cube. Repeated gate wires do not create disjoint physical coordinates: substitute repetitions into the actual truth table before transforming. Physical restrictions that define h_T are Boolean slices, while their coefficient processes, Q and R are spectral constructions. No physical-tree history, empty coefficient, singleton, child or terminal entropy obligation is removed by the mixed-sector identity.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Full independent reconstruction of A044's exact local claim

The2^k signed Boolean terms in twice Z_S have even sum for k>=1, so Z_S is integer. The normalized mixed physical derivative D_S f is the alternating sum divided by2^k, hence Z_S=2^(k-1)D_S f. Integrating centered product coordinates gives m_S=2^(k-1)c_S. Expanding the complementary physical profile in its original orthonormal basis gives coefficient 2^(k-1)fhat(S union U)/sqrt(v_S) at complementary U. Parseval therefore proves

    V_S=4^(k-1)/v_S * sum_{T strictly containing S}P(T).

For any integer variable Z, Z^2>=|Z| pointwise. If t=|EZ|<=1, Var Z>=t-t^2. Since ln(1/t)<=1/t-1, h(EZ)<=2t(1-t)<=2Var Z. For t>=1, h(EZ)<=0. These steps require no affine profile, fixed remaining dimension or small amplitude cap. Splitting c=m/2^(k-1) gives the exact identity

    E_S=(k-1)ln4 P(S)+(v_S/4^(k-1))h(m_S),

and then the source inequality E_S<=(k-1)ln4P(S)+2sum_{T strictly containing S}P(T).

For Fourier degree<=3 let B=sum_degree2 P and T=sum_degree3 P. Singleton charges are <=4B+6T, pair charges <=ln4 B+6T, triple charges <=ln16 T. Both copies of6T are real debits. With Delta=B+2T, their sum is <=(6+ln4)Delta. This is Hplus<=(2+6/ln2)Delta in bits, not a statement about all degrees. No special prime truth table is needed for this corollary.

The genuine disjoint composition consequence is also checked at the stated scope. For child signs f_i on disjoint physical supports, contract constant children, set their induced outer variances sigma_i=Var(f_i), and w_i=I_i(g)/sigma_i>=0. Tensoring centered nonempty child expansions yields

    E(g(f_i))=E_induced(g)+sum_i w_i E(f_i),
    Delta(g(f_i))=Delta_induced(g)+sum_i w_i Delta(f_i).

The same constant therefore propagates at every actual induced bias. For uniform physical inputs the missing empty entropy is at most(log2 e)Var, so the bit constant2+6/ln2 also bounds ordinary H/I. Reused physical variables do not satisfy the tensor identity; zero-variance children contract before divisors.

Summing the local strict-superset charges counts a degree-d packet2(2^d-2) times over all nonempty proper submasks, and2binom(d,2) over pairs when d>=3. Degree2 packets contribute zero strict pair funding. Source PROOF.md equation(4) explicitly contains this d>=3 qualification; it governs the shorthand pair-multiplicity clause accepted here. Fair parity of degree d>=3 has entropy0, Delta=d-1, and raw pair bill d(d-1), proving the source's price d obstruction. These are actual same-function Fourier packets. No unsupported line is found within the source local/bounded-degree/composition/congestion statement at that declared strict-superset scope. Independent acceptance does not accept its remaining universal allocation conjecture.

### 2. The optimal mean-only zero-mean refund is physically realizable

For0<t<1 the integer second-moment bound gives Var Z>=t(1-t), and exactly

    h(m)=kappa(t)t(1-t)<=kappa(t)Var Z,
    kappa(t)=2t ln(1/t)/(1-t), t=|m|.

This is sharper than the constant2, tends to0 with t, and gives zero charge at a zero mean. It is the smallest nonnegative variance multiplier depending only on the mean that works for every actual Boolean pair cofactor under every product law: take f=2*1{X1=X2=C=1}-1, choose S={1,2} at any interior pair biases and give C probability t. Its physical pair Z_S is exactly1{C=1}, so mean t and variance t(1-t), attaining equality. Negating the sign covers negative means. Thus an arbitrary mean-only local coefficient lambda(m) valid for all such physical profiles must satisfy lambda(m)>=kappa(|m|) for0<|m|<1. Six rational actual AND3 admission certificates are saved; the proof holds for every real interior t, without a grid argument.

At pairs, multiplying this scalar inequality by v_S/4 gives a valid local refunded strict-superset payment. The question is its actual ownership multiplicity when all pairs are summed. Pointwise local sharpness does not prove that multiplicity is bounded.

### 3. Explicit fair Boolean family defeats even that sharp refund

For every integer r>=1 put n=16r^2, m=n/2, and take n fair data bits and one fresh fair selector C. Let N count the data ones, define

    g=+1 if N<=m-r or N>=m+r, and -1 otherwise,
    chi=(-1)^N,
    F=chi when C=0, and g when C=1.

These formulas specify an actual finite Boolean truth table for every r. No spectral law is presumed realizable. All data coordinates are essential through the parity branch, and the selector is essential because its two branches differ. Count symmetry preserves the exact complementary cofactor multiplicities; no physical independence of Fourier labels is introduced.

Let b=Pr[Bin(n-1,1/2)=m-r], and let a denote g's common fair degree-two coefficient. Differentiating g in one bit gives -1 at other-count m-r and +1 at other-count m+r-1, zero elsewhere. The two boundary probabilities are b by symmetry. The conditional mean of another sign coordinate at other-count K is [2K-(n-1)]/(n-1). Consequently

    a=2(2r-1)b/(n-1)>0.

For F every physical DATA pair has Fourier coefficient a/2, hence its integer profile mean m_S=2(a/2)=a. Pairs containing C have zero mean, because g and parity have no singleton coefficients. Those selector pairs are completely refunded and play no role in the obstruction.

The two full-data Fourier packets, without and with C, have masses (1+ghat([n]))^2/4 and (1-ghat([n]))^2/4. Their combined mass is(1+ghat([n])^2)/2>=1/2. Both strictly contain every data pair since n>=16. Each of binom(n,2) nonzero pair means therefore draws on that SAME combined packet mass. Hence

    L_kappa(F)>=binom(n,2)kappa(a)/2.

This is a packet-ownership obstruction that remains after all zero means are refunded; it does not rely on a fictitious product reference or duplicated physical coordinates.

### 4. Rigorous all-r lower bound; no asymptotic or floating premise

Let q=Pr[Bin(n-1,1/2)=m-1], a maximal central probability. That binomial has variance(n-1)/4<4r^2. Chebyshev gives probability at least3/4 within distance4r of its mean. At most8r+1 integer values lie there, so

    q>=3/[4(8r+1)]>=1/(12r).

The exact central-to-boundary ratio is

    b/q=product_{j=1}^{r-1}(m-j)/(m+j).

With m=8r^2, each factor is at least1-1/(4r). Bernoulli's inequality makes the product at least1-(r-1)/(4r)>=3/4. Thus b>=1/(16r), and

    a>=1/[8(n-1)].

For the upper bound use permutation symmetry: writing L=sum_i(2X_i-1), the sum of all pair characters is(L^2-n)/2. Thus a=E[g(L)(L^2-n)]/[n(n-1)]. Since E|L^2-n|<=E L^2+n=2n,

    a<=2/(n-1)<1.

It follows that kappa(a)>=2a ln(1/a)>=ln((n-1)/2)/[4(n-1)]. Therefore

    L_kappa(F)>=n ln((n-1)/2)/16.

Physical data influence averages that of parity and g, so I(F)=(n+I(g))/2+Inf_C(F). Since I(g)<=n and Inf_C<=1, I(F)<=n+1. Thus Delta(F)<=n+1<=3n/2 for n>=2, and rigorously

    L_kappa(F)/Delta(F)>=ln((n-1)/2)/24 -> infinity.

The exact finite certificates additionally verify I(F)<=n+1/2 on this declared family; that sharper fact is not used in the infinite proof. The deliberately weaker analytic n+1 bound avoids introducing an unproved universal selector improvement.

### 5. Complete physical certificates and scope of the negative result

With Eg and top=E[g(-1)^N], the exact physical identities used in the certificates are

    I(g)=2nb,
    I(F)=(n+2nb)/2+(1-top)/2,
    Var(F)=1-(Eg/2)^2.

For a fixed data pair and complementary count k, Z_g=[g(k+2)-2g(k+1)+g(k)]/2, while Z_chi=2(-1)^k. Their selector mixture has mean a and second moment2+(1/2)E Z_g^2. Therefore its actual strict-superset mass is[2+(1/2)E Z_g^2-a^2]/4, agreeing with the product-Fourier Parseval scaling. This computes the ENTIRE refunded pair bill exactly as a rational factor times the rigorously enclosed logarithm in kappa(a), not just the two-packet lower bound.

The family's actual ordinary entropy satisfies H(F)<=n+1 bits, while I(F)>=n/2. Thus H(F)/I(F)<=2+2/n. It is a rigorously paid Boolean control family, not an FEI disproof.

Since every universally valid nonnegative mean-only profile multiplier must be at least kappa, the divergence also excludes any such scalar multiplier as a universal all-pairs packet price. It does NOT exclude using exact h(m), the full profile law, negative charges, selected pairs, correlated references or a single shared packet owner. Summing exact scalar residuals and proving a selected whole-set/terminal logarithmic charge remain different, open tasks. A044's theorem, the prior quadratic-tail energy theorem and the newly paid prime56067 upper theorem are not refuted.

Include the empty coefficient. Under S~mu define M_A=H(S_A|S_B). Draw physical Z with its actual product probabilities, then draw T from the normalized squared biased spectrum of the Boolean restriction f_Z. Define J_A=I(Z;T) and L_A=H(mu)-E_Z H(mu_Z).

The exact frozen potential is P_A=M_A+Delta_A, where Delta_A=sum_{i in A}[h2(p_i)+log2(max(p_i,1-p_i))]. The live assertion is 0<=J_A<=P_A, hence L_A<=2M_A+Delta_A. All entropies here are Shannon bits, not corrected biased FEI+ entropy. Zero masses contribute zero; deterministic input coordinates must be contracted before defining the basis. FROZEN_POTENTIAL.json contains the complete interface and source version.

The substantive endpoint target addressed here is a separate correction: prove necessity of a rare logarithm by an actual lower-bound family, rather than by the asymptotic of an available upper bound. We prove for g_p,q(x,y)=(-1)^(xy), A={X}, fixed 0<q<1/2, and p down to zero:

    J_X-M_X = 4q(1-2q) p log2(1/p) + O_q(p).

In particular q=1/4 forces leading coefficient 1/2. Consequently any universal scalar one-bit tax tau(p) in J_X<=M_X+tau(p), valid for all actual Boolean functions and all interior residual biases, must satisfy liminf_{p->0} tau(p)/(p log2(1/p)) >= 1/2. No tau(p)=O(p) can work. This is a helper obstruction on a product-biased two-bit family, not a uniform FEI counterfamily.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

1. Exact physical channel. Expanding f_Z in the A coordinates and using biased Parseval gives Pr(S_B=T)=sum_R fhat_p(R union T)^2=E_Z fhat_Z(T)^2. Each child is Boolean, so its squared spectrum has mass one. Thus the joint channel's marginal T is the parent B-marginal. The entropy chain rule yields L_A=M_A+J_A; mutual information gives J_A>=0. This argument is self-contained and handles zero fibers by omission.

2. Boolean source and spectrum. The AND sign has truth table (1,1,1,-1) and identity g=1-2XY. Independence, E phi_X=E phi_Y=0, E X phi_X=sqrt(p(1-p)), and E Y phi_Y=sqrt(q(1-q)) give

    fhat(empty)=1-2pq,
    fhat(X)=-2q sqrt(p(1-p)),
    fhat(Y)=-2p sqrt(q(1-q)),
    fhat(XY)=-2 sqrt(p(1-p)q(1-q)).

Their exact squared law, in that order, is

    a=(1-2pq)^2,
    b=4p(1-p)q^2,
    c=4p^2q(1-q),
    d=4p(1-p)q(1-q).

It sums to one and is an actual Boolean spectrum. Put u=4q(1-q), r=pu. Then Pr(S_Y=1)=c+d=r. The X=0 child is constant. The X=1 child is 1-2Y, with squared masses (1-u,u). Therefore

    M_X=H(a,b,c,d)-h2(pu),
    J_X=h2(pu)-p h2(u),
    E(p,q):=J_X-M_X=2h2(pu)-H(a,b,c,d)-p h2(u).

3. Controlled asymptotic. For a fixed alpha>0 and t(p)=alpha p+O(p^2),

    -t log2(t)=alpha p log2(1/p)+O_alpha(p).

This follows by t/p=alpha+O(p), whose logarithm is bounded, and p^2 log(1/p)=O(p). Also -(1-v)log2(1-v)=O(v) for v=O(p), and a mass O(p^2) contributes O(p^2 log(1/p))=O(p). These estimates follow from the mean value theorem on compact positive intervals and the boundedness of p log(1/p); no finite calculation is used to infer the limit.

For fixed q in (0,1/2), b=4q^2p+O_q(p^2), d=4q(1-q)p+O_q(p^2), c=O_q(p^2), and a=1-O_q(p). Hence

    H(a,b,c,d)=4q p log2(1/p)+O_q(p),
    h2(pu)=u p log2(1/p)+O_q(p).

Substitution proves

    E(p,q)=(2u-4q)p log2(1/p)+O_q(p)
          =4q(1-2q)p log2(1/p)+O_q(p).

At q=1/4, this coefficient is 1/2. Indeed 4q(1-2q)=1/2-8(q-1/4)^2, so 1/2 is the largest leading coefficient forced by this fixed-q AND subfamily. It is not proved to be the largest coefficient over all Boolean gates.

4. Quantifiers and correction. If a scalar tau(p) satisfies the universal one-bit inequality, the actual q=1/4 example gives tau(p)>=E(p,1/4). Divide by p log2(1/p)>0 and pass to liminf. It is at least 1/2. In particular, for every finite K there exists epsilon_K>0 such that E(p,1/4)>Kp for all 0<p<epsilon_K. Thus the universal O(p) tax is impossible. This supplies the missing lower-bound family in B09 section 5(4); that sentence's original reasoning remains invalid, while its endpoint conclusion now has an independent elementary proof.

For 0<p<=1/2 the existing tax simplifies exactly to

    Delta(p)=p log2((1-p)/p),
    Delta(p)/(p log2(1/p)) -> 1.

The actual excess/Delta ratio tends to 1/2. Therefore a replacement tau(p)=c p log2(1/p)+O(p) requires c>=1/2. The current coefficient 1 is not refuted. Sharp universal leading coefficient in [1/2,1] is unpaid, conditional on acceptance of the B09 core upper bound.

5. Aligned disjoint tensor scope guard. Products of Boolean signs on disjoint input blocks have product Fourier laws; their physical-child channels also factor for aligned reveals. Conditional entropy and mutual information add, so M,J and actual excess add. The fixed p=1/8,q=1/4 witness repeated m times forces block tax greater than m*(1195/10000) bits. A tax bounded independently of block size, or a fixed multiple of max_i Delta(p_i), cannot replace the sum universally. This is an elementary consequence of product distributions; no broader composition or shared-variable additivity is asserted.

First unsupported line for the main FEI route: a finite dimension-independent influence payment of the complete actual restriction/composition cost, including terminal entropy and nonreused taxes. Neither the present endpoint obstruction nor B09's candidate uncertainty bound proves that payment.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A046 stage 2 follows the B10 lead's instruction to retire intake and independently reconstruct the one-bit reduction in B10L-ONEBIT-HALF-TAX@1. The original stage-1 endpoint is not recomputed. Full uniform-cube FEI remains OPEN.

For every finite residual product cube, common real orthonormal Fourier basis, real children G,H of unit L2 norm, and independent physical selector X with Pr(X=1)=p in (0,1), let g_T,h_T be their coefficients. Boolean children are included. Define q_T=(1-p)g_T^2+p h_T^2, posterior u_T=p h_T^2/q_T, selector-present spectral mass b_T=p(1-p)(h_T-g_T)^2, and v_T=b_T/q_T, omitting q_T=0. Let M=H(S_X|S_residual), J=I(X;T) for the actual child-spectral experiment. Entropies are Shannon bits and 0 log 0=0.

The reconstructed exact identity is

    J-M = h2(p)-sum_T q_T [h2(u_T)+h2(v_T)].

Writing (sqrt(1-p)g_T,sqrt(p)h_T)=sqrt(q_T)(cos t_T,sin t_T) and beta=arcsin sqrt(p) gives u_T=sin^2 t_T and v_T=sin^2(t_T-beta). Therefore J-M<=tau(p), where tau(p)=h2(p)-min_t F_p(t), F_p(t)=h2(sin^2 t)+h2(sin^2(t-beta)). If beta uses r=min(p,1-p), swap child labels first when p>1/2. This correction is necessary in the literal all-p claim.

We also repair the source's rounded asymptotic, proving independently

    tau(p)=(p/2)log2(1/p)+(log2(e)/2-1)p+O(p^2 log(1/p))

as p decreases to zero. The source's exact coefficient -0.2787 in an o(p) expression must be replaced by log2(e)/2-1. The finite-p inequality tau<=Delta/2, its full scalar certificate, multi-bit additivity, and global influence payment are separate review obligations.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

1. Independent reconstruction without angles. Write F(X,y)=G(y)+X(H(y)-G(y)). Since phi_X=(X-p)/sqrt(p(1-p)), the two parent coefficients over residual label T are

    alpha_T=(1-p)g_T+p h_T,
    gamma_T=sqrt(p(1-p))(h_T-g_T).

Expanding the squares cancels the cross terms and gives alpha_T^2+gamma_T^2=q_T. Parseval on each child gives sum_T q_T=1. The joint physical-child/spectral probabilities are (1-p)g_T^2 and p h_T^2. Each child law is normalized by hypothesis, so the selector marginal is exactly Bernoulli(p), the spectral marginal is q, and the selector posterior given T is u_T. Thus J=h2(p)-sum q_T h2(u_T). The parent spectral label posterior is v_T, hence M=sum q_T h2(v_T). Subtraction proves the identity. Zero q implies g_T=h_T=0 and is omitted. No Booleanity or entropic uncertainty theorem enters this reconstruction beyond child normalization.

2. Angles and orientation. For q_T>0, the normalized two-vector is a real point on the unit circle. Taking beta in (0,pi/2), sin beta=sqrt(p), cos beta=sqrt(1-p), yields

    alpha_T/sqrt(q_T)=cos(t_T-beta),
    gamma_T/sqrt(q_T)=sin(t_T-beta).

The source's expression with sin^2 beta=min(p,1-p) is valid on p<=1/2 as written. On p>1/2 relabel X'=1-X, p'=1-p, G'=H,H'=G. Parent absent mass is unchanged and the present coefficient changes sign, so both spectral masses and M are unchanged. The channel merely relabels its physical input, leaving J unchanged. The posterior u changes to 1-u and binary entropy is invariant. Only after this relabeling may beta=arcsin sqrt(min(p,1-p)) be used in the stated vector. Alternatively keep beta=arcsin sqrt(p) for all p.

The literal unswapped claim is false: choose G=H=1, p=3/4. Actual M=J=0. Its claimed smaller beta gives sin(t-beta)=2p-1=1/2, so its right-hand side becomes -h2(1/4)<-1/2 bits. This is a Boolean source satisfying the source's hypotheses. It refutes that literal identity, not the scalar half-tax upper conclusion. The identity's derivation in the source REPORT already uses the correct beta; the repair belongs in its all-p statement.

3. Necessary moment and scope. Physical-child normalization also gives

    sum_T q_T u_T=p,  sum_T q_T(1-u_T)=1-p.

Therefore an arbitrary choice of angles and weights satisfying only sum q=1 is not necessarily realizable by two normalized children. The pointwise minimum of F nevertheless gives a valid universal upper bound, because every summand is at least min F. Attainment over normalized real children additionally requires the fixed moment, and attainment over Boolean children additionally requires Boolean inverse transforms. We do not assert that either condition blocks or proves attainment. A047 is already assigned that optimization; it must not be confused with the accepted identity.

4. Independent scalar endpoint repair. Let 0<p<=1/16 and beta=arcsin sqrt(p). F has period pi/2 and symmetry t->beta-t. These reduce every angle to t=beta/2+d with 0<=d<=pi/4. Let a=|sin t|, c=|sin(t-beta)|. The sine triangle identity gives a+c>=sin beta=sqrt(p): on 0<=t<=beta use sin t+sin(beta-t)>=sin beta; on t>=beta the sum is increasing up to the domain endpoint and has value sin beta at t=beta. Also a^2<= (1+sqrt(p))/2<=5/8 and c^2<=1/2.

Put s0=e^(-3/2). If max(a,c)>=s0, at least one squared amplitude belongs to [e^-3,5/8]. Binary entropy has a strictly positive minimum C0 on this fixed compact interval. The trial t=beta/2 has F=2h2((1-sqrt(1-p))/2), tending to zero. Consequently, for all sufficiently small p, a minimizing angle has a,c<s0. This is an existence argument for a neighborhood, not an assertion of an uncomputed explicit cutoff.

On [0,s0], k(s)=s^2 log2(1/s^2), extended by k(0)=0, is increasing and convex: its second derivative is (-4 ln s-6)/ln2>=0 and its first derivative is nonnegative. Jensen and a+c>=sqrt(p) give

    k(a)+k(c)>=2k((a+c)/2)>=2k(sqrt(p)/2)
             =(p/2)log2(4/p).

For 0<=x<1, -ln(1-x)>=x, hence h2(x)>=x log2(1/x)+x(1-x)/ln2. Put S=a^2+c^2. Then S>=p/2 and S<=2e^-3<1/2; a^4+c^4<=S^2 and S-S^2 is increasing up to 1/2. Therefore

    F(t)>= (p/2)log2(4/p)+(p/2-p^2/4)/ln2.

The symmetric trial has x=(1-sqrt(1-p))/2=p/4+O(p^2). Binary entropy expansion, with x of order p, gives

    2h2(x)=(p/2)log2(1/p)+p+(p/2)log2(e)
            +O(p^2 log(1/p)).

The lower bound and trial upper bound squeeze min F to this same expansion. Since h2(p)=p log2(1/p)+p log2(e)+O(p^2), subtracting proves the exact tax expansion stated above. This proves the intended linear coefficient without assuming an exactly symmetric minimizer. It also shows why substituting a rounded -0.2787 as an exact coefficient cannot be accompanied by an o(p) remainder.

5. Analytic half-tax boundary review. LEMMA_REVIEW.md gives the independent all-domain proofs: for p<=2/25 the large-amplitude branch has F>7/25>131/500>R(2/25)>=R(p), with the compact endpoints certified by rational entropy and exp(3) bounds; the small-amplitude branch has F-R>(27/100)p-(41/100)p^2>0. For p in [489/1000,1/2] and |theta-beta|<=301/1000, phi''=-4/ln2+2cos(2theta)log2(cot^2 theta)>=-4/ln2, because the final factors have the same sign. Integrating gives the source semiconcavity bound. A rational sine lower bound gives h2(sin^2 e)>=(97/100)e^2 log2[1/((97/100)e^2)]; the net quadratic bracket is >1/2 over this widened region. When e<0 the negative linear term is bounded by (13/200)*(301/1000-489/2000), smaller than (1/2)*(489/2000)^2; e>=0 is immediate. This discharges the source's lemma-B boxes with exact threshold slack. Full derivations and fourteen rational constant checks are in the accompanying file/output. These analytic regions are checked proofs; intermediate box signs are source-code replay, not a new independent rational certificate.

6. Universal bridge limitation. In actual physical restriction of a uniform cube, every unrevealed original coordinate remains fair conditional on previous coordinate values. Consequently p=1/2 at every such node. A rare biased selector arising from an effective event or a gate output is a different ledger and needs an explicit physical-budget conversion. The exact channel identity is valid for both, but a p log(1/p) tax cannot simply be inserted into a uniform physical-coordinate telescope as though the original coordinate had bias p. Existing A048 common-factor guards likewise leave arbitrary expected leaf entropy untouched.

First unsupported lines are separated: acceptance of tau(p)<=Delta(p)/2 over all p needs an independent reconstruction of the source scalar analytic/certificate chain; extension to a general block needs its additivity argument; a full-FEI consequence needs an actual hereditary nonreusing physical influence payment of conditional erasure plus terminal entropy. None follows from the exact one-bit identity. The corrected scalar endpoint expansion is proved here; the entire original combined claim is not independently accepted.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Independent reconstruction of the scalar prerequisite.** Put r=min(p,1-p). In the two real bases separated by beta, let u,w be the smaller probabilities. The two selected larger-probability basis vectors have overlap at most sqrt(1-r). Their projector sum has maximal eigenvalue 1+overlap, hence u+w>=delta=1-sqrt(1-r). Here delta<1/2, u,w<=1/2. If either probability exceeds delta, monotonicity gives h(u)+h(w)>=h(delta). Otherwise concavity on the segment u+w=delta gives the same inequality. Since delta>=r/2, h(delta)>=h(r/2)>=h(r)/2. Thus 0<=tau*<=h(p)/2, with no finite-p numerical minimizer or multi-bit uncertainty premise.

The symmetric trial t=beta/2 gives tau*>=h(p)-2h((1-sqrt(1-p))/2). Since the latter argument is p/4+O(p^2), tau*/h(p)->1/2 as p->0. At p=1/2, tau*=0: the positive-coefficient power series for h((1+z)/2) gives h(x)>=4x(1-x), and the two rotated bounds sum to one, attained at a basis vector.

**Reserve and root payment.** Let ell_v=h(p_i)/2-tau*(p_i)>=0. Independence of unrevealed inputs gives j_v=h(p_i)-c_v and exactly tau*-c_v/2=j_v/2-ell_v. The positive part is at most j_v/2. The physical conditional-output reserve has drift R_v-E R_child=j_v/2. Finite tree summation releases exactly I(Pi;Y)/2 and leaves E H(Y|Pi)/2 at stopping leaves; it never releases that terminal account twice. The transcript chain rule gives H(Pi|Y)=sum w_v c_v, proving the refund statement before any influence comparison.

For a fixed full physical order, H(Y)=sum_i I(Y;X_i|X_<i). Adding X_>i to the first argument and using product independence bounds each term by I(Y;X_i|X_-i)=H(Y|X_-i)=h(p_i)Inf_i^phys. Therefore H(Y)<=sum h(p_i)Inf_i^phys<=I_phys. This proves the root payment for adaptive or partially stopped trees too; H(Y|Pi) remains visible.

**Optimality, with exact quantifiers.** For lambda<1/2, the rare-endpoint limit chooses a fixed q with tau*(q)>lambda h(q). Take f_r=1-2 product_i X_i under q-biased inputs, with its essential short-circuit tree. Then A_T=tau*(q)(1-q^r)/(1-q), H(Pi|f_r)=h(q)(1-q^r)/(1-q)-h(q^r), and I_phys=r q^(r-1). Thus A_T-lambda H(Pi|f_r) tends to a positive constant but influence tends to zero. No finite C works. The previously proved refund gives lambda=1/2,C=1/2. This is optimality in that declared scalar bank family; it does not compare all structural repairs or supply a spectral bank. The underlying point-defect falsifier is credited to A048/A050, not a new FEI disproof.

**Terminal spectral ledger.** Direct actual squared-coefficient algebra gives the entropy decrement M_v+J_v. Set S_T=sum_v w_v[h(p_i)/2-(J_v-M_v)], M_T=sum w_v M_v. It is nonnegative by the scalar prerequisite and the one-bit angular reduction. The physical transcript chain rule and finite entropy telescope give exactly

    Hhat(f)=E Hhat(f_leaf)+2M_T+H(Pi|Y)/2+I(Pi;Y)/2-S_T.

Only the output-information term has been paid. The rest includes signed spectral erasure/uncertainty and all terminal Fourier entropy. At fair physical coordinates tau*=0, sum w h(p_i)/2=H(Pi)/2, so the output/transcript substitution cancels that same account exactly and leaves the original fair spectral ledger. A rare logical event does not turn a fair physical bit into a biased physical query.

**Independent compatibility test.** For fair full computing orders form the joint root Fourier set/transcript law directly, not by assuming an information-chain identity: for retained label T choose branch b with probability childhat_b(T)^2/[2q_T]. Multiplication of these kernels along each path gives the exact rational joint. Its physical transcript marginal is uniform and its child spectral law is correct. The kernel depends only on current retained root labels; conditional on them and history it is independent of earlier discarded labels. Hence I(S0;Pi)=sum w_v J_v. Because Y is determined by a full computing transcript,

    H(Pi|Y)-H(Pi|S0)=I(S0;Pi)-H(Y).

For f=(-1)^X this difference is -1. For f=(-1)^(X1 X2) it is -1+(3/4)log2(3), rigorously in (0.188721875540867,0.188721875540868). Both directions of universal conditional-entropy domination fail already on actual fair Boolean functions. These are reconstructions of A050's existing controls, not fresh obstruction claims. An essential short-circuit tree or the full tree gives the same difference in the second witness; the added dummy outcome contributes equally to both conditional entropies.

**Honest first unsupported line:** a normalized common-space spectral reference supplies H(Pi|Y)/2 once, including the signed side-information correction, and pays 2M_T-S_T plus terminal Fourier entropy from one original physical influence account. The present review does not establish it. The accepted local reserve alone cannot make this inference.

**Obsolete repairs rejected before computation.** Unchanged tax-free J<=M, any universal O(p) tax, raw restriction loss and fixed Renyi order below one are retired as in the prior A046 ledger. Essential contraction removes dummy tax but cannot repair the A048 essential point-defect tax-payment failure. # complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete checked derivation is PROOF.md. Its critical universal steps are summarized here.

For arbitrary actual Boolean branch signs G,H with gamma=Pr(G!=H)>0, a fresh j-bit point switch F=G+A_j(H-G) has d=rho gamma, rho=2^{-(j-1)}. Parseval gives sum(Hhat-Ghat)^2=4gamma. The nonempty residual-selector Fourier rows contribute exactly 2(1-rho)d to erasure. In the empty residual row the derivative-sector mass totals rho d, and the combined mass is at most one. Applying the binary remainder inequality and log-sum yields

    B_j/d <= 2(1-rho)+rho log2(euler/(rho^2 gamma)).

This independently accepts the paired-row slice of A025 v1 unchanged, without accepting its entire bundled claim. No repair is supplied. If gamma>=2^-D, take h=ceil(log2(D+1)), m=h+2. At j>m, k=j-1>=h+2, so 2^-k D<=1/4 and 2k2^-k<=1. Since log2(euler)-2<0, B_j/d<=13/4<4. Thus only the final m live layers can be bad, even at arbitrarily small gamma.

For the specified three-event controller, q=2^-R,r=2^-T,eta=2^-(L+R+T),e=q+r-qr-eta. Since eta<=qr/2, e>=max(q,r); balance gives gamma=e/2>=2^{-(min(R,T)+1)}. This proves the stated m for all positive widths.

Let alpha=2^-s, JB=(L+R+T)eta, JC=R q(1-r)+T r(1-q), JD=JB+JC, pi=eta+alpha e. The disjoint directional cuts of WUV and U OR V give the exact accounts

    I_sep=s alpha e,
    I_other=(1-alpha)JB+alpha JC,
    I_terminal=pi I(g), I(F)=I_sep+I_other+I_terminal.

At live layer j the actual queried influence is 2^-j e and its face cost is c_j=2^-j[JD+(j-1)e+e I(g)]. Define M_j=min(j,m) and

    V_j=2^-j[M_j JD+e M_j(M_j-1)/2+M_j e I(g)], V_0=0.

On failure set the separator reserve to zero; the remaining point controller still has its separate influence account. The fair success child has reserve V_(j-1). Direct subtraction proves V_j-V_(j-1)/2=c_j for j<=m and zero for j>m. Every possible bad-layer price is at most this drift. The reserve can increase on a success branch, but its expected drift is exactly owned and is not restarted. Finite summation leaves zero terminal reserve and proves Q_bad,separator<=V_s.

With M=min(s,m), alpha JD<=I_other, alpha e I(g)<=I_terminal and M<=s imply

    V_s<=M I_other+(M-1)I_sep/2+M I_terminal<=M I(F).

The remaining controller descendants have constant-child or two-point-union splits covered by the reconstructed A023 good-node inequality, hence are strictly 4-good. With JS_i<=2d_i/ln2 and every core terminal retained,

    H(F)<=(4+2/ln2)(I_sep+I_other)+V_s+pi H(g).

This is parameter-dependent payment. No universal terminal certificate or width-independent root reserve follows.

The output/spectral conditioning correction is retained explicitly. For this declared coherent ROOT-spectral physical coupling, J_tree=sum w_v JS_v=I(S0;Pi). At the stopped partition, I(Y;Pi)=h2(pi/2)-pi. Thus

    C_T=H(Pi|Y)-H(Pi|S0)=J_tree-[h2(pi/2)-pi],
    C_T<=2(I_sep+I_other)/ln2-[h2(pi/2)-pi].

This reconstructs A092's existing conversion at its root-compatible scope. It is not another refund to add to the face ledger. # complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md contains the full all-dimensional derivation. Let q=2^-R,r=2^-T,eta=2^-(L+R+T), beta=q+r-qr,e=beta-eta. The underlying switch branches are G=1+WUV(g-1), H=1+(U OR V)(g-1). Balance gives EG=1-eta, EH=1-beta, Pr(G!=H)=e/2. At a live point query let rho=2^{-(j-1)} and d=rho e/2. Nonempty residual-selector frequencies contribute exactly 2(1-rho)d to erasure. For empty residual-selector frequencies the paired coefficients are u_T=Ghat(T)+rho Delta_T/2, v_T=-rho Delta_T/2, Delta=Hhat-Ghat.

Separating the FULL constant data row gives u_0=1-eta-d and v_0=d. On every nonconstant data row, including controller and arbitrary core frequencies, exact Boolean/Parseval identities yield

    sum Ghat^2=2eta-eta^2,
    sum Ghat Delta=-eta e,
    sum Delta^2=2e-e^2.

Hence the exceptional nonconstant total and derivative masses are exactly q_*=2eta-eta^2-rho eta e+rho^2(e-e^2/2) and b_*=rho^2 e(2-e)/4. In particular q_*<=2eta+rho^2 e. Binary entropy remainder and log-sum, with theta=eta/e, give

    B_j/d <=2+rho log2(euler/(2-e))
               +rho log2(1+2theta/rho^2)
               +d log2(euler/d^2).

Compatible nested events imply eta<=8e^2/25: writing t=sqrt(qr)<=1/2, e>=2t-(3/2)t^2>=(5/4)t and eta<=t^2/2. For e<=1/8, c=1/ln2<3/2 and euler<11/4 imply the three variable terms above are strictly below 2/3, 9/20 and 19/32 respectively. The middle bound follows from ln(1+x^2)<=x, giving rho log2(1+2theta/rho^2)<=c sqrt(2theta)<(6/5)sqrt(e)<9/20. The last bound uses d<=1/16 and monotonicity of d log2(euler/d^2). Thus B_j/d<1781/480<4 at EVERY layer, even as disagreement tends to zero. Since min(R,T)>=4 implies e<=q+r<=1/8, those cases need no separator reserve at all.

For the remaining widths min(R,T)<=3, the independently accepted v1 cutoff gives at most m=ceil(log2(min(R,T)+2))+2<=5 eligible layers. Put m_*=0 in the wide-block rare regime and m_*=m otherwise. With alpha=2^-s, JB=(L+R+T)eta, JC=R q(1-r)+T r(1-q), JD=JB+JC and pi=eta+alpha e, retain the exact accounts I_sep=s alpha e, I_other=(1-alpha)JB+alpha JC and I_terminal=pi I(g). The modified reserve

    W_j=2^-j[M_j JD+e M_j(M_j-1)/2+M_j e I(g)],
    M_j=min(j,m_*), W_0=0,

is set to zero on separator failure. Its physical drift W_j-W_(j-1)/2 is the actual face cost c_j=2^-j[JD+(j-1)e+e I(g)] when j<=m_* and zero otherwise. All potentially bad queries are covered by that drift. No budget is recreated; early successes may raise the reserve but have zero expected release. Finite summation and alpha JD<=I_other give

    Q_bad<=W_s<=5 I_other+2 I_sep+5 I_terminal<=5 I(F).

The point-conjunction/union descendants remain good by the accepted v1 scope, so this is the complete controller-prefix price. The full terminal-inclusive entropy ledger is H(F)<=(4+2/ln2)(I_sep+I_other)+W_s+pi H(g). If a core rate C0 is separately certified, the conclusion is H(F)<=max(9+2/ln2,C0+5)I(F). The new advance is K4 localized payment, not an improved class entropy constant over A021's already-paid K7 route.

For A040's explicit width-obstruction family w=2^h-2, all h>=3 have min(R,T)>=4 and hence actual prefix bill zero. Its ORIGINAL V envelope still has V/I~log2(w); the reviewed obstruction remains intact. W filters those already-good faces out instead of increasing their allowance. This is not an FEI counterexample or a reversal of Q_bad<=V.

The root-compatible signed output/spectral correction remains C_T=J_tree-[h2(pi/2)-pi] as in the prior stage. It is neither canceled nor paid by this new row bound, and prescribed witness conditioning and reference resets are additional obligations. First unsupported universal line: transport an actual-bad debit through a general shared-data or interleaved data/selector history using genuinely unused child surplus, including balance loss, all inherited terminals and the prescribed-reference correction. The key eta<=8e^2/25 relies on this nested compatible geometry; a large-contrast bent/point-flip branch pair need not satisfy it.

# A047 boundary search: sharp fixed-child endpoint and unequal-bias signs

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Freeze B09's actual one-selector ledger, M=H(S_X|S_Y), J=H(S_Y)-E H(child spectrum), and debt J-M. The tax-free J<=M helper is already refuted; the new target is the sharp possible coefficient in a repaired endpoint tax, not another refutation of that retired statement. For every fixed finite pair of Boolean children g,h in a common fixed product-biased orthonormal basis, select h with probability p and g otherwise, independently of the data. All entropies are in bits. Prove a universal bound on lim (J-M)/(p log2(1/p)), characterize sharpness, and certify boundary signs. Data dimension and data biases are fixed in taking this limit; no uniform remainder in those parameters is asserted.

Result: the exact coefficient is 2Z+2c-2 <= 1/2-2(c-1/2)^2 <= 1/2, where c=E(gh) and Z=sum_{G_T=0} H_T^2. The maximum is attained by genuine Boolean children, including balanced children and a dictator typical child. For disjoint child spectral supports M=J=h2(p) exactly for all p. The sharply unequal AND endpoint q->0 at fixed p has debt 4p(1-p)q log2((1-p)/p)+O_p(q^2 log(1/q)). Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2 was read in full. README, CURRENT_STATUS snapshot 2026-10-11T20:06:24Z, claim/obstruction/packet registries, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS, campaign plan and A047 contract were consulted. The board snapshot is 2026-10-11 21:55 UTC. B10/STREAM_NOTES.md was absent, so no lead direction was inferred. A046's board entry already identifies its AND lower-bound intake; this stage adds the arbitrary-child upper coefficient and zero-debt classification.

Original B09 REPORT.md sections 2, 4, 5, 8, run 20261011T195500Z-account01-B09-biased-restriction-uncertainty, source commit adb671ffcda143fa68347806bb96e8c305621a7d, supplies the frozen ledger. The repair report verification/B09_interval_repair_20261011/REPORT.md supplies the signed-division warning. Neither the B09 uncertainty theorem nor an upstream A046 claim is a proof dependency here.

The supplemental CAMPAIGN, CLAIMS, COUNTEREXAMPLES, SEED_LEMMAS and NATIVE_MAPPING were consulted; sources/ONE_BIT_ENDPOINT.md, BLOCK_RESTRICTION_IDENTITY_2026-09-18.md and GAMMA_ENDPOINT_LEDGER.md were read for distinction between exact identities and universal payment. The threshold 38pp and earlier 29pp text introductions/Theorem A, and the percolation 30pp and other 31pp introductions/Theorems 1.1-1.2 were read for scope and normalization. These papers are context only; their remaining proofs were not reconstructed and no theorem from them is assumed. The source-byte manifest records hashes, not claims of full proof audit. No web or priority search was needed for this self-contained calculation; no novelty claim is made.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Write G_T,H_T for normalized child Fourier coefficients. Parent squared masses on residual label T and selector label 0,1 are

    a_T=((1-p)G_T+pH_T)^2,
    b_T=p(1-p)(H_T-G_T)^2,
    q_T=a_T+b_T=(1-p)G_T^2+pH_T^2.

For finite lists, positive limiting masses have entropy change O(p); masses p*r+O(p^2) have entropy r*p*log2(1/p)+O(p); masses O(p^2) have entropy O(p^2 log(1/p)). Thus H(q) gains coefficient Z=sum_{G_T=0}H_T^2, while H(a,b) gains Z+D, where D=sum_{G_T!=0}(H_T-G_T)^2. Averaged child entropy has only a linear change. Therefore J has leading coefficient Z, M has D, and the debt has Z-D. By Parseval D+Z=2-2c, so debt coefficient=2Z+2c-2. Cauchy-Schwarz on g's support gives c^2<=1-Z; substitute and complete the square to obtain <=1/2-2(c-1/2)^2. Equality in the final bound requires c=1/2 and H restricted to g's spectral support equals G/2.

On two fair data bits take g=1 and h=1-2*1_{Y1=Y2=0}. Then c=1/2, Z=3/4, D=1/4: the coefficient is 1/2. Multiply both by a fresh fair character: this permutes residual Fourier labels, preserving M,J,c,Z and makes both children balanced. The typical child becomes a dictator. Hence any universal replacement tax with fixed-child endpoint coefficient strictly less than 1/2 is false, even when both children are balanced. This strengthens endpoint information but is not a family with unbounded H/I.

Disjoint supports give marginal law q made from two distinguishable pieces, with weights 1-p,p. Hence J=h2(p). In a G-only fiber b_T/q_T=p; in an H-only fiber b_T/q_T=1-p, hence M=h2(p) as well. A constant and a balanced child are an important instance. Equal children give M=J=0; opposite children give J=0,M=h2(4p(1-p)).

For biased AND, its four squared masses are ((1-2pq)^2,4p(1-p)q^2,4p^2q(1-q),4p(1-p)q(1-q)). At fixed q the rare-p coefficient is 4q(1-2q). At fixed p and q->0 put v=4pq(1-q). Grouping the Y-present parent masses gives H(parent)=h2(v)+v*h2(p)+O_p(q^2 log(1/q)); the remaining X-only mass is O_p(q^2). Therefore J-M=2h2(v)-H(parent)-p*h2(4q(1-q))=4pq[log2(1/p)-h2(p)]+O_p(q^2 log(1/q)). Since the bracket equals (1-p)log2((1-p)/p), the stated expansion follows. It is positive to leading order for p<1/2 and negative for p>1/2.

The first unsupported line is a dimension-independent finite-p improvement, for example J-M <= (1/2)[h2(p)+log2 max(p,1-p)]. The endpoint theorem does not prove that inequality; the O_{g,h}(p) constants depend on the fixed pair and basis. It also does not control simultaneous p,q endpoints or pay a composition/stopping tree. Full details are in BOUNDARY_ASYMPTOTICS.tex.

# A047 stage 2: exact real supremum and certified Boolean separation

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Follow B10 lead v2's A047 direction. For an independent selector X of bias p and two real L2-unit children G,H in a common orthonormal product basis, let M=H(S_X|S_Y), J=H(S_Y)-(1-p)H(G^2)-pH(H^2). Entropy is in bits. Compute the sharp real supremum of J-M at every fixed interior p, and compare it to the Boolean supremum over all ordered children on at most three UNIFORM residual data bits at p=1/8,1/4,3/8. Arbitrarily biased residual measures are not included in this finite census.

The real supremum equals tau*(p)=h2(p)-min_t[h2(sin^2 t)+h2(sin^2(t-b))], sin^2 b=p. The lower convex envelope at abscissa p equals that minimum exactly. Two residual Fourier labels suffice for attainment, hence one uniform residual bit suffices for REAL children. The finite Boolean suprema are exactly the witness prime-log expressions in out/CENSUS.json. Strict gaps to certified real witnesses are established at all three requested biases.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Full proof is in DERIVATION.md. For q_T=(1-p)G_T^2+pH_T^2, set (sqrt(1-p)G_T,sqrt(p)H_T)=sqrt(q_T)(cos t_T,sin t_T). Normalization gives sum q_T=1 and sum q_T sin^2 t_T=p. Exact parent masses yield

    J-M=h2(p)-sum_T q_T F_p(t_T),
    F_p(t)=h2(sin^2 t)+h2(sin^2(t-b)).

Conversely every finite angle mixture satisfying these two constraints defines unit real children. Thus the sharp real supremum is h2(p) minus the lower convex envelope of (sin^2 t,F_p(t)) at p. Let m=min F_p and t0 minimize it. F_p(0)=h2(p), while F_p(t0)>=h2(x), x=sin^2 t0. For 0<p<1/2 this excludes p<x<1-p. Complement t0 by pi/2 if necessary to arrange x<=p. Its orthogonal companion has abscissa 1-x and the same F_p=m. Weights (1-x-p)/(1-2x) and (p-x)/(1-2x) are nonnegative, average the abscissae to p, and average F_p to m. This proves exact attainment with two labels. Child exchange covers p>1/2; at p=1/2 a complementary equal-weight pair, or a single minimizer with abscissa 1/2, works. Explicit normalized coefficient vectors are given in DERIVATION.md. Their inverse transforms are real and need not be Boolean.

Real lower witnesses use rational x=sin^2 t<=p and the same complementary mixture. Their second measurement probability is y=p+(1-2p)x-2sqrt(p(1-p)x(1-x)), so the attained debt is h2(p)-h2(x)-h2(y). Rational square-root enclosures and monotonicity of h2 on [0,1/2] certify these witnesses without a numerical optimizer or an unproved minimizer formula.

For Boolean children use all sign tables and integer Walsh coefficients. Each pair's exact debt is a sum over fibers of 2eta(q)-eta(a)-eta(b)-(1-p)eta(G^2)-p eta(H^2), with eta(s)=-s log2 s and exact parent masses a=((1-p)G+pH)^2,b=p(1-p)(H-G)^2,q=a+b. Rational log intervals certify every pair, then maximum lower/upper endpoints enclose the finite supremum. Every interval-overlapping candidate has the same exact rational prime-log vector as the witness; all excluded candidates have certified smaller debt. Thus the reported candidate counts are exact maximizing-pair counts, not floating ties. This settles the finite domain without extrapolating to more bits.

First unsupported line: a dimension-independent Boolean improvement below tau*(p), or any deduction that these small uniform-data gaps persist for arbitrarily many residual bits or moving product biases. Neither the exact real supremum nor the finite Boolean census pays the C019 expected path tax, conditional M term or terminal entropy. The trigonometric minimum's full numerical value is not computed by this stage; certified witness lower bounds suffice for the strict comparisons.

# A047 stage 3: paid side-conditioning conversion and its rare-output boundary

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Consume the new A050 half-output-conditional transcript repair rather than repeat the completed real-supremum census or A048's retired blanket-tax payment. Let f be an actual Boolean sign under any finite independent interior product law and let Pi be a finite deterministic physical singleton stopping transcript with no coordinate rerevealed on a path. Define R=I_phys(f)-E I_phys(f_leaf), the physically removed unweighted pivotal influence. All entropy is in bits; product-biased spectral energy is not substituted for I_phys.

Place root spectrum S0 and Pi on the function-specific coherent lift and append any finite witness/side information Z=(W,A) by a single root conditional law P(Z|S0), followed by the same lift. Let o=I(Y;Pi), Y=f(X), j_Z=I(Z;Pi), and Gamma_Z=H(Pi|Y)-H(Pi|Z). This stage proves

    Gamma_Z=j_Z-o,
    o/2+[Gamma_Z]_+/2=max(o,j_Z)/2 <= (2/ln2)R,

with the sharper constant 1/ln2 for uniform physical inputs. Thus output release and positive conversion cost share ONE removed-influence account. The accepted A050 bank estimate becomes

    A_tax <= H(Pi|Z)/2+(2/ln2)R,

or the uniform sharpening. This converts the conditioning of a declared entropy bank; it does not supply that bank from an actual reference. Uniform scalar tax is already zero, so no new uniform FEI constant follows from this tax corollary alone.

The rare-output boundary rules out the stronger conversion-only allowance Gamma_+/2<=I/2, already for a fair five-bit point defect. Allowing future/history-derived side information without the root Markov condition rules out EVERY finite influence constant even on partial essential-query trees.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md contains the complete proof. For a queried coordinate i of bias p, the actual residual spectral marginal is q_T=(1-p)G_T^2+pH_T^2, equivalently the sum of its two parent orthonormal spectral masses. On a positive fiber choose physical branch b with probability P(X_i=b)*childhat_b(T)^2/q_T, retaining T. Biased Parseval gives the actual branch law and actual child spectra. The kernel depends on S0 only through the retained label and history. Consequently J_T=sum_v w_v J_v=I(S0;Pi). The arbitrary root append imposes Z -> S0 -> Pi, so j_Z<=J_T. Partial leaves retain their actual output and Fourier entropy; attaching Y with the physical conditional output law gives the correct joint (Pi,Y).

At each actual history the remaining physical inputs stay independent. Adding unqueried data to I(X_i;Y|history) bounds it by H(Y|other inputs,history)=h2(p_i)Inf_i^phys(f_v). Summation gives o<=sum w h2(p_i)Inf_i^phys<=R. This improves the root-I account to REMOVED influence, retaining the output entropy left at the stopping frontier.

A simple product-law channel estimate is self-contained: ln u<=u-1 bounds each fiber's natural-unit mixture information by p(1-p)(G^2-H^2)^2/q. Weighted Cauchy-Schwarz gives (|G|+|H|)^2<=q/[p(1-p)], so this is at most (|G|-|H|)^2<=(G-H)^2. Sum and apply Parseval to obtain J_v<=4 Inf_i^phys/ln2 and J_T<=4R/ln2. Under uniform inputs the existing sharper two-point entropy bound gives J_v<=2 Inf_i/ln2; its elementary scalar derivative proof is reproduced in DERIVATION.md. No sharpness of the product-law constant is asserted.

The two conditional transcript entropies differ by j_Z-o exactly. Hence o/2+[Gamma_Z]_+/2=max(o,j_Z)/2, paid by the displayed channel/removed-influence bound. This identity is the nonreuse point: output information and conversion are not each assigned a second full influence budget. For the A050 inequality A_tax<=o/2+H(Pi|Y)/2, replace the two terms together to get H(Pi|Z)/2+j_Z/2. The latter is influence-paid; the conditional history term still requires funding.

For the exact terminal Fourier ledger let M_T=sum w M_v and Dbar_T=sum w[h2(p_i)/2-(J_v-M_v)]. For every coherently appended Z,

    Hhat(f)=E Hhat(f_leaf)+2M_T+H(Pi|Z)/2+j_Z/2-Dbar_T.

The replacement is exact regardless of whether its separate terms are cheaply funded. Since H(Pi|Z)+j_Z=H(Pi), cancellation reduces it to the original spectral telescope. In particular no hidden proof of erasure payment has been obtained. A reoptimized descendant witness or additional side conditioning that violates Z -> S0 -> Pi requires its own injection/reset correction.

For the fair r-bit point defect f_r=1-2*1_{all ones}, delta=2^-r and I=2r delta. In its essential short-circuit computing tree, put v_s=4*2^-s*(1-2^-s). Direct spectra give

    J_T=sum_{s=0}^{r-1}2^{-(r-s-1)}[h2(v_s/2)-h2(v_s)/2],
    Gamma_S0=J_T-h2(delta).

Cancellation in h2(v/2)-h2(v)/2 yields J_T=4r delta+O(delta), while h2(delta)=r delta+O(delta). Therefore Gamma_S0/(2I)->3/4 and (o+[Gamma_S0]_+)/(2I)->1. These are new conversion consequences of the existing A048 point-defect channel mechanism. At r=5, Gamma/(2I) is certified around 0.5148522592022458, strictly greater than 1/2; r<5 in the frozen family does not exceed 1/2. This is minimal only among the checked essential short-circuit point-defect chains, not among all Boolean functions.

For a partial tree stopping after its first essential query, let A_future=Pi. Removed influence is R=2delta, output release o=h2(delta)-h2(2delta)/2=delta+O(delta^2), and the incompatible conditioning conversion is 1-o. Its ratio to 2R diverges. At r=16 the exact lower bound exceeds 16000. Root-appending a fresh draw from P(A_future|S0) and then independently propagating the lift would produce a different joint law; identifying that draw with the actual future branch is an unaccounted channel change. Thus the Markov/side-conditioning hypothesis is essential, even when every queried coordinate is essential.

First unsupported line: one fixed normalized whole-set/witness reference supplies H(Pi|W,A)/2 once, with the required root side information, and pays the remaining signed M/terminal cost from the exclusive physical budget. This stage prices only compatible bank conversion and does not prove that line, arbitrary reference-reset compatibility, optimal conversion constants, or full FEI.

The overlapping function is exactly

    F(z,a,b,y)=1-2[(z AND a AND {g(y)=-1}) OR (z AND b AND {h(y)=-1})],

on five fair physical bits, with all 16^2 ordered core pairs. A single tree queries each shared physical bit at most once; partial leaves retain their full induced core spectra. These are validation fixtures for a universal conditioning theorem, not another isolated FEI-class claim.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A048/B10 verifies the tensor and activation scope of the existing B09-BIASED-BLOCK-UNCERTAINTY v1 candidate. It does not open a third broad potential candidate: A046 and A047 already have complete pending bundles. The original B09 uncertainty theorem remains pending independent review. Full FEI remains OPEN.

For a genuine Boolean sign f under an interior independent product input law, reveal physical block A and let B be its complement. In the normalized product Fourier basis let S have law fhat(S)^2, including the empty atom. Define M_A=H(S_A|S_B), J_A=I(Z;T), where Z is the actual physical assignment on A and T is sampled from the normalized Boolean child spectrum, L_A=H(f)-E_Z H(f_Z)=M_A+J_A, Delta_A=sum_{i in A}[h2(p_i)+log2 max(p_i,1-p_i)], and P_A=M_A+Delta_A. The existing assertion is J_A<=P_A. Entropies are bits. Deterministic coordinates are contracted. Ordinary physical influence and biased spectral energy are explicitly distinguished below.

The target is exact transformation of this ledger under aligned disjoint tensor powers, common unrevealed factors, and sparse activations. No universal bound on M or on terminal entropy is presumed. A separate collision normalization control uses v,Z,L_coll,T_coll; its L is not the restriction decrement L_A.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**1. Exact product and common-factor laws.** Biased orthonormal Fourier coefficients of f(x)g(y) on disjoint independent inputs are fhat(R)ghat(U); their squared law is a product. For aligned reveals A=A_f union A_g the physical assignment and child-spectrum experiment are also independent products. Entropy chain rules therefore give M=M_f+M_g, J=J_f+J_g, L=L_f+L_g, Delta=Delta_f+Delta_g and P=P_f+P_g. All identities hold for arbitrary finite factor counts and different biases, including zero spectral atoms. Thus t aligned copies give exactly t times each ledger, with no t log t term. Uniform H and I also multiply by t. This independently reconstructs the aligned tensor component of A046 and the narrow valid interpretation of B09 section 5; it does not extend to overlapping variables or nonlinear composition.

If an arbitrary independent Boolean factor g is wholly unrevealed, M,J,L,Delta,P are unchanged, while H(fg)=H(f)+H(g) and E H((fg)_Z)=E H(f_Z)+H(g). The proof follows either from the product channel or by cancellation in these two entropies. Taking g as t independent copies of any seed makes the unpaid terminal entropy exactly tH(seed). This is a mathematical obstruction to interpreting a paid local decrement as a paid whole entropy. It does not refute any claim that actually includes the terminal cost.

**2. Irrelevant-coordinate tax.** Append a fresh biased coordinate X which f ignores and include X in A. Its spectral label is deterministic empty and its physical value is independent of T. Hence M,J,L and revealed-block influence do not change, but Delta increases by delta(p). For 0<p<1/2, delta(p)=p log2((1-p)/p)>0. An explicit witness is f(y)=2y-1 with y fair, A={X}, p=1/8: M=J=L=I_A=0, P=(log2 7)/8>0. Even total-influence payment P<=K I(f) is impossible for the unreduced displayed P: append r such revealed dummies, retain I(f)=1, and P=r(log2 7)/8.

This is a failed *payment* of the displayed upper bound, not a failure of J<=P, and no source is alleged to have already proved that payment. Contracting physically irrelevant coordinates repairs this specific problem: apply the original candidate to A_eff, the coordinates in A on which f actually depends. Independence proves the same M,J and removes the dummy tax. It does not solve payment on essential coordinates. A character factor has positive influence and is not an irrelevant coordinate; these two operations must not be conflated.

**3. Shared biased selector and moving tensors.** Let g be any Boolean core under a fixed product law, with coefficients G_T, mean m, v=1-m^2, and F=1+X(g-1), where X is a fresh bit with P(X=1)=p. Then, for each nonempty data label T, the selector-absent/present squared masses are p^2 G_T^2 and p(1-p)G_T^2. At the empty data label they are a0=(1-p+pm)^2 and b0=p(1-p)(1-m)^2, whose sum is q0=1-pv. The residual marginal is q0 at empty and pG_T^2 elsewhere. Consequently, writing e(x)=-x log2 x and e(0)=0,

    M=e(a0)+e(b0)-e(q0)+pv h2(p),
    J=h2(pv)-p h2(v),
    H(F)=pH(g)+M+J.

The last identity follows because the two physical children are constant 1 and g. Thus M,J and debt depend only on p and the core mean, not on its Fourier entropy. For g_t=product of t disjoint copies of a fixed core with mean mu, replace m by mu^t and H(g) by tH(g_1). A common selector is not an aligned product of t selectors: its ledger is this mean-dependent expression, rather than t copies of the one-core ledger. For balanced cores, M=J=h2(p), L=2h2(p), debt zero, and the terminal term is ptH(g_1). This is consistent with A047's constant/balanced zero-debt class; it is a scope test, not a new endpoint discovery.

For |mu|<1 and fixed p, the displayed expressions are continuous as mu^t tends to zero, so M,J tend to h2(p) despite the growing core entropy. No dimension-dependent asymptotic remainder is needed: the formula is exact at every t. For a balanced core the selector's biased spectral energy is Q_X=sum_T b_T=2p(1-p), whereas its physical pivotal probability is 1/2. L/Q_X=h2(p)/(p(1-p)) diverges logarithmically as p tends to zero. This diagnostic cannot be called a divergence of L divided by physical influence; it uses spectral energy under bias. The terminal term still contains the arbitrary core problem.

**4. Uniform point-control activation, including unbalanced cores.** Now A_k is the indicator of one point on k fresh fair bits, p=2^-k, and F=1+A_k(g-1), with uniform Boolean core g and m=E g. Write d=1-m and v=1-m^2. All nonempty-data masses are p^2 G_T^2 for each of 2^k control labels. At empty data the empty-control mass is (1-pd)^2 and each nonempty-control mass is p^2d^2. Put q0=1-pv and r=p(1-p)d^2. The exact ledgers are

    M=q0 h2(r/q0)+r log2(2^k-1)+pv k,
    J=h2(pv)-p h2(v),
    L=e((1-pd)^2)+(p-p^2)d^2[2k-log2(d^2)]+2kpv-p e(m^2),
    I_A=kp d,   I(F)=pI(g)+kp d,   E H(F_Z)=pH(g).

Terms involving zero d are zero by continuity. Direct edge counting proves the influence: each control is pivotal with probability 2p P(g=-1)=pd, and each data influence is multiplied by p. The entropy expression follows by summing the two explicit mass classes and subtracting pH(g).

There is an elementary paid local bound for this specific activation block. Since e((1-y)^2)<=2y/ln2 for 0<=y<=1, and d^2 log2(1/d^2)<=2d/(e ln2) for 0<d<=1 (for d>=1 this term is nonpositive), discard the negative -p e(m^2) and -2kp^2d^2 terms to obtain

    0<=L<=4kp d+[2(1+1/e)/ln2]pd
            <=[4+2(1+1/e)/(k ln2)] I_A <=8 I_A.

Here d^2+v=2d. The constant in square brackets is below 8: e>8/3 and ln2>11/16 imply 2(1+1/e)/ln2<4. The latter logarithm bound follows from the first two positive atanh terms, ln2>2(1/3+1/81)=56/81>11/16. The final inequality is strict when I_A>0; the d=0 case is identically zero. Positivity of L follows from L=M+J, each an entropy/information. This paid activation decrement is compatible with arbitrary terminal entropy pH(g); it does not close FEI for arbitrary activated cores.

Keeping logarithms and remainders separate gives a uniform estimate across every m in [-1,1], including moving tensors:

    |L-4pk(1-m)|<=17p+8kp^2,
    |J-pkv|<3p,
    |M-pk(3-4m+m^2)|<=20p+8kp^2.

To check the first bound, the empty term is at most 4p/ln2<8p; |d^2 log2 d^2|<=8 on [0,4]; e(m^2)<=1/(e ln2)<1; and 2kp^2d^2<=8kp^2. For J, expand h2(pv)-p h2(v)=pvk+e(1-pv)-p e(1-v); the remaining absolute value is at most p/ln2+p/(e ln2)<3p. Thus the only activation logarithm is k=log2(1/p); no t log t appears. The balanced case reduces to the exact identities already proved in threshold Proposition 8.9 and A021. The present general-mean ledger supplies a verification envelope across unbalanced tensor cores.

**5. Collision normalization and Renyi guards.** For any finite nonnegative collision array v with total Z>0, L_coll=sum e(v_U), R=v/Z and T_coll=L_coll/Z, direct expansion gives T_coll=H(R)-log2 Z. For product-compatible amplitude references the collision arrays tensor, so Z_t=Z^t, L_t=t Z^(t-1)L_coll, T_t=tT_coll, H(R_t)=tH(R). The raw exponential factor is a known control, not a discovery here. Rescaling v by c>0 preserves R but sends T to T-log2 c. A collision tensor factor with Z!=1 must therefore retain its log normalizer; arbitrary independent factors cannot be erased from raw-L payments. Product phase completions must also be preserved; pointwise sign choices at zeros are not a tensor theorem. No new Boolean collision payment or recovery bound is claimed.

The supplied exact point-defect calculation shows each fixed lower-order spectral Renyi entropy H_alpha/I tends to infinity for alpha<1, whereas Shannon H/I tends to 4. A fixed-Renyi substitution cannot be used to repair any of the tested budgets. This is an existing control, not a new disproof of Shannon FEI.

The first unsupported universal line is: after contracting irrelevant revealed coordinates, pay the complete stopping-tree conditional cost plus the expected terminal entropy by one dimension-independent influence budget. Aligned additivity, common-factor cancellation and a paid point-control decrement do not establish that statement.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A048 stage 2 follows B10 STREAM_NOTES v2 (2026-10-11 23:31Z): determine whether the sum of the existing sharp taxes on essential revealed bits can be paid by an absolute multiple of influence on point-defect tensors and activations. This is payment/scope verification of the existing potential, not a new potential or another broad candidate. Full FEI remains OPEN.

Define the lead's angular tax tau*(p)=h2(p)-min_t F_p(t), where F_p(t)=h2(sin^2 t)+h2(sin^2(t-b)) and sin^2 b=min(p,1-p). The one-selector actual debt is E=J-M; all children are actual Boolean restrictions, Fourier bases are orthonormal for the actual product law, and entropies include the empty coefficient. For a finite physical singleton tree, node reach weights are w_v and the blanket tax bill is A_T=sum_v w_v tau*(p_{i_v}), charged only at essential queries, with no coordinate reused along a path. The target payment is A_T<=C I for one finite constant C over the tested family and all parameters.

Under nonuniform physical inputs distinguish I_phys=sum_i Pr(f(X)!=f(X^i)) from spectral energy I_spec=sum_S |S| fhat_p(S)^2. They coincide only on the uniform cube. A collapsed mask with induced bias is a different coordinate system from its physical controls. A036's K is a KL divergence, not an absolute multiplicative constant; we call it K_KL and separately test whether it could cover the collapsed tax. All logarithms are base two.

Outcome: true physical tax on the fair A036 class is identically zero. The essential-bit blanket tax payment fails on genuine biased point defects even for an optimal essential computing tree. It also fails when a fixed group of fair controls is priced as one biased logical mask and the core is a growing point defect. The actual uncertainty-defect reserve cancels almost all overcharge, and the positive residual actual debt is paid on arbitrary-product-biased point-defect trees by 4 I_phys. These are exact scope results, not a Shannon FEI disproof or a universal tree theorem.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**1. What is unconditional about tau*.** Its angular definition alone gives tau*(p)<=h2(p). Choosing an eigenstate gives min F<=h2(p), so tau*>=0. For any actual unit-norm child pair, the exact posterior/parent-mass identity gives

    E=J-M=h2(p)-sum_T q_T F_p(t_T)<=tau*(p).

This elementary reduction is rebuilt from the actual two child coefficient vectors as in the lead report. Thus the genuine biased AND instance at p=1/8,q=1/4, whose independent exact certificate gives E>1195/10000, proves tau*(1/8)>1195/10000. No numerical minimization, claim of attaining tau* by a Boolean pair, or unaccepted half-tax bound is needed.

At p=1/2, tau*=0 exactly. To see this without a theorem citation, expand h2((1+z)/2)=1-sum_{k>=1} z^(2k)/(ln2*2k(2k-1)). The coefficients are positive and sum to one (evaluate at z=1 by monotone convergence), so h2(x)>=4x(1-x). With x=sin^2 t and y=sin^2(t-pi/4), the two lower bounds sum to sin^2(2t)+cos^2(2t)=1. F>=1 and F(0)=1. This proves min F=1 and tau*=h2(1/2)-1=0.

**2. Essential biased point-defect tax is unpayable.** Let f_r=1-2 product_{i=1}^r X_i, with independent P(X_i=1)=q=1/8. Every physical coordinate is essential. Its exceptional input has probability delta=q^r. Each physical pivotal probability is q^(r-1), so

    I_phys=r q^(r-1),   I_spec=4r(1-q)q^r.

In every exact essential singleton computing tree, the unique live all-one path must query every remaining bit until it sees a zero or exhausts the bits. A zero makes the restriction constant; before seeing a zero every remaining bit is essential. The reach weights along the exceptional path are 1,q,...,q^(r-1). All minimal essential policies therefore have the identical expected query count (1-q^r)/(1-q). More queries cannot improve a nonnegative tax bill. Randomized policies are mixtures and obey the same lower bound. Consequently

    A_T=tau*(q) (1-q^r)/(1-q),
    A_T/I_phys=tau*(q) (1-q^r)/[(1-q)r q^(r-1)] -> infinity.

This fails a payment by any finite C even though the dummy-coordinate repair has been made, all queried bits are essential, and the tree is already optimal for the tax. It also fails payment by spectral energy, which differs here by the fixed factor 4q(1-q). For t disjoint copies, influence and the expected tax bill both multiply by t; the unbounded parameter is defect dimension r, not tensor count. The same tax payment failure holds under a common arbitrary independent unrevealed factor when charged only on the defect coordinates; the factor's terminal entropy must remain.

The corresponding physical path entropy is

    H(transcript)=h2(q)(1-q^r)/(1-q).

Hence A_T<=H(transcript) follows from tau*<=h2, but this path entropy also has unbounded ratio to influence. Since the tree computes f, H(transcript|f)=H(transcript)-h2(q^r), which tends to h2(q)/(1-q)>0. C024 path entropy is an explicit possible accounting source, not an influence-paid free credit. This closes the lead's unqualified essential-bit tax payment question negatively; it does not refute a reserve-corrected ledger.

**3. Exact residual and uncertainty-defect cancellation.** For any actual selector node set U(p)=min F_p and

    D_v=sum_T q_T[F_p(t_T)-U(p)]>=0.

Then tau*(p)-D_v=E_v=J_v-M_v exactly. This is the uncertainty-defect reserve proposed by the lead/A047. For the actual tree,

    A_T-sum_v w_v D_v=sum_v w_v E_v,
    H(f)-E H(leaf)=sum_v w_v(M_v+J_v).

A reserve must be transported on the same physical tree; it cannot be replaced by a second independent copy of path entropy or an unrelated KL reference.

For a point-defect split with selected bias p and residual exceptional probability eps, the inactive child is constant and the active child has mean m=1-2eps, variance v=4eps(1-eps). From direct masses,

    J=h2(pv)-p h2(v),
    M=e((1-p+pm)^2)+e(p(1-p)(1-m)^2)-e(1-pv)+pv h2(p).

These are exact for all eps, including the final eps=1 split. The residual data sample is empty for X=0; for X=1 it is nonempty with probability v, and its nonempty label law is independent of X. Enrich the experiment with an independent Bernoulli(v) erasure flag and that nonempty label. The enriched channel reveals X only when the flag is one, so data processing gives

    [E]_+ <= J <= v h2(p) <=4eps h2(p).

For a heterogeneous point defect with delta=product_i p_i, in any essential short-circuit ordering the reach weight before querying i is w=delta/(p_i eps). Thus

    sum_v w_v [E_v]_+ <=sum_v w_v J_v
           <=4 delta sum_i h2(p_i)/p_i <=4 I_phys.

No ancestral influence is reused: w_v Inf_{i_v}(f_v)=delta/p_i, and the sum is precisely I_phys. The same proof applies factor by factor to finite disjoint point-defect tensors, because wholly unrevealed factors preserve the local M,J and actual pivotal probability. This pays the positive *reserve-corrected* tax debt on this family. It does not pay the conditional M sum for arbitrary children, prove general FEI under arbitrary biases, or admit overlapping control events.

For homogeneous q fixed, the exact spectrum also quantifies cancellation. For nonempty S,

    P_S=4delta^2 product_{i in S}(1-q)/q,
    P_empty=(1-2delta)^2,
    H(f_r)=e((1-2delta)^2)+e(4delta)+4delta r h2(q)-e(4delta^2).

Therefore, as r tends to infinity,

    H/I_phys ->4q[log2(1/q)+h2(q)],
    J_tree/I_phys ->4q log2(1/q),
    E_tree/I_phys ->4q[log2(1/q)-h2(q)].

For the second limit, write s for the number of residual bits after each query. Uniformly for sufficiently large s at fixed q, J_s=4q^(s+1)log2(1/q)+O_q(q^(2s)); this follows from h2(qv)-q h2(v)=qv log2(1/q)+O_q(v^2). Multiply by reach q^(r-s-1) and sum s=0,...,r-1; the finite small-s terms and the geometric remainder are O_q(q^(r-1)). Thus J_tree=4r q^r log2(1/q)+O_q(q^(r-1)). The entropy formula has remainder O_q(delta)+O_q(rdelta^2). Division proves the three limits, with errors O_q(1/r)+O_q(delta). At q=1/8 the last limit is positive: h2(q)<1<3=log2(1/q), so actual positive debt remains even though it tends to zero in absolute units.

Since |E_tree|<=H (M_tree,J_tree>=0 and H=M_tree+J_tree), E_tree tends to zero while A_T tends to tau*(q)/(1-q)>0. Thus sum wD_v/A_T ->1. Almost all of the blanket tax must be canceled by actual uncertainty defect; simply dropping the reserve changes an influence-scale charge into a constant charge. This is the exact correction this verification supplies to A050/A064.

**4. Fair activation versus logical-mask pricing, inside A036's original class.** Let g_n be a fair n-bit point defect, let A_3 be a point indicator on three fresh fair controls, and F_n=1+A_3(g_n-1). Then F_n is exactly a fair point defect on N=n+3 bits. Every bit is essential, and it has full Fourier support for N>=2. Each actual physical query has p_i=1/2 and tax zero; A036's K_KL>=T/4-8I holds at its stated uniform class if that proof is accepted. This present verification does not need to assume that inequality.

If the three controls are instead priced as one logical selector of bias p=1/8, the assigned tax is at least 1195/10000 independently of n. The actual influences are

    d=2^(1-N),   I_total=N d,   I_controls=3d.

Both tend to zero, so the collapsed tax cannot be paid by C I_total or C I_controls for any finite C. The prior general-mean activation bound pays the actual physical block decrement by 8I_controls; it does not pay the collapsed scalar upper-bound tax. The logical M of the mask and physical M of the three-control block differ, even though their J channels agree because all inactive assignments have the same child.

Nor does the original product-marginal KL budget supply a missing constant reserve. For this uniform point defect, K_KL=N h2(d)-H(F_n)>=0, and

    K_KL<=N h2(d)<=N d[log2(1/d)+log2 e]<N(N+1)d ->0.

Here log2 e<2. Thus for any fixed a,b>=0, aK_KL+bI_total tends to zero and cannot pay tau*(1/8). A finite rational certificate is N=13: K_KL+8I_total <13*22/4096=143/2048<1195/10000<tau*(1/8). This lies within A036's point-defect class and exposes only the extra collapsed-mask tax payment, not the collision inequality or recovery inequality themselves. It cannot be repaired by rescaling the raw collision L, which is retired. On disjoint tensors K_KL and I both add; any repeated collapsed-mask bill adds, so no tensor normalization changes this comparison.

For contrast, a genuinely balanced core under k fair point controls has I_controls=kp, p=2^-k. A single logical-mask tax obeys the unconditional bound tau*(p)<=h2(p)<=p[k+log2 e], so it is paid by (1+log2 e/k)I_controls. Conditional on acceptance of the lead's half-tax theorem it is at most (kp)/2. The near-constant-core counterexample shows precisely why essentiality alone does not provide this balanced-core payment. Neither statement bounds arbitrary terminal H(g).

The first unsupported line for a universal repair is: transport the actual D_v reserve (and any compatible KL credit) on one physical tree so that the remaining M_v and terminal entropy are paid from exclusive physical influence accounts. This stage proves that transport/payment for the positive E_v part on point-defect tensors only; it does not establish the general residual bound.
