# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A049 / B10 minimal falsifier extraction. Full uniform-cube FEI remains OPEN. This stage is an extraction and verification of the already refuted B09-TAXFREE-BIASED-UNCERTAINTY helper, not a new search against that retired assertion. Its scope is: for every finite Boolean sign under an interior product bias and every singleton physical reveal X, claim J_X <= M_X, with M_X=H(S_X|S_res), and J_X=I(X;T) in the experiment that chooses the physical child with its actual probability and then samples its normalized squared Fourier spectrum. All entropies use bits, include the empty atom, and use the product-biased orthonormal basis. Zero atoms contribute zero.

The extraction gives the smallest total physical dimension, n=2, one fixed common bias p=q=1/4, a complete four-entry truth table, and one integer sign comparison. More restrictive minima are n=3 with all residual coordinates fair, and n=4 with fair residual coordinates and both physical children balanced. The latter minima hold for arbitrary interior selector bias, not just the displayed 1/4. The common dyadic bias has the smallest possible interior dyadic denominator: denominator 2 is the uniform case and fails to witness the assertion on every two-bit truth table; denominator 4 supplies the witness. No optimality over all rational denominators or gate representations is claimed.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Definitions and exact Boolean source

For independent bits X,Y in {0,1} with Pr(X=1)=p and Pr(Y=1)=q, take

    f(X,Y)=1-2XY.

Equivalently on the sign cube, X=(1+x)/2 and Y=(1+y)/2. The input bias here is not uniform; the corresponding sign-coordinate means are -1/2 when p=q=1/4.

| X | Y | f |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 0 | +1 |
| 1 | 0 | +1 |
| 0 | 1 | +1 |
| 1 | 1 | -1 |

In the orthonormal centered basis phi_X=(X-p)/sqrt(p(1-p)), phi_Y=(Y-q)/sqrt(q(1-q)), the coefficients are

    1-2pq, -2q sqrt(p(1-p)), -2p sqrt(q(1-q)),
    -2 sqrt(p(1-p)q(1-q)).

At p=q=1/4 these become (7/8,-sqrt(3)/8,-sqrt(3)/8,-3/8). Their squared law, ordered empty,X,Y,XY, is

    P=(49,3,3,9)/64.

The retained Y-label marginal is Q=(13,3)/16. The X=0 child is constant with spectrum (1,0). The X=1 child is 1-2Y with spectrum R=(1,3)/4. Thus Q=(3/4)(1,0)+(1/4)R, proving that the channel is the actual normalized physical-child experiment.

The definitions give

    M=H(P)-H(Q),   J=H(Q)-(1/4)H(R),
    E:=J-M=2H(Q)-H(P)-(1/4)H(R).

These formulas also follow directly from the entropy chain rule, without using B09's pending uncertainty theorem.

## 2. One exact integer sign certificate

Expanding the entropy expressions and collecting prime logarithms gives

    32 E = 48 + 6 log2(3) + 49 log2(7) - 52 log2(13)
         = log2(L/R),
    L=2^48 * 3^6 * 7^49,
    R=13^52.

Exact integers:

    L=52719499781386523982199270969636322098232779259710399315968
    R= 8415003868347247618489696679505181495471801448798649088081
    L-R=44304495913039276363709574290131140602760977810911750227887.

Hence E>0 follows from one integer comparison. The stronger comparisons 4R<L<8R give the rigorous margin

    1/16 < J-M < 3/32 bits.

No approximation to a logarithm, interval library, transcendental error bound or floating-point entropy evaluation is needed. The generic checker independently clears all rational entropy weights: it obtains the rational product (L/R)^2 and scale 64, agreeing with the collected-prime expression.

## 3. Minimal number of variables, and the common bias

On n<=1, revealing a relevant nonempty block leaves no residual coordinate. The retained spectral label is deterministic, so J=0. If the revealed block is empty or irrelevant, J=M=0. Since conditional entropy M>=0, no smaller-dimensional violation exists. The displayed two-bit Boolean witness proves dimension minimality over all interior product biases.

For the common dyadic bias, the only interior choice of reduced denominator 2 is p=q=1/2. Every two-bit sign truth table is either a signed character (8 tables) or has exactly one sign different from the other three (8 tables). A signed character has a point-mass spectrum, and E=0. A one-defect table has flat squared spectrum on four labels, so H(P)=2, H(Q)=1; revealing a coordinate gives one constant child and one fair dictator child, whose average entropy is 1/2. Hence E=2-2-1/2=-1/2. This proves analytically, and exhaustive exact arithmetic confirms, that the coarsest common interior dyadic bias supplies no violation. The denominator-4 choice p=q=1/4 does.

This is a fixed finite simplification. Setting both p and q rare together is not a replacement for A046's sharp endpoint family, which fixes q=1/4 and takes only p to zero.

## 4. Fair residual coordinates: minimum n=3

Let g,h be the actual Boolean children on a fair m-bit residual cube, and let the independent selector choose h with probability p. With G_T,H_T their real Fourier coefficients, parent masses and the residual marginal are

    a_T=((1-p)G_T+pH_T)^2,
    b_T=p(1-p)(H_T-G_T)^2,
    q_T=(1-p)G_T^2+pH_T^2.

If g=h, then M=J=0. If g=-h, their squared spectra coincide, so J=0 and M=h2(4p(1-p))>=0. If their spectral supports are disjoint, the residual label determines the physical child and J=h2(p). Each occupied fiber has selector-label entropy h2(p), so M=h2(p) and E=0.

On m<=1, the Boolean children are signed characters, so every pair is equal, opposite or has disjoint spectral supports. Thus no selector bias can produce E>0 with fewer than two fair residual coordinates.

For m=2 choose g=1 and h=1-2UV, with U,V independent fair bits. The h coefficients are (1/2,1/2,1/2,-1/2), up to truth-table convention. At p=1/4 the parent squared masses consist of (49,3)/64 at the empty residual label and (1,3)/64 at each of its three nonempty residual labels. The residual marginal is (13,1,1,1)/16, and H(hat h^2)=2.

Aggregating those three identical nonempty fibers, or substituting in the entropy expression, gives exactly the same E as section 2. Refining a residual label into three equally weighted labels adds (3p/4)log2(3) to H(Q), the same amount to H(P), and (3/4)log2(3) to H(hat h^2); their coefficients 2,-1,-p cancel. The parent circuit is

    F(X,U,V)=1-2XUV,   Pr(X=1)=1/4, U,V fair.

Its truth table, indexed X+2U+4V, is [1,1,1,1,1,1,1,-1]. This supplies the n=3 witness, with the same integer certificate, and proves its minimum under fair residual inputs.

## 5. Balanced children with fair residual inputs: minimum n=4

A balanced Boolean function on a fair two-bit cube has two plus signs and two minus signs. The six such tables are exactly the signed nonempty characters. Balanced children on m<=2 therefore again fall into the equal, opposite or disjoint cases, which have E<=0 for every selector bias. On m=0 balanced sign children do not exist.

On three fair residual bits Z,U,V set

    g(Z,U,V)=(-1)^Z,
    h(Z,U,V)=(-1)^Z(1-2UV).

Both children are balanced. Multiplying both earlier children by a fresh fair character translates every Fourier label by the same fixed set. It preserves H(P), H(Q), both child entropies, M and J. The full circuit is

    F(X,Z,U,V)=(-1)^Z(1-2XUV),
    Pr(X=1)=1/4, Z,U,V fair.

The complete 16-entry truth table, spectra and both balanced child tables are in out/BALANCED_CERTIFICATE.json. The same positive E and integer comparison hold. Thus n=4 is minimal when residual coordinates must be fair and both actual physical children balanced. The checker verifies invariance by comparing the independently generated entropy-product ratios exactly, not by comparing decimal entropies.

## 6. Scope and first unsupported line

This package minimizes a known failed tax-free helper. It does not refute B09's J<=M+Delta(p), A096's sharper uniform upper tax, coefficient-1/2 taxed proposals, existential coordinate selection, an optimized stopping tree, or full FEI. In the original uniform cube the two-bit AND spectrum is flat and H/I=2, providing no conjecture violation. A positive finite biased helper debt is not an unbounded uniform H/I family.

First unsupported line toward the main target: bound the full conditional cost M plus a valid bias tax, and the terminal entropy, by a single nonreused physical influence budget. Neither finite minimality nor the known sharp endpoint coefficient proves that step.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## Actual Boolean enumeration and exact transform

Fix m and N=2^m. Each truth table supplies four children indexed z=z1+2z2, with integer residual Walsh vectors W_z(T). Each normalized child coefficient is W_z(T)/N. Global output negation changes every coefficient's sign and leaves M,J and all squared spectra fixed. Fix the all-zero input output to +1: exactly half the tables remain, with no fixed points under negation. No variable, bias or input-complement quotient is used.

Write p1=a/8, p2=b/8, a,b in {1,2,3,4}. For each residual label T put

    L=(8-a)(8-b)W00+a(8-b)W10+(8-a)b W01+ab W11,
    D1=(8-b)(W10-W00)+b(W11-W01),
    D2=(8-a)(W01-W00)+a(W11-W10),
    D12=W11-W01-W10+W00.

The squared parent masses, indexed by the two block frequency bits, are exactly

    [L^2, a(8-a)D1^2, b(8-b)D2^2,
     a(8-a)b(8-b)D12^2] / (4096 N^2).

These formulas follow by expanding each centered selector basis and averaging the independent physical selectors. The residual marginal Q_T is the sum of those four masses. Independently, Q_T=sum_z mu_A(z)W_z(T)^2/N^2, with physical child weights

    [(8-a)(8-b),a(8-b),(8-a)b,ab]/64.

Let P be the entire parent law and C_z the normalized child law. Then the chain-rule definitions give the exact debt

    E=J_A-M_A=2H(Q)-H(P)-sum_z mu_A(z)H(C_z).

Every transformed table is checked for nonnegative masses and exact normalization by integer array comparisons. The 48 attaining records are independently reconstructed by direct weighted truth-table centered-basis moments, divided by rational squared basis norms; their child marginal identities are checked as Fractions.

## Certifying the target without assuming additivity

For any single-qubit angle with x=sin^2 t,

    y=sin^2(t-beta)=p+(1-2p)x-2sqrt(p(1-p)x(1-x)).

The definition of tau* immediately implies

    tau*(p) >= h2(p)-h2(x)-h2(y).

Use x=1/32,1/16,1/256 at p=1/8,1/4,3/8, respectively. Rational square-root brackets from integer isqrt at scale 2^100 enclose y, which lies in [0,1/2]. Binary entropy is increasing there, so rational entropy endpoint bounds enclose each witness debt. At p=1/2, a basis vector gives tau*>=0. No minimizer characterization, real-child attainment theorem, half-Delta theorem or tensor-additivity theorem is assumed by this comparison.

Each enumerated E is bounded above by the sum of these explicit lower witnesses. Therefore it is bounded above by the sum of the true tau* values. The weakest strictly positive certified gap over every nonuniform bias pair, every m and every table exceeds 7/1000 bit; its exact rational value is in out/INDEPENDENT_RECONSTRUCTION.json. The pair (1/2,1/2) is certified separately by its exactly zero finite maximum.

## Exact finite maxima and ties

Each rational entropy contribution is rounded outwards to a common 2^-40 lattice. The maximum lower endpoint and maximum upper endpoint enclose the finite supremum. Choose a witness attaining the greatest lower endpoint; every candidate whose upper endpoint overlaps it is expanded into a rational prime-log coefficient vector. Every such vector equals the witness's vector exactly. All other candidates have upper endpoint strictly below that witness's lower endpoint. This proves exact finite maximality and exact tie counts. Equality of coefficient vectors is sufficient; no independence or converse theorem for prime logarithms is asserted.

For m=0,1, the exact maximum is zero at every bias pair. For m=2 let E1,E2,E3 denote the exact two-fair-residual singleton maxima at selector biases 1/8,1/4,3/8, respectively, and E4=0. This block census gives maximum E_min(a,b). Exact prime-log vectors and all counts are in out/CENSUS.json; approximate displays are E1=0.1195450160, E2=0.0827281513, E3=0.0094404728. Those decimals are descriptive, not the sign certificate. The equality with the already completed A047 singleton census is an observed and exactly checked finite identity, not a theorem for more residual bits.

The zero-denominator pair p1=p2=1/2 is excluded from the ratio. Among the other pairs, the global ratio maximum occurs at m=2 and bias pair (1/8,1/2), or its selector-coordinate interchange. Bounds on all other pairs are strictly smaller. Exact symmetry and the prime-log expressions identify the two attaining bias pairs, rather than relying on displayed decimal equality.

## Minimal attaining certificate with both revealed bits essential

Let X1 have probability 1/8 of being 1, and let X2,U,V be fair independent bits. Reveal A={X1,X2} and set

    f(X1,X2,U,V)=(-1)^X2 (1-2X1UV).

All four coordinates are essential. Its complete truth table, indexed X1+2X2+4U+8V, has mask 37683; the actual 16 sign values and the exact circuit/spectra are stored in out/EXTREMAL_CERTIFICATE.json. Multiplication by the fair revealed character (-1)^X2 translates the block spectral label; its squared child spectra are unchanged by the physical X2 sign. Thus the actual block debt equals that of the rare-selector quarter-defect singleton without silently treating X2 as an irrelevant physical input.

The exact debt E satisfies

    128 E=224+225 log2(3)+225 log2(5)+14 log2(7)-232 log2(29).

Its positive sign is certified by the modest-denominator integer comparison

    2^224 * 3^225 * 5^225 * 7^14 > 29^232.

Here Delta_A/2=(log2 7)/16. Consequently the exact global ratio is

    R=[224+225 log2(3)+225 log2(5)+14 log2(7)-232 log2(29)]
      /[8 log2(7)].

Sign-correct rational log bounds certify

    68132470/100000000 < R < 68132471/100000000.

The entire frozen grid with m<=1 has maximum zero. This attaining positive-debt circuit therefore has the minimum possible four total bits inside the searched domain, including the requirement that both revealed bits be essential. No minimality outside the frozen bias grid/fair residual assumptions is claimed. It is an extremal stress certificate, not a violation of the target bound.

## Logarithm error proof and arithmetic limits

For positive rational u, write u=2^k y with 1<=y<2, and z=(y-1)/(y+1) in [0,1/3). The 32-term rational sum

    L(y)=2 sum_{j=0}^{31} z^(2j+1)/(2j+1)

has nonnegative remainder at most 2z^65/[65(1-z^2)]. The same expansion at y=2, z=1/3, gives strictly positive rational bounds on ln2. Divide the nonnegative ln(y) endpoints by opposite ln2 endpoints and add k. Multiplication by -u reverses endpoints before dyadic rounding. All cached mass evaluations and integer array aggregations preserve outward containment. NumPy arrays contain only small integers: counts, mass numerators, and fixed-lattice entropy sums; no floating-point spectrum, entropy, search selection or sign enters a certificate.

The initial verifier mistakenly required an exactly zero debt's rounded upper endpoint to be zero; it failed before any expensive ratio calculation. That assertion was repaired to use the already certified empty prime-log vector. A potential oversized integer exponentiation for fine ratio bounds was also removed before execution; a process-stop check found no matching live process. Only the entropy sign with denominator 128 uses integer powers. Fine ratio signs use the rational log bounds above. out/VERIFIER_REPAIR.md records these facts. There was no oversized job or artifact and the census was unaffected.

First unsupported line: extending this restricted verification to arbitrary residual dimension or residual product biases, or funding the valid tax and conditional M plus terminal entropy in a universal nonreusing C019 ledger. # exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Entropies are bits, influence is pivotal probability. The false extension under test is

    o/2 + [Gamma_W]_+/2 <= R/ln(2),
    o=I(Y;Pi), Gamma_W=H(Pi|Y)-H(Pi|W), R=I(F)-E I(F_leaf),

when W is allowed to be the actual adaptively labelled terminal spectrum without the root-Markov hypothesis. A047's root-compatible theorem and A050's repaired injection allowance are not refuted. Full FEI is OPEN.

We give a fixed 17-variable exact certificate, down from A048's 56-variable finite certificate. Moreover 17 is the minimum over the following explicitly restricted class: F=G(x) times parity on 2^r separate coordinates, G an arbitrary uniform k-bit Boolean core; query r distinct fixed core coordinates in order, each essential at every reached node; then query one parity coordinate indexed injectively by the full r-bit prefix and stop; use W equal to the actual ambient terminal spectral set. Here r>=1, k>=r, n=k+2^r. No minimum over arbitrary Boolean functions or arbitrary stopping trees is claimed.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Encode a cube sign coordinate by (-1)^bit. Put z=(z0,z1,z2), u=(u0,...,u5), each bit uniform, and let A0=1_{u=0}, A1=1_{u=(1,0,...,0)}. Define the Boolean circuit

    N = (A0 AND ((z2=0) OR (z0=z1=1)))
        OR (A1 AND (z1=z2=1)),
    G = 1-2N,
    F = G product_{b=0}^7 (-1)^{y_b}.

The two guard events are disjoint. In least-significant-bit order z0,z1,z2,u0,...,u5, the complete negative core set is {0,1,2,3,7,14,15}; this supplies a second exact description without parameters. Query z0,z1,z2, obtain B=z0+2z1+4z2, then query y_B, obtain C and stop. Pi=(B,C) is uniform on 16 transcripts.

The undirected core crossing-edge counts in coordinate order are (1,3,5,5,7,7,7,7,7). Each crossing edge contributes 2/512=1/256 influence. Hence I(G)=49/256 and I(F)=8+49/256=2097/256. The seven reached core-query node influences are, by depth and prefix, 1/256; (1/128,1/64); (1/64,1/64,1/32,1/64). All are positive, and every y_B query has influence one. Every root coordinate is essential as well. Averaging these node influences and adding the final parity query gives R=1+(1+3+5)/256=265/256. The terminal influence is exactly 229/32, so no remaining parity influence has been discarded.

For completeness the terminal core functions comprise five one-point defects on six guard bits, two constants, and one two-point defect at {0,e0}, the last equivalent to a one-point defect on five bits and independent of u0. Thus, writing h_t=1-2*1_{0^t},

    E H(F_leaf) = (5/8)H(h_6)+(1/8)H(h_5),
    E I(F_leaf) = 7+(5/8)*6*2^-5+(1/8)*5*2^-4 = 229/32.

The root entropy is H(G), independently certified by the integer Walsh transform; its complete exact prime-log vector is in certificate.json. At least seven unqueried parity coordinates remain at every leaf. Therefore Y=F is conditionally fair given Pi, o=0 and H(Pi|Y)=4.

Use A048's coherent lift explicitly: for a parent spectral set S at query i let T=S\{i}, q_T=hat f(T)^2+hat f(T union {i})^2; choose physical outcome b with probability hat f_b(T)^2/(2q_T), and retain T. The sum of the two probabilities is one because hat f_b(T)=hat f(T)+(-1)^b hat f(T union {i}). Summing over the root law gives the physical fair outcome and the squared Fourier law in that child. Iterating gives W=S0\Q(Pi), with actual descendant laws. Zero-mass q_T pairs are not sampled.

The root parity spectrum is the deterministic full eight-label set. Consequently the parity part of W is precisely that set minus y_B, and its missing label recovers B. Its law given B does not depend on C, since changing the queried parity bit changes only a global sign. It follows exactly that I(W;Pi)=3 and H(Pi|W)=1. Therefore Gamma_W=3 and conversion=3/2 bits. Conditional on S0, W is equivalent to B: its core part is the deterministic root projection off z0,z1,z2 and its missing parity label encodes B. Hence

    e=H(W|S0)=I(W;Pi|S0)=H(B|S0)=3-I(S0;Pi).

The checker constructs the entire rational joint root/prefix/terminal law (3,740 nonzero final entries), checks all physical prefix masses and all child Fourier laws, and proves this entropy identity by exact prime-log cancellation. Fixed y0 instead of y_B gives e=0, also checked by exact cancellation. This distinguishes adaptive ambient labels from a root-conditional copy. The repaired A050 theorem carries e/2 and is respected.

The sign certificate needs only three terms of the positive atanh series. For z=1/3,

    L=2(z+z^3/3+z^5/5)=842/1215 < ln(2)
      < L+2z^7/(7(1-z^2))=23581/34020=U <139/200.

The lower bound is strict since the remaining terms are positive; the upper bound follows by replacing all remaining odd denominators by 7 and summing the geometric tail. Since L>265/384,

    3/2 - (265/256)/ln(2)
      > 3/2 - (265/256)/L
      =1353/215552 >1/256 >0.

This is a rigorous sign comparison, not a floating search. F is far from a disproof of FEI: its Fourier support has at most 512 points, so H(F)<=9 while I(F)>8 and H(F)/I(F)<9/8.

Minimum in the stated class: at depth ell<r there are 2^ell reached prefix cells. Essentiality of the queried coordinate in each cell requires at least one crossing edge in that cell. The cells are disjoint for this coordinate, giving Inf_ell(G)>=2^ell*2^(1-k). Influence on an unqueried coordinate averages exactly under restriction of other coordinates. Consequently

    R >= 1+(2^r-1)*2^(1-k).

As above conversion=r/2 for every G in the class. For r<=2, r/2<=1<R/ln(2), so no violation. For r=3 and k<=8, R>=1+7/128=135/128, whereas (3/2)ln(2)<(3/2)(139/200)=417/400<135/128; again no violation. For r>=4, n=k+2^r>=r+2^r>=20. Thus no member has n<17, and our k=9,r=3 member attains n=17. This argument quantifies over all cores in the class rather than enumerating them.

An earlier analytic 16-variable idea with a single point defect fails essentiality: after its first zero query the remaining core becomes constant. It was rejected before execution, and is not exported as an essential-tree certificate. The first unsupported global line would be removing the fixed-prefix/parity assumptions from the minimality theorem. We do not take that step. The universal FEI history-bank payment also remains unproved.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## Arbitrary-seed reduction and signed common-factor change

Let I_g=I(g). Direct physical edges give

    Inf_a(F)=Inf_z(F)=alpha/2,
    I(F)=1+alpha+I_g/4,
    average I(F_child)=1+alpha/2+I_g/4.

Thus removed influence d=alpha/2, with the common parity's influence ONE retained at both terminals. The query is essential for every alpha>0; if g depends on every seed bit all root coordinates are essential. Both terminal functions are nonconstant because y remains.

Both distinct parent cores have mean zero, so R_lab(F)=1. At a=0 the contracted child has one label and reserve zero; at a=1 both distinct cores again have mean zero and reserve one. Hence kappa(F)=1/2 and kappa/d=1/alpha. For g=1-2*1_{w=0^t}, t>=1, alpha=2^-t and all variables are essential. Consequently kappa/d=2^t is unbounded. This is a data-query extension of the known shared-core reserve objection, not a refutation of its independent-core theorem.

Before multiplying by y, E h=1-alpha and E g=1-2alpha. The same contracted labels give

    R_lab(G)=alpha-alpha^2/2,
    average R_lab(G_child)=alpha(1-alpha),
    kappa(G)=alpha^2/2.

The common parity leaves every root/child Fourier entropy, erased-sector M, coherent branch information J and the true D unchanged, because it only shifts Fourier labels by a deterministic parity frequency. It boosts kappa by (1-alpha^2)/2, and shifts the residual by its NEGATIVE. Exactly

    D = R_lab(root)-average R_lab(child)+(D-kappa).

No positive release can be removed from this equality independently of its signed companion. The fixed query set {a} also gives zero actual descendant-reference injection: W=S0\{a} is a deterministic root projection. This fixture has no schedule-information loophole.

## The entire seed spectrum cancels from the data decrement

Put phi(p)=-p log2 p, with phi(0)=0. At the root, empty-seed-frequency masses are (1-alpha/2)^2 and three copies of alpha^2/4. At the active child they are (1-alpha)^2 and alpha^2; the inactive child is a character and has entropy zero. For each nonempty seed Fourier coefficient c, the root has four masses c^2/16 and the active child has two masses c^2/4. In D their contribution is (c^2/2), whose sum is Var(g)/2=2alpha(1-alpha). Therefore, for EVERY seed, independently of its Fourier shape,

    D(alpha)=phi((1-alpha/2)^2)+3phi(alpha^2/4)
              -phi((1-alpha)^2)/2-phi(alpha^2)/2
              +2alpha(1-alpha).

This reduces the signed-reserve target to one scalar parameter without dropping the seed's retained terminal entropy. The finite census tests the identity against direct spectra; the second implementation reconstructs it from these masses and direct signed-character sums.

## Fixed six-variable circuit and exact signs

Choose t=3 and g=-1 only at w=000. In LSB bit order a,z,w0,w1,w2,y,

Consequently

    H_root=8-(225/128)log2(15),
    mean H_terminal=3-(49/64)log2(7),
    D=5+(49/64)log2(7)-(225/128)log2(15).

    2^600 * 7^98 < 15^225 < 2^604 * 7^98

proves 9/32<D<5/16. Since kappa=1/2, the signed residual satisfies

the last sign proved by 15^225>2^864. Thus the actual small excess can be paid once from this specified active-core terminal surplus. # exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A050/B10 scoped repair is proved locally, with independent review pending. Full FEI remains OPEN. The failed potential is the universal biased singleton tax-free claim J<=M, where M=H(S_X|S_Y), J=I(X;T) in the actual physical-child/squared-spectrum channel, and L=H(F)-E H(F_X)=M+J. All entropies are Shannon bits and include the empty coefficient. This stage repairs that existing failed statement rather than opening another broad candidate while B10's review cap is reached.

Additional hypothesis (OI): in a common normalized product Fourier basis for two Boolean children g,h, every residual label T in their shared support satisfies |G_T|=|H_T|. Labels appearing in only one child are unrestricted. For any interior selector bias p, this implies J<=M exactly. For a fair selector it implies the stronger paid decrement L<=4 Inf_X(F), with sharp constant 4. For every finite physical fair restriction tree satisfying OI at every queried node, with arbitrary Boolean terminal leaves,

    H(F) <= 4[I(F)-E I(F_leaf)] + E H(F_leaf).

In particular, if all leaves satisfy H<=C_leaf I, then H(F)<=max(4,C_leaf) I(F). No hypothesis about terminal entropy is silently added. A stopping tree means actual physical restrictions, fair branch probabilities, no repeated physical query along a path, and a finite set of nodes; no spectral conditioning is substituted for physical restriction.

The hypothesis is the weakest sufficient one in the explicitly ordered tested chain below, not claimed globally weakest or necessary. Our unbounded application is a fresh fair selector between arbitrary equal-rank quadratic signs on the same arbitrarily large data block, including arbitrary linear/constant terms. Those functions have H(F)<=4 I(F); branches may have partially overlapping affine Fourier supports. Tensor products and hierarchical multiplexers with disjoint balanced child data blocks and these modules as leaves preserve the stated tree admission and bound. A common arbitrary independent factor preserves OI and the local decrement, while retaining its entire entropy as a terminal obligation.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Exact overlap law, with arbitrary product-biased data

Let G_T,H_T be real normalized child coefficients; both squared lists have sum one. Let C be their common support, w=sum_C G_T^2=sum_C H_T^2, b=sum_{T in C:G_T=-H_T} G_T^2, and a=1-w. Under OI every common coefficient is equal or opposite; outside C the two exclusive support masses each total a. For F=g at X=0 and h at X=1, independent P(X=1)=p, the parent selector-absent and selector-present masses in residual fiber T are

    A_T=((1-p)G_T+p H_T)^2,
    B_T=p(1-p)(H_T-G_T)^2,
    Q_T=A_T+B_T=(1-p)G_T^2+p H_T^2.

The physical-channel joint masses are (1-p)G_T^2,p H_T^2. Therefore L=M+J by the entropy chain rule, independently of any uncertainty theorem. In a common fiber, the posterior physical probability is p. In an exclusive fiber it is 0 or 1. Thus H(X|T)=w h2(p), and

    J=a h2(p).

On common equal-sign fibers B_T/Q_T=0; on common opposite-sign fibers it is 4p(1-p); on g-only fibers it is p; on h-only fibers it is 1-p. Their Q masses are respectively the common weights, (1-p)a,pa. Consequently

    M=a h2(p)+b h2(4p(1-p)),
    M-J=b h2(4p(1-p))>=0,
    L=2a h2(p)+b h2(4p(1-p)).                 (1)

This repairs J<=M for all interior biases, with no additive tax. At p=1/2, h2(4p(1-p))=h2(1)=0, so M=J=a and L=2a. Parseval gives c=E[gh]=w-2b, hence the actual physical selector influence is

    d=P(g!=h)=(1-c)/2=a/2+b.

Therefore L=2a<=4d, with equality exactly when b=0 (including the zero case). This is a payment of the complete decrement, not only of J. Under biased selector inputs d is still the physical pivotal probability; the biased spectral selector energy is 4p(1-p)d. Formula (1) is not a dimension-independent linear payment by that spectral energy as p tends to zero.

## 2. Terminal-corrected physical tree, influence never reused

At a fair physical split i of any Boolean u, every other coordinate influence is the average of the corresponding child influences, by conditioning the pivotal event on x_i. Hence

    I(u)=Inf_i(u)+(I(u_0)+I(u_1))/2.

For a finite physical tree with node reach weights pi_v, repeated substitution yields sum_v pi_v Inf_{i_v}(u_v)=I(F)-E I(F_leaf). Entropy telescopes on precisely the same tree as H(F)-E H(F_leaf)=sum_v pi_v L_v. OI and (1) imply

    H(F)-E H(F_leaf) <=4 sum_v pi_v Inf_{i_v}(u_v)
                         =4[I(F)-E I(F_leaf)].       (2)

This proves the target and max-constant closure. An irrelevant query has identical children and L=d=0; it incurs no artificial bias tax. If OI fails at some queried nodes, define the actual excess e_v=max(0,L_v-4d_v). The same argument gives the honest version of (2) with +sum_bad pi_v e_v. Bounding that bad-node residual is the remaining universal obligation, not a theorem asserted here.

## 3. Equal-rank quadratic signs, including shared data

Use uniform x in F_2^n. A quadratic sign is f(x)=(-1)^{q(x)}, where q has degree at most two including linear and constant terms. Let B be the polar alternating matrix: q(x+u)+q(x)+q(u)+q(0)=x^T B u, and r=rank(B). Its autocorrelation is

    E_x f(x)f(x+u)=(-1)^{q(u)+q(0)} 1_{u in ker B}.

The phase u->q(u)+q(0) is linear on ker B because its polar form vanishes there. Fourier inversion of the autocorrelation therefore gives fhat(S)^2=2^-r on one affine coset of (ker B)^perp and zero elsewhere. The support has 2^r elements. This proves the flat-spectrum fact for arbitrary n without assuming a literature result.

Any pair of quadratic signs with the same polar rank r has equal nonzero Fourier magnitude 2^(-r/2); it satisfies OI even if affine supports overlap only partly. For an affine support of dimension r, at least r coordinates vary: projection into all varying coordinates is injective, so their count is >=r. Every varying coordinate is fair on that affine support, giving I(f)>=r/2 and H(f)=r<=2 I(f). Thus the selector-first tree has two leaves paid at constant 2; (2) gives H(F)<=4 I(F) on this unbounded shared-data family. Rank zero includes characters and constants.

Strict partial-overlap example: g=(-1)^{y1*y2}, h=(-1)^{y2*y3} on three uniform bits. Both ranks are 2. Their common support is {empty,{y2}}, both common coefficients +1/2, so w=1/2,b=0,d=1/4. The fair selector F has L=1=4d, H(F)=3 and I(F)=5/4. This proves sharpness of local constant 4 while its full H/I is 12/5, not an FEI counterexample.

## 4. Ordered repairs, operations, and exclusions

The tested nested list is R1: distinct characters; R2: disjoint child Fourier supports; R3: OI; R4: unrestricted actual Boolean children. R1 implies R2 implies R3. Both sufficient implications are strict: a constant paired with a balanced fresh-character times a bent function gives disjoint non-character support; the quadratic example above gives partial overlap satisfying R3. R4 fails J<=M on the original biased AND witness. Thus R3 is the weakest sufficient condition in this tested ordered list only. Equal-rank quadratic pairs and isospectral pairs are useful additional subfamilies of R3; neither a global necessity theorem nor a globally weakest hypothesis is claimed.

For an arbitrary independent common Boolean factor k, child coefficients become G_T K_U,H_T K_U. Intersections factor and nonzero common magnitudes remain equal; w,b,d,M,J,L stay unchanged because sum_U K_U^2=1. However terminal entropy gains H(k), and terminal influence gains I(k); (2) retains both. No FEI bound on arbitrary k follows. Multiplying both children by a uniform residual character permutes their spectra, preserves w,b,d, and makes balanced versions if the character is on a fresh bit. Biased coordinate multiplication is not claimed to be a spectral permutation.

For disjoint tensors of admitted pairs, shared supports are products and magnitudes multiply, so OI remains true. For admitted physical trees, process factors one at a time; the other factors are common terminal factors at each query. Influence and entropy add, and leaf constants remain unchanged. For balanced Boolean branches on disjoint physical data blocks, their Fourier supports are disjoint in the full data label space: the only possible intersection is empty, and balancing removes it. Therefore a recursively built fresh-selector hierarchy of disjoint balanced OI modules admits a tree querying outer selectors and then each reached module's selector, with equal-rank quadratic leaves. Balance may be enforced using a fresh common character. Arbitrary depth, module dimension and tensor count are permitted; H<=4I persists. Physical selectors are fresh, and only the data inside each module may be shared.

OI is not closed under arbitrary data restriction. In the partial-overlap example, set y1=0: g becomes constant 1 while h remains a rank-2 quadratic. At their common empty label the magnitudes are 1 and 1/2, violating OI. Consequently we require OI only at actual queried nodes of the chosen tree and do not assert hereditary admission for every tree. Arbitrary weighted thresholds, arbitrary low-energy shared-data multiplexers, all reused-selector circuits, and percolation crossings are not shown to satisfy it. The accepted sparse-bent point-surgery obstruction has typical child g and child g-2 point: its coefficients are generally perturbed in magnitude, so this repair is not an admission proof for that obstruction tree. Blindly declaring it admissible would be the first unsupported line.

## 5. Retain the original obstruction

For biased AND sign 1-2XY with P(X=1)=P(Y=1)=1/4, squared parent masses (empty,X,Y,XY) are (196,12,12,36)/256; the residual Y-present mass is 3/16. The child at X=0 is constant, and the child at X=1 has spectral masses (1/4,3/4), so

    J-M=2 h2(3/16)-H(196,12,12,36 over 256)-(1/4)h2(3/4)>1/16.

The exact rational-log sign is reproduced in out/ORIGINAL_COUNTEREXAMPLE.json. The common empty magnitude is 1 versus 1/2, so the witness violates OI exactly where the repair requires it. Fair residual coordinates and balanced children alone do not repair the claim (A047/A049); common fresh-character balancing preserves that failing common magnitude ratio. In the uniform cube this AND is a harmless flat bent spectrum and does not disprove FEI.

The first unsupported universal line is the existence for every Boolean F of a physical stopping tree whose bad-node excess sum and complete terminal entropy are paid by one dimension-independent influence budget. Equation (2) proves that payment exactly on its declared nodewise hypothesis, not that hypothesis for all functions.

At node v define c_v=H(X_i|Y,history=v) and w_v its physical reach weight. The new scoped repair is

    sum_v w_v [tau*(p_i)-c_v/2]_+ <= I(Pi;Y)/2 <= I_phys(f)/2,
    sum_v w_v tau*(p_i) <= I_phys(f)/2 + H(Pi|Y)/2.        (A)

The required extra resource is the declared output-conditional transcript bank H(Pi|Y)/2, supplied at most once. We prove the refund coefficient 1/2 is the smallest sufficient coefficient in the explicitly tested family of bounds A_T<=C I_phys+lambda H(Pi|f), with finite C and nonnegative lambda, uniformly over all product-biased Boolean functions and essential computing trees. # complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md gives the complete derivation, including every boundary/conditioning convention. Its critical steps are summarized here.

**Scalar bound.** For the two bases defining the angular tax, pick each larger-probability basis vector. Their rank-one projectors sum to an operator with maximum eigenvalue at most 1+sqrt(1-r), r=min(p,1-p). If u,w are the two smaller probabilities, then u+w>=delta=1-sqrt(1-r). Binary entropy monotonicity/concavity imply h2(u)+h2(w)>=h2(delta)>=h2(r/2)>=h2(r)/2. Thus 0<=tau*(p)<=h2(p)/2 for all biases, without any interval coverage or multi-bit additivity premise. At the symmetric angle t=beta/2, tau*>=h2(p)-2h2((1-sqrt(1-p))/2), giving tau*/h2(p)->1/2 at the rare endpoint. At uniform bias tau*(1/2)=0 exactly, by h2(x)>=4x(1-x) and the complementary sin/cos identity.

**Local bank drift and terminal balance.** Let j_v=I(X_i;Y|history=v)=h2(p_i)-c_v and ell_v=h2(p_i)/2-tau*(p_i)>=0. Exactly

    tau*(p_i)-c_v/2=j_v/2-ell_v.

The nonnegative reserve R_v=H(Y|history=v)/2 has drift j_v/2. Its complete-tree release is I(Pi;Y)/2, retaining E H(Y|Pi)/2 at stopping leaves. Therefore the positive part of each net tax is paid on one conserved account; the account is not reopened at each depth. The finite transcript chain rule also gives sum w c_v=H(Pi|Y), so this proves both inequalities in (A). It applies to partial and adaptive deterministic trees and respects every zero-probability/constant endpoint.

**Root payment.** H(Y)=I(Y;X)=sum_i I(Y;X_i|X_<i). Add later coordinates to the first mutual-information argument and use input independence: each term is at most I(Y;X_i|X_-i)=H(Y|X_-i)=h2(p_i)Inf_i^phys(f). Thus H(Y)<=sum_i h2(p_i)Inf_i^phys<=I_phys. This pays only the output-information release and proves (A) without FEI. Biased spectral energy is not substituted for physical influence.

**Sharpness of the declared extra-bank coefficient.** If lambda<1/2, choose a fixed rare bias q with tau*(q)>lambda h2(q). In the essential short-circuit computing tree of the r-bit q-biased point defect,

    A_T=tau*(q)(1-q^r)/(1-q),
    H(Pi|f)=h2(q)(1-q^r)/(1-q)-h2(q^r),
    I_phys=r q^(r-1).

Then A_T-lambda H(Pi|f) tends to a positive constant while I_phys tends to zero, so no finite C works. Lambda=1/2 works by (A). The failure without a refund and the point-defect tree are existing A048 results, credited rather than presented as new FEI counterexamples; the new result is the all-tree positive-part repair and the exact minimal bank coefficient.

**Terminal-inclusive C019 substitution.** For the actual child pair define M_v=H(queried Fourier label|remaining Fourier labels), J_v=physical-child/spectral mutual information, and D_v=tau*(p_i)-(J_v-M_v)>=0. All are under the actual inherited product basis. Let M_T=sum w M_v and S_T=sum w(ell_v+D_v). Then, exactly,

    H_hat(f)=E H_hat(f_leaf)+2M_T+H(Pi|Y)/2
                                    +I(Pi;Y)/2-S_T.          (B)

Only I(Pi;Y)/2 is paid by (A). What remains is the signed complete quantity 2M_T+H(Pi|Y)/2-S_T+E H_hat(f_leaf). The sum ell+D equals h2(p_i)/2-(J_v-M_v), so the residual can be computed exactly without optimizing tau*. At physical uniform biases tau*=0 and ell=h2(1/2)/2=1/2; the matching transcript term cancels exactly. No positive logical-mask tax is smuggled into a fair physical coordinate. C018's actual erasure remains unpaid; its identity is not replaced by output entropy.

**Compatibility control.** Reconstruct A037's fair coherent lift by selecting physical b with kernel childhat_b(T)^2/(2q_T) and retaining T, the remaining root-spectral labels. Boolean child Parseval gives fair physical branches and actual child spectra, and the kernel's dependence only on T gives I(S0;Pi)=sum w J_v. On a complete computing tree attach actual Y as its transcript-measurable output. Then

    H(Pi|Y)-H(Pi|S0)=I(S0;Pi)-I(Y;Pi).

Neither conditional entropy universally dominates the other. The fair one-bit character has difference -1. The two-bit bent AND under its forward essential computing tree has difference 1-h2(1/4)>0. This demonstrates the exact side-information mismatch. The output-conditional refund is not automatically the spectral-witness refund. A common-space reference needs to supply it, with its channel/reset corrections, once.

**Essential contraction and operations.** An ignored revealed coordinate has deterministic empty spectral label and independent physical outcome, so deleting it preserves M_A,J_A and lowers any assigned nonnegative tax. Essential contraction remains valid but alone cannot pay the scalar tax (A048). The current refund even gives nonpositive net tax at an irrelevant node because c_v=h2(p_i). Adaptive tree cuts retain the remaining output reserve and the conditional-transcript suffix entropy, with no double release. On overlapping events sharing a physical control, (A) applies to the single actual tree and queries that control once; it supplies the tax component's ownership only. Independent factorwise versions may use separate independent factor outputs and banks. A common factor remains in the actual full output and terminal Fourier entropy; replacing that output or treating its entropy as paid is not authorized. Logical masks need an explicit conversion to original physical controls. Root-block sharp additivity, arbitrary reused-selector composition, all-node OI heredity and a universal spectral residual payment are not proved.

**First unsupported line:** a normalized compatible C024 reference supplies H(Pi|Y)/2 as an actual single-use refund with the correct spectral side conditioning and all channel/reset corrections, and the signed residual in (B) plus terminal Fourier entropy is bounded by the remaining exclusive influence budget. The result does not prove that line, and terminal output entropy cannot pay terminal Fourier entropy.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

UNIVERSAL_DESCENT_SQUEEZE.md contains a self-contained all-dimensional argument: reveal all but a least-influential bit. Every child has zero Fourier entropy, so phi(f)<=H(f)/(I-min_i Inf_i)<=n(H/I)/(n-1). At a function attaining the finite-dimensional ratio maximum M_n, all proper children have ratio<=M_n, hence every block has L_A>=M_n I_A. Therefore M_n<=K_desc(n)<=n M_n/(n-1). No all-dimensional optimizer is assumed. It follows that K_desc(n) tends to C_* in the extended real sense, and bounded block-descent is equivalent in finiteness to FEI. The lead's proposed phi/(H/I)<=1.33 check is automatic for n>=4, so it is not diagnostic evidence of a paid universal restriction rule. This is a specific mathematical disagreement about interpreting the experiment, not rejection of the lead's phi>4.54 target.

COMPOSITION_CERTIFICATES.md proves the formula enumeration, exact block-orbit reduction, entropy vector representation and rational-log enclosures. Every orbit minimum and finite maximum is certified. The census has117,240 block orbits representing470,340 labelled proper blocks. Every phi upper endpoint is strictly below227/50=4.54. The exact dimensional maxima display as4.3198821089,4.1919153810,4.2044382917,4.3104363691 for n=6,7,8,9. Thus there is no first composition exceeding4.54 in this frozen family. Point defects maximize at6,7, but a four-bit double-NAND module combined with literal leaves maximizes at8, and two such modules plus a literal maximize at9. An eight-bit three-level complete binary NAND tree has exactly phi/(H/I)=8/7, showing the pointwise squeeze factor is sharp at that dimension. All seven named seed controls also have phi<4.54; their exact values and minimizing blocks are saved.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

TWO_BIT_RESERVE.md gives the complete proof. Classify normalized physical Fourier rows by their actual two-bit Boolean phase: constant, nonconstant with c<=1/16, or nonconstant with c>1/16; their masses are W0,Wg,Wb. Every nonconstant two-bit Boolean phase has influence at least 1. Spectral TV<=sqrt(c) then gives row influence>=1/2 on good rows. Their H-KI is at most 2-K/2, preserving a positive margin for K>4. Constant phases have nonempty spectral mass e<=c and a Gibbs bound H-KI<=r_K=log2(1+3*2^-K). Bad rows have H-KI<=2 and Wb<=16 C_A.

For every two-coordinate cut and K>=4, the actual complete reserve satisfies

    D_K+S_A+(K/2-2)Wg <= Gamma_A+r_K W0+2Wb.

An alternative with no exponential floor has right side Gamma_A+psi_2(min(1,C_A))+2Wb, hence at C_A<=3/4 it is bounded by log2(1+C_A)+h2(C_A)+(log2 3+32)C_A. This improves the generic square-root phase error to O(C_A log(1/C_A)) for two-bit blocks at K>=4. An elementary quartic Cauchy--Schwarz argument also proves C_A<=6 I_A, tying collision and exceptional mass to the same queried influence account. No sharpness is claimed for 6 or 32.

Exact guard: f=(-1)^((x1 xor x2)y) has C_x1=C_x2=0 but C_pair=1. Thus an unconditional bound C_pair<=c(C_x1+C_x2) fails for every finite c. This low-ratio function is not dimension-minimal; it identifies the signed degree-two parity cross term that a guarded critical-source proof must price.

First unsupported line: a dimension-independent K and a pair/larger-block selection whose collision, constant-phase remainder and exceptional mass are smaller than the retained positive witness/child/phase reserve. # complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

AFFINE_SELECTION Sections1--6 provide a complete elementary proof. Every
invertible affine permutation of Fourier labels preserves Booleanity and
entropy. At a finite D_K maximum it cannot reduce influence. Single row
replacement and translation force all parity marginal costs to dominate
the largest original coordinate influence they use. Conversely a rank
count at each cost threshold proves these inequalities characterize the
minimum across the ENTIRE finite affine orbit, including ties.

Spectral affine-span compression selects independent original coordinate
projections and centers each at its majority. It gives an actual Boolean
g on d=affdim(support) bits with H(g)=H(f), I(g)<=I(f). Finite orbit
minimization then proves full FEI(K) is equivalent at the SAME K to FEI(K)
on full-affine-span/minimum-basis representatives. Exact finite cap
identity and preservation of epsilon-nearness under nondecreasing-deficit
reductions are proved without assuming any unbounded-dimension optimizer.
At the existing minimal/maximal selector, d=n and every pair obeys
q_ij<=min(p_i,p_j)/2. Quantitative epsilon/K errors are explicit.

The signed physical child bank is retained exactly as
D_K+S_A=H-EH(child)-K I_A; affine changes do not renew it or identify
transformed children with the original physical children. A law uniform
on {0,e_1,...,e_n} passes every new affine condition with unbounded H/I,
but violates Boolean convolution, so it refutes only the probability-law
relaxation. FIRST UNSUPPORTED LINE: combine these minimum-basis constraints
with actual signed Boolean convolution/refund structure to pay a proper
physical pair from SAME unused D+S_A with one influence owner.

# A052 / B11: critical-source structure

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every Boolean sign f on every finite uniform cube, derive a structural restriction under either (i) R(g)<=R(f) for every proper physical restriction g, or (ii) H(f)>KI(f) and H(g)<=KI(g) for every proper physical restriction, at a fixed finite K>=0. Constants have H=I=0 and are never divided by I. A dimension-minimal K-witness satisfies (ii). Entropy is in bits except the explicitly named historical raw-indicator comparisons and natural-unit certificates. The new conclusion concerns every rank-r binary spectral syndrome VS, every physical pivot block A of size r with V_A invertible, and every syndrome word y0. It is not a connected-support theorem or a FEI solution.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2; README, CURRENT_STATUS, CLAIM_REGISTRY, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY; A051--A055 PACKET_STATUS entries; STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS B11; plan accounting/obstruction and B11 sections, especially lines 433--451 and 830--860. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete statement and main derivation are CRITICAL_SOURCE_STRUCTURE.tex, equations (1)--(3). Put kappa=2/ln2. For C=A^c, let Y=VS and e0(t)=V_A^-1(y0+V_Ct). Define b=E|S_A+e0(S_C)| and epsilon=P(Y!=y0). The physical Hamming weight gives b<=r epsilon. The partial Fourier channel v_t(x)=sum_e fhat(e,t)chi_e(x) has child probabilities v_t(x)^2, whose mean is the S_C marginal. Hence

    L_A=H(S_A|S_C)+J_A=H(Y|S_C)+J_A.

For each t, multiply v_t by chi_e0(t). This leaves its square unchanged and shifts its Fourier labels by e0(t). Logarithmic Sobolev applied to the absolute value of this real function gives

    (ln2) J_A=sum_t Ent(v_t^2)
        <=2 sum_(e,t) |e+e0(t)| fhat(e,t)^2 = 2b.

Here is a self-contained justification of the analytic inequality used. For a nonnegative two-point function (u,v), homogeneity and symmetry reduce to (t,1), t>=1. Twice the difference between the desired Dirichlet bound and Ent is

    F(t)=(t^2+1)ln((t^2+1)/2)-2t^2 ln t+(t-1)^2.

F(1)=0 and F'(t)=2t ln((t^2+1)/(2t^2))+2(t-1). The difference 1-1/t-ln(2t^2/(t^2+1)) vanishes at one and has derivative (t-1)^2/[t^2(t^2+1)]>=0. Thus F'>=0. Symmetry F(t)=t^2 F(1/t) and continuity handle the remaining values. Entropy tensorization then proves Ent(w^2)<=1/2 sum_i E(|w(x)|-|w(x+e_i)|)^2<=2 sum_i ||D_i w||_2^2 for real w on a uniform product cube. Tensorization follows inductively by conditioning and convexity of Ent; no biased inequality is imported. Parseval after each fiber's character shift yields the displayed 2b. Zero marginal fibers vanish identically.

Criticality is used only in the physical-child deficit comparison:

    L_A-R(f)I_A=-E[H(f_x)-R(f)I(f_x)]>=0.

Consequently R(f)I_A<=H(Y|S_C)+kappa b. In the threshold-critical form the left side is KI_A+D_K(f). Since H(Y)<=h2(epsilon)+epsilon log2(2^r-1), the explicit concentration obstruction is

    KI_A+D_K(f) <= h2(epsilon)
        +epsilon log2(2^r-1)+kappa r epsilon.

For a nonempty flip mask u and a pivot i in its support, r=1, b=epsilon_u, where epsilon_u=(1-|E f(x)f(x+u)|)/2. Thus KI_i+D_K(f)<=h2(epsilon_u)+kappa epsilon_u. In particular a positive-entropy ratio-critical source in essential coordinates has no exact signed translation symmetry. This strengthens the exact-symmetry removal baseline quantitatively, without a uniform positive lower bound when influence vanishes.

PRUNING_SCOPE.md replays both historical failures, checks their higher-ratio physical restrictions in the inherited indicator orientation, and separately certifies that both sign lifts have actual point-defect descendants with ratio more than 1/2 larger. Components are never assumed to be descendants. Arbitrary-core activation remains FEI-hard as a class, but its amplified K-violations contain smaller K-violating physical cores and fail dimension minimality. Descendants are not assumed critical. The reviewed raw-AND-power degree-moment obstruction is retained at its distinct scope.

First unsupported line: for some absolute finite K, every prospective dimension-critical K-witness admits a proper physical pivot block with H(VS|S_C)+kappa b<=KI_A. Such a block would contradict the strictly positive deficit. The present result supplies a necessary spectral-disorder restriction and a sufficient certificate for extracting a better child; it does not prove that an affordable block exists universally or pay component excess.

This stage moves C018 erasure/block payment toward C019 stopping-tree payment: the unpaid block erasure H(S_A|S_C) is represented exactly as conditional affine-syndrome entropy, and the physical channel term has the additional bound (2/ln2)b measured in physical pivot distance.
